# Wiedisync — Dev / Deploy Log

Operator-facing history of backend, deploy, and migration work on the wiedisync platform.
Newest first.

This is the dev/ops narrative (migration numbers, dev + prod deploy status, internal IDs,
file paths, root-cause notes). It is deliberately distinct from:

- **CHANGELOG.md** — curated, user-facing release notes (English, semver), mirrored in `src/modules/changelog/ChangelogPage.tsx`.
- **git log** — the full commit-level detail.

At the end of a substantive backend/deploy session, append a dated entry here (one line per entry).
`CLAUDE.md → Recent dev log` keeps only the last few entries for at-a-glance context.

- **2026-08-12** **The sync-down died on a column migration 303 dropped, and half-applied for two days (no migration, no code change — `clubdesk:deploy` only, prod)** — the superadmin "Sync down from ClubDesk" button reported `Sync failed — see the member sync log`. Root cause was **purely a deploy gap**: migration 303 dropped `members.otn_bb` from prod on 10.08., but `/opt/clubdesk-sync/import-clubdesk-csv.mjs` was still the 08.08. copy that writes it, so psql aborted on `column m.otn_bb does not exist`. `npm run scripts:deploy` does NOT reach that directory — only `clubdesk:deploy` does ([[clubdesk-sync-script-copies]]), and it was never run when 303 landed. **Every sync-down since 2026-08-10 14:56 UTC had failed — 6 runs.** ⚠⚠ **The failure is loud but PARTIAL, which is the real hazard**: `ON_ERROR_STOP=1` kills the run *where it stands*, so everything before the bad statement had already committed (export refresh of 1155 contacts, addresses, ClubDesk linking, identity, federation of origin, trainer licences, contact fields) while everything at-or-after it silently never ran — the entire officials transaction (`referee_vb`/`referee_bb`/`scorer_vb` from the "VB SC" licence/`otr1|2_bb`/`otn1|2_bb`/the VB-referee→scorer rule), the ambiguous-link report, and the `public_stats.member_count` refresh behind the kscw.ch About page (stuck at 677 vs 678 actual). "Sync failed" therefore does **not** mean "nothing changed", and none of the stale half is visible in the UI. ⚠ Diffed all 14 clubdesk scripts by `md5sum` against the repo first — `import-clubdesk-csv.mjs` was the only stale one, so the deploy shipped exactly one file. ⚠ Deploying it also activated **migration 302's other half** (the register triple `register_status`/`eintritt`/`austritt`), which had never run on prod: dry-ran it as a read-only SELECT before shipping → 4 members, all correct on the re-run. **Anna Lara Bennefeld** (28, volleyball) `Aktivmitglied` → `Ehemaliges Mitglied` (ClubDesk wins; operator confirmed she is ehemalig); **Julien Brugger** (733) + **Paul Orator** (734), both basketball, filled to `Aktivmitglied` + Eintritt 10.08.2026. **Timo Beyerlein** (131, basketball) is the interesting no-op — ClubDesk holds an **Austritt 30.07.2026 against a `Passivmitglied` status**, the contradictory pair `members_austritt_needs_departed_status` refuses, so the importer's `CASE` correctly drops the date rather than abort the transaction. ⚠ That register row will keep being silently dropped until ClubDesk itself agrees with itself — fix it there, not here. Post-run: `members_referee` 22, the new `members_otn_unresolved` **0** (as the migration predicted), no ambiguous links, `members_active_total` 678, `public_stats` refreshed to 678. No repo file changed, so no version bump — the code was already right, only the VPS copy was old. Also triaged the same-day `[i18n] failed to load "en" dictionary` on `/club`: **crawler noise, not a regression** — `/js/i18n/en.json` serves 200 (incl. under a bingbot UA), only 2 occurrences in 2 days, both bingbot, neither correlating with a deploy. Left unmuted deliberately: the message is a genuine failure signal for real users and muting it by string would hide a real breakage. The separate `https://mailto:unsubscribe@kscw.ch/`-origin credential errors in the same log are **already closed** — `error_mute_rules` #8 (07.08.) covers them, last occurrence 06.08.

- **2026-08-11** **`referees_json` was fixed in the prod DATABASE but not in the prod BRANCH — a loaded gun aimed at the next perms run (no new DB work, prod)** — shipping `1e023391` to prod turned out to be code-only: migration **311 was already applied to prod at 12:29** (recorded in `kscw_migrations`, sha `58ce22d3…` matching the file byte-for-byte) and the anonymous `GET /items/games?fields=…,referees_json` already returned **200**, so the website calendar was working. What was missing was the **authoritative** half: prod's `setup-permissions.mjs` still lacked `'referees_json'` in `PUBLIC_GAME_FIELDS`. ⚠⚠ That is worse than a plain missing fix, because `setup-permissions.mjs` is **declarative and destructive** — it clears a policy's rows and recreates them from the constant. CLAUDE.md mandates re-running `db:setup-perms:*` after every deploy, so the very next run **from a prod checkout** would have rewritten the public games row WITHOUT the field and silently re-broken the calendar for every month, with the migration journal still showing 311 applied. **Lesson: applying a permission by hand (or by `db:migrate`, which is git-blind) fixes the row, not the source of truth — the constant and the DB must land together, and only the constant survives the next reconcile.** Cherry-picked as `18019bbb`; verified afterwards that prod's `setup-permissions.mjs:878` carries the field, `311-…sql` is on the branch, and the calendar's real query (`fields=id,date,league,referees_json,kscw_team.id` over a month window) returns rows anonymously. ⚠ `origin/prod` moved twice more during the session (`5c22d637` → … → `2a73bc62` `feat(wadmin)`), so `git fetch` before every cherry-pick, and the cherry-pick sits on whatever the operator pushed in the meantime. ⚠ curl with unquoted `filter[date][_gte]=…` returned an EMPTY body and no status line — bash ate the brackets; URL-encode (`filter%5Bdate%5D%5B_gte%5D`) or the "broken API" you are diagnosing is your own shell.

- **2026-08-11** **A push to `prod` deployed NO frontend for ~3.5 hours and nothing failed (CI only, prod)** — found while verifying v1.80.0. `wiedisync.kscw.ch` had moved to the KSCW account's **`kscw-wiedisync`** Pages project, which is **Direct Upload** (a CF Pages project is permanently either git-connected or Direct Upload — it can never be attached to a repo), so `.github/workflows/deploy-cloudflare-pages.yml` is its ONLY deploy path. That workflow already declared `on: push: branches: [prod, dev]` — but **a workflow runs from the branch being pushed**, and the file existed only on `dev`. So `prod` pushes ran nothing at all: the project's production deployment sat at `5c22d637` (13:10) while prod HEAD moved to `a2fd8207` and then `dd31fc1b`, and the site served **v1.79.0** with no failing run, no red X, nothing to notice. `kscw-spielplanung` was in the same state behind `spielplanung.wiedisync.kscw.ch`. Fixed by putting the file on `prod` (`56fcfa22`) as its **final state from dev** rather than replaying `e8de6b93` + `5ccd6f1c` + `a722ad2d`, which touch nothing else and would only have committed two superseded versions on the way; the push then deployed both projects (`4e0c5b07`, `4ad39b63`). ⚠⚠ **`npx wrangler pages deploy dist` from a local build is NOT a substitute and was nearly used as one.** The member-app bundle needs `VITE_FEATURE_MESSAGING`, `VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY`, `VITE_SENTRY_DSN` and `VITE_SCHEDULING_ORIGIN` — two from repo secrets — and a build without them is **green, deploys fine, and ships messaging off, maps broken and no error reporting**. The stale-changelog symptom would have been traded for a real regression. Read the deploy workflow's `env:` block before hand-rolling any Pages upload. ⚠ **`assets:check:prod` is not a deploy check** — it reported "all 95 assets reachable" against the OLD bundle twice. Only a marker crawl for a string unique to YOUR change proves a build shipped ([[cf-pages-hash-flip-not-your-build]]), and a mid-publish read returned 55 chunks instead of 93, so re-read until the count is stable. ⚠ A poll loop guarded on `grep "marker: [a-zA-Z]"` exits immediately — it matches `marker: NOT FOUND`. ⚠ The stale-frontend window is invisible from git alone: `origin/prod` and `origin/dev` differed in **zero** frontend-affecting files the whole time, so a source diff says "nothing to ship" while the site is three commits behind. Bonus from the same run: the workflow's unconditional sourcemap strip is now on prod, and `index-*.js.map` returns **404** on both hosts — `spielplanung.wiedisync.kscw.ch` had been serving its full source (200) for months. ⚠ Still open: the run warns **Sentry source-map upload FAILED for kscw-wiedisync**, so this release's stack traces stay minified (deliberately a warning, not a failure — a Sentry outage must not block a deploy).

- **2026-08-11** **Real sport scoping for the members read-privacy hook — one rule, shared with the DELETE gate (no migration, ext-only, dev+prod)** — narrows the same-day exemption below. A `vb_admin` now reads `birthdate` + `ahv_nummer` unredacted for the **volleyball** section and a `bb_admin` for the **basketball** one; each still gets the ordinary member view of the other section, and a **dual** sport admin is unconfined (no boundary can be drawn between two sections one person runs — `sportAdminScope()` returns null for them AND for non-admins, so a caller must establish staffness separately; the hook does, then reads null as `'all'`). ⚠ **Not a second copy of the rule**: `resolveMemberSport` was lifted out of `delete-impact.js` — where it already gates `POST /kscw/admin/delete-member` — into **`kscw-endpoints/src/member-sport.js`**, and both callers now import it; `kscw-hooks` already imports 10 modules from `kscw-endpoints` (`error-log`, `season`, `email-template`, …) so this needed no new plumbing. `sportScopeError` keeps identical behaviour, re-expressed through `sportAdminScope` + `sportScopeAllows`. ⚠⚠ **The read path had to be BATCHED**: delete-impact's per-member form runs ~5 queries, and the Data Explorer pulls **~700 members in ONE request** → ~3500 queries behind a single page load. `resolveMemberSports(db, ids, { memberRows })` answers for the whole page in **four** (three junctions + `teams`), reusing the `members` rows the hook had already fetched for its gating flags — so the extended `select` there now also carries `sektion, beitragskategorie`. Non-staff callers issue **none** of them (the resolver only runs when `scope` is set). ⚠ The rule, unchanged and now pinned by 23 tests: teams outrank `sektion` outranks the `beitragskategorie` prefix; **a coach has NO roster row**, so `member_teams` + `teams_coaches` + `teams_responsibles` are all read (a player-only join reports "no team" for exactly the people who run the teams); **team NAMES lie** ('Herren 2 H3' is basketball) so only `teams.sport` counts; the category match is a **prefix** ('Aktivmitglied VB' is not a volleyball marker); two sports → `'both'`. ⚠ **`'both'` is permissive on purpose** — a club-level or unresolvable member stays visible to BOTH sections, because a passive member or a fresh signup with `requested_team` but no `member_teams` row yet must not vanish from the section that has to process them. An item with **no id** cannot be resolved at all and falls through to redaction. Real prod distribution: **305 volleyball / 298 basketball / 74 both** of 677 active, so the scope actually bites rather than collapsing to 'both' — the reporting bb_admin (226, herself basketball across four BB teams) now reads 372 unredacted, and **member 140 Anouk Böhler** from her screenshot resolves basketball (Sektion Basketball, `BB Minis Turnier`, DU12 + MU10), i.e. the exact reported case is covered. Gates: build ✓, lint 0 errors, **1334** unit (23 new), `node --check` on all three edited files. ⚠ `database` is destructured in `registerDeleteImpact(router, { database, … })` at line 195 and `sportScopeError` sits inside that closure — `node --check` does NOT catch an out-of-scope binding, so that was verified by reading the signature. Dev + prod both loaded the new **cross-package import** cleanly (`KSCW hooks loaded` + `Server started`, no exceptions) — that was the real deploy risk, since a bad relative path there crashes extension loading wholesale. Prod cherry-pick `dd31fc1b`, scratch worktree as before. Changelog wording for v1.80.0 was corrected **in place** rather than cutting a release: the un-scoped behaviour was live under an hour and `CLAUDE.md` says collapse same-day iterations of one root cause into a single entry.

- **2026-08-11** **Two layers disagreed about who may read a birthdate, and the hook won silently (no migration, ext + frontend, dev+prod, v1.80.0)** — reported as "Anne Grimshaw, Sport Admin for BB, cannot see birthdates and AHV numbers". Root cause is **`kscw-hooks/src/index.js` §11 `filter('members.items.read')`**, which exempts only `context.accountability.admin` — true **only for policies carrying `admin_access`**, i.e. Administrator and Superuser. Sport admins used to satisfy it through the hand-made `Sport Admin -> KSCW Admin` attachment (`admin_access = true`, created 2026-03-29); **§3b of `setup-permissions.mjs` prunes that row** (correctly — it made every vb_admin/bb_admin a full Directus superadmin), shipped `b4738011` on **2026-08-05**, and the same prune invisibly switched this hook ON for them. Result: Directus grants `KSCW Sport Admin` a `members` read with **`fields = '*'`** while the hook nulled `birthdate` for every member whose `birthdate_visibility = 'hidden'` — the **Postgres DEFAULT**, so **620 of 677** active members, i.e. the register read as though the club held no birthdates — and nulled `ahv_nummer` for **all** of them unconditionally. Fixed with a named `PRIVACY_EXEMPT_ROLES = ['admin','superuser','vb_admin','bb_admin']`, one indexed `members.where('user', …).select('role')` lookup **per request, not per item** (the explorer reads ~700 members in one call), mirroring the already-live pattern at line 507. ⚠ **NOT sport-scoped** — no other field on this record is, the Directus grant behind it is club-wide, and one policy is shared by vb_admin and bb_admin so no filter can tell the sections apart (the same reason `members.delete` is withheld, §9). Coach/TR/Vorstand/member, the own-record `continue` and the anonymous branch are untouched. ⚠⚠ **The diagnosis went down a wrong path first and the lesson is general**: the Directus permission layer was read end-to-end (live `directus_permissions` on prod, then the actual `process-ast/lib/get-cases.js` **inside the running container** — `allowedFields` is the union of `fields` from every row with no item filter, so Sport Admin's unfiltered `'*'` means `injectCases` never wraps anything) and it **correctly proved no masking happens there**. That proof was right and the conclusion drawn from it — "so it must be the grid's `DEFAULT_VISIBLE = ['last_name','first_name']`" — was wrong. **A `filter` hook runs after authorization and can null a field the policy fully grants; a permission audit that stops at `directus_permissions` is blind to it.** What settled it was the user's screenshot (detail view, "Birthdate visibility: Hidden" rendered, Birthdate card absent) plus one psql row: member 140 has `birthdate = 2016-11-17` **and** an `ahv_nummer`, so hide-empty could not explain it. ⚠ **Neither dev nor prod could functionally verify the authenticated branch**: dev's items API is license-restricted (`items is a restricted resource`, `license.source: null` — 403s even for the **admin** token, and that error is raised before any filter hook runs, so it is not a signal about the hook), and prod has **no member token in the vault** (`db:smoke:prod` still cannot run — the `.env.local` slot has been empty since 2026-07-11). Verified instead: `node --check`; `members.role` is **jsonb** so node-postgres hands back a real array (the `Array.isArray` guard is sound); hooks loaded + `Server started` with no exceptions on both; and the hook's **anonymous** branch demonstrably running on prod (`/items/members` 200 returning `Oscar B.` / `Alexander L.`, the website name-abbreviation). The sport-admin branch itself is code-inspection + Anne's own reload. Also shipped: the **datapoint search** in `/admin/explore` (`memberFieldSearch.ts` + `ExplorerFieldSearch.tsx`) — the explorer holds ~110 columns over 12 groups and both views hide most by default, so finding `ahv_nummer` meant knowing its group and its toggle; ranking is over label + column name + group + help + an alias table, folding `licen[cs]e` and carrying German terms (`geburtsdatum`, `lizenz`). ⚠ In the detail view a focus **overrides hide-empty, show-technical AND the sport gate** — a search that returns a blank page because the member has no value is the bug being fixed — and `dirtySet` rides in the render plan so changing the focus mid-edit cannot hide a pending change. ⚠ In the grid the focused columns are merged **for display only**; the localStorage column set is never rewritten. ⚠ `GRID_MEMBER_COLUMN_KEYS` was briefly exported from `ExplorerGrid.tsx` and **eslint `react-refresh/only-export-components` rejected it** (a component file exporting a constant kills fast refresh for a 1900-line component); the mechanical extraction of the ~120-line column catalog into its own module went wrong mid-way and was **reverted** in favour of moving the "no column here" hint into the grid itself — better UX and zero exports. Gates: build ✓, lint **0 errors**, **1311** unit (13 new). **Prod shipped as a cherry-pick** (`a2fd8207`) in a scratch `git worktree`, so a parallel session's uncommitted `directus/scripts/vps/` + `workers/push/` edits were never touched — and note **`origin/prod` moved mid-session** (the user pushed `5c22d637` while this was in flight), so the stale local `prod` ref made the first divergence read wrong: **`git fetch` before reasoning about dev↔prod**. ⚠ **`assets:check:prod` passing is NOT proof your build shipped** — it reported "all 95 assets reachable" against the **old** bundle; the marker crawl (`explorerDatapointPlaceholder`, `1.80.0`) across the full 93-chunk graph is what proved it, and a mid-publish read showed only **55** chunks, so re-read until the count is stable. ⚠ A first poll loop exited instantly because `grep "marker: [a-zA-Z]"` matches `marker: NOT FOUND`.

- **2026-08-11** **The Schreiber-Ausbildung gets one mailbox instead of three destinations (no migration, ext-only + OpnForm config, dev+prod)** — everything about a scorer course now lands in `scorerausbildung@wiedisync.kscw.ch`. It was scattered: the two OpnForm signup forms already copied that address on `send_to`, but their `reply_to` was **two personal inboxes** (`cp-smile@gmx.ch` on the DE form, `luca.canepa@gmail.com` on the EN one); the „Matchblatt eingegangen" upload notification defaulted to the general `admin@wiedisync.kscw.ch` club box; and the pass/fail exam-result mail went out with **no `Reply-To` at all** — `EMAIL_FROM` is `wiedisync@noreply.kscw.ch`, so the one mail a participant is most likely to answer replied into nothing. One exported `SCORER_AUSBILDUNG_EMAIL` in `scorer-exam.js` is now both the `SCORER_EXAM_NOTIFY_EMAILS` default and (imported, not retyped) the exam-result `Reply-To` in `wadmin.js`, so the notification recipient and the reply address cannot drift apart. ⚠ **`Reply-To`, deliberately not `From`** — sending *as* the box would need its own SES identity and `wiedisync.kscw.ch` is DMARC `p=quarantine`, so an unverified From there fails SPF and is silently quarantined at the receiver (the `finance@mail.kscw.ch` failure mode); `Reply-To` needs no verification. Checked rather than assumed that Directus's `MailService` does not filter it: `dist/services/mail/index.js:62` spreads `...emailOptions` into `sendMail`, so `replyTo` rides through like `attachments` already does. ⚠ The **OpnForm** half lives on `forms.kscw.ch`, not in this repo — `PUT /open/forms/:id/integrations/:iid` needs the **whole** `data` object echoed back, and Laravel's `validated()` drops any `data.*` key without a validation rule (`pdf_template_ids` has none — `null` on both forms, so harmless here, but it would silently eat a populated one). Both payloads were dumped to disk first and diffed after: `reply_to` is the only field that moved on either form, zero keys dropped. ⚠ **Dev cannot verify this one** — dev's `.env` carries `SCORER_EXAM_NOTIFY_EMAILS=` present-but-empty (the 2026-07-29 test-upload silencer), so the recipient change is inert there by design; prod leaves it unset and takes the new default, confirmed by `printenv` after the deploy. Prod shipped as a **cherry-pick** (`5c22d637`), pushed to `origin/prod` **before** `ext:deploy:prod` — the script ships `origin/<branch>` via `git archive` and never the worktree, so deploying first would have shipped prod's old code; done in a scratch `git worktree` so a parallel session's uncommitted `directus/scripts/vps/` edits were never touched. Gates: 1298 unit ✓, `node --check` on both edited files, dev 63 routes + `Extensions loaded`, prod likewise. **Mailbox verified against Migadu**: `scorerausbildung@wiedisync.kscw.ch` ("Scorer Ausbildung") exists on `wiedisync.kscw.ch` alongside `admin@` and `dmarc@`, with `may_receive: true`, `is_internal: false`, `expireable: false`, an empty `recipient_denylist` and no forwardings — so it accepts external mail and keeps it. ⚠⚠ **The Migadu API lives on `https://api.migadu.com/v1/`, NOT on `admin.migadu.com` (that host is the web panel).** Every call to `admin.migadu.com/api/v1/…` returns a plain `404 "Not Found"` — no `401`, no `WWW-Authenticate` — so a wrong-host call is indistinguishable from a bad credential or a domain the account does not own, and it cost a full round of wrong diagnoses here (blamed the account password, then the key, then guessed the domains sat under another organisation). ⚠ The tell that it was never an auth problem: a **control query against a domain known to hold mailboxes** (`spielplanung.kscw.ch`, whose `volleyball@`/`basketball@` sync over IMAP every 10 min) 404s identically — when a call that must return rows returns the same error as the call under investigation, suspect the transport, not the credential. Auth is Basic with the **account contact address** (`luca.canepa@gmail.com`) and an API key from the panel's My Account → API Keys; vaulted as `Migadu - admin API key (Claude)`. ⚠ Also note an SMTP `RCPT TO` probe is NOT available as a fallback from the VPS — **Hetzner blocks outbound port 25**, so it times out (`rc=124`).

- **2026-08-11** **`spielplanung-dev.kscw.ch` is finally a dev environment — sibling Pages project, not a branch alias (CF config only, no code, no migration)** — the misnomer flagged on 2026-08-05 and re-confirmed today: both scheduling hostnames were custom domains on the single `wiedisync-spielplanung` project (`production_branch: prod`), and **a CF Pages custom domain always serves the production deployment**, so both served a byte-identical prod bundle. `src/lib/api.ts` resolves `API_URL` from `window.location.hostname` and that host is not in the `isProd` pin list, so it fell through to the `VITE_DIRECTUS_URL` baked into the *prod* build → **anyone testing there was mutating prod data**. ⚠ **INFRA.md's suggested fix — "re-attach it as a dev branch alias" — is not a thing CF Pages supports.** The only mechanism is a **second project**: created `wiedisync-spielplanung-dev` (same GitHub source, `production_branch: dev`, `npm run build:scheduling`, `VITE_DIRECTUS_URL=https://directus-dev.kscw.ch`, `VITE_SCHEDULING_ORIGIN=https://spielplanung-dev.kscw.ch`, `preview_deployment_setting: none`), built it from `dev` and **verified it on its own `pages.dev` BEFORE moving the hostname**, so the domain never pointed at a project with no deployment. Then detach from the prod project → attach to the new one → `PATCH` the CNAME in the **active** zone (`4da8886…`, not the dead `5f4af0e…` one) → `active` in ~2 min, 200 throughout. ⚠ **Proof it actually changed behaviour, not just the label**: `VITE_DIRECTUS_URL` is inlined at build time, so the two bundles must differ — the prod-host chunk carries `https://directus.kscw.ch` **twice** (the `isProd` branch *plus* the inlined VITE value) while the dev-host chunk carries it **once**, its VITE slot now holding `directus-dev`. The two hostnames also serve different entry chunks for the first time. ⚠⚠ **Deleting the hostname was the operator's first instinct and was the wrong call** — it is the ONLY `.kscw.ch` host for the scheduling app, and the Directus session cookie is scoped to `.kscw.ch`, so no `*.pages.dev` origin can hold it; deleting it would have removed the only place authenticated scheduling flows can be tested (the reason it was created during the 2026-06-18 SSO migration). It also has a live code dependency: `email-template.js`'s `SCHEDULING_URL` points dev Directus at it, which keeps working unchanged. ⚠ The operator's dashboard deletion had **not** taken effect — the API still listed the domain and the DNS record; check the API, not the dashboard, before assuming a CF change landed. ⚠ A push to `dev` now builds **both** scheduling projects (`wiedisync-spielplanung` still has `preview_deployment_setting: all`) — harmless, two builds per push. ⚠ `rbw` returned an **empty** CF token mid-session and the resulting `9106 Missing Authorization` responses parsed as "domains: None / 0 DNS records", which reads exactly like "someone deleted the production domain" — always assert the token length before trusting an API answer.

- **2026-08-11** **The guests' door, native: `/e/:token` public event signup (migration 310, dev+prod, v1.79.0)** — closes the gap left when the share links shipped members-only. `events.public_share_token` (UNIQUE, CHECK `^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{24,64}$`, minted server-side from `crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('base64url')`) plus `event_public_signups`. ⚠ **The token, never the event id**: `/events/42` is enumerable, which is fine behind `AuthRoute` but not in a URL handed to strangers — here the token IS the authorisation, so it has to be unguessable. Table deliberately **not registered in Directus** (same posture as `site_text`/309 and `website_admin_access`/063): no `/items/event_public_signups` surface, no public policy to grant, reachable only through `public-event-signup.js`. ⚠⚠ **The trap the whole design exists to avoid**: a MEMBER signing up through a public door writes no `participations` row, so the event card reads "0 going" while the hall fills — the page calls `isAuthenticated()` and sends anyone with a session to `/events/:id` instead of rendering the form. Exposure is deliberately narrow: the public read returns title/when/where + a count and **never** `created_by`, `invited_roles`, the team/member junctions or any participant name (a roster carries minors' names and this link leaves the club); missing, revoked and deleted all answer **404** so the endpoint is not an oracle for tokens that once existed; Turnstile + per-IP limits on both routes, IP stored only as a salted sha256. Minting is admin/sport-admin/creator — same authority as the OpnForm door, deliberately **not** coach/TR, because this publishes a URL that reaches outside the club. Signups also join the existing `/events/:id/signups` merge, or the new door would have written rows nobody could see. **Verified on dev against the real DB**: mint → anonymous read (0 leaked fields) → captcha-less POST **400** → bogus-captcha POST 400 → unknown token 404 → malformed token 404 (regex-bounded, no query) → duplicate email refused **case-insensitively** by the `lower(email)` partial index while a second NULL-email walk-up is allowed → rotate kills the old link instantly (old 404 / new 200) → revoke 404s the link but **keeps the signups** → unauthenticated mint 401. Test rows deleted afterwards. **Prod**: `db:snapshot:prod` (386M offsite) → `db:migrate:prod` standalone (⚠ the chained form has printed a false "✓ Up to date" before — see [[migrate-silent-noop-gotcha]]) → objects verified in `information_schema` (column + 9-col table + 3 indexes) → `ext:deploy:prod` → post-deploy sweep 6/6 collections 200, `/kscw/public/teams` 200, **0 error-log entries**, and a full mint → anonymous read → SPA route 200 → revoke round-trip against a real event, left as found. ⚠ Dev's **items API is license-restricted** (`items is a restricted resource`, the documented keyless-dev state), so dev testing had to go through the `/kscw/*` knex endpoints and psql — the vault's `kscw-dev-admin-token` is also stale because the nightly refresh re-pins tokens; log in with `kscw-dev-admin-user` instead.

- **2026-08-11** **Backend batch to prod — migration 305 + the bp-sync and duty-arrival fixes (prod)** — the three commits that had been sitting on dev because they could not ride a frontend push: `b6d87492` (form re-publish spammed the club + duty arrival drift, carrying **305-forms-published-notified-at.sql**), `fc0e6f83` (fresh install replayed 269 migrations), `4ad664a4` (intra-club basketball fixture lost the away team). ⚠ **Cherry-picked to prod and pushed FIRST** — `ext:deploy` ships `origin/<branch>` via `git archive`, never the worktree, so deploying before the push would have shipped prod's old code; `scripts/deploy-extensions.sh` even warns on WIP (line 67), which is why the uncommitted `wadmin` work in the tree was never at risk. Gates on the prod branch before pushing: build ✓, 1288 unit, 130 script. ⚠⚠ **`npm run deploy:prod` did NOT complete as a chain** and this is worth knowing before trusting it: (a) `db:migrate:prod` printed **"✓ Up to date — 313 migrations applied"** — the documented [[migrate-silent-noop-gotcha]] shape — so the migration was verified out-of-band instead of taken on trust: `kscw_migrations` lists `305-forms-published-notified-at.sql` AND `information_schema` confirms `forms.published_notified_at timestamptz` exists and reads through the items API (already backfilled, first row `2026-07-14`); (b) `db:setup-perms:prod` **failed on a missing token** because the chain was run from the prod *worktree*, which has no `.env.local` — the script auto-loads it from the repo root, so this step must run from the main checkout; (c) it then needs the documented **password login, not the static token** (`kscw-prod-admin-token` lacks `directus_roles` → 403 at `POST /roles`), i.e. `ADMIN_EMAIL=… ADMIN_PASSWORD=$(rbw get kscw-prod-admin-user …)`. ⚠ The first perms attempt **502'd at step 3b** — not a script fault: `docker inspect` showed `RestartCount=0 exit=0 oom=false` with `StartedAt` seconds earlier on **both** containers, i.e. a deliberate `docker restart` from a **parallel session running `ext:deploy`** (that session also pushed `site-text` commits to dev in the same window). Re-run clean: **570 permissions, 0 errors**. Because that parallel deploy shipped `origin/prod` — which by then was my pushed `cf72a758` — the extension half was already live, verified by marker rather than redeployed (`published_notified_at` present in the container's `kscw-hooks/src`, `duty-arrival-parity.test.js` on disk); re-running `ext:deploy:prod` would only have restarted Directus again. ⚠ An early marker check read `0` because it grepped `kscw-hooks/dist` — the deployed layout is `src/`, no build step; a wrong path reads exactly like a missing deploy. `db:smoke:prod` still **cannot run from lenovoserver** (the `SMOKE_TEST_TOKEN` / `DIRECTUS_PROD_USER_TOKEN_MEMBER` slot in `.env.local` is empty — open since 2026-07-11), so the manual sweep stood in: 9/9 core collections 200 (`members`, `teams`, `games`, `trainings`, `events`, `participations`, `absences`, `notifications`, `forms`), `/kscw/public/teams` 200, and **0 error-log entries for the whole deploy window**.

- **2026-08-11** **Loading-race sweep: the boot spinner lifted before the data it was supposed to be waiting for (no migration, dev+prod)** — reported as "a lot of things get loaded after the page gets loaded". The mechanism was never missing: `usePageReady` already implements ONE app-level spinner (a routed page calls `useReportPageLoading(flag)`, Layout keeps a single fullscreen spinner up over chrome **and** content until it clears). The bug is that pages report a composite that misses some of their own queries. **Real fixes, 3 pages**: (1) **HomePage** — the events query is gated `enabled: !eventIdsLoading`, so while the visible-event IDs resolve the query has **not started** and `eventsLoading` is `false`; the gate has to include the query that gates the query, or the page reveals with an empty events section that pops in. Also folded in `tickerScopeLoading` (admin → `allActiveTeamsLoading`, else `memberTeamsLoading`) so the results ticker stops re-scoping after reveal. (2) **GamesPage** — on the rankings/scoreboard tabs the reported flag narrowed to `rankingsLoading` alone, so the team filter bar arrived after the reveal; team scope is chrome on every tab and now always gates, with the active tab's data on top. (3) **AbsencesPage** — `allTeams` backs the team filter and `visibleTeams`, both painted on first render, but its `isLoading` was never captured. ⚠ **Four pages I had flagged turned out to be CORRECT** once the composite behind the reported flag was resolved — reading `useReportPageLoading(x)` without following `x` overstates the problem: `ScorerPage`'s `upcomingLoading` = `gamesLoading || membersLoading || teamsDataLoading || memberTeamsLoading`; `CalendarPage`'s `!hasLoadedOnce` is component state so it **resets per mount** (gates every visit, while correctly not re-gating when paging months); `NewsArchivePage` gates first load but not "load more"; `TeamDetail`'s uncovered queries are `canManage`-gated admin extras. ⚠⚠ **Never fold a user-toggled query into the boot gate** — `ScorerPage`'s `pastLoading` is `enabled: showPast`, and including it would blank the whole page behind the fullscreen boot spinner every time somebody clicks "show past". A disabled query reports `isLoading === false` (v5: `isPending && isFetching`), so it is safe to *include* one that is only gated on `!!user`, but not one gated on a control the user flips. Sweep: of every routed page in `App.tsx`, only `EmbedGamesPage` (renders outside `Layout`, so the gate does not apply), `FeedbackPage` and `SignUpPage` have no gate, all three correctly. ⚠ Known remaining pop-in, deliberately not fixed: `AbsencesPage`'s `TeamWeeklySection` is a child component owning its own `useTeamAbsences` + spinner, so the parent cannot gate it without lifting the hook.

- **2026-08-11** **Three prod hostnames had been frozen on an old build for ~3 releases — the Pages projects were renamed and DNS was never repointed (no migration, CF config only)** — found while verifying the v1.78.0 push, which appeared not to deploy. It had deployed perfectly; `wiedisync.kscw.ch` was simply not being served by the `wiedisync` Pages project. **A renamed CF Pages project's old `*.pages.dev` subdomain keeps resolving and keeps serving the deployment that was current at rename time** — it does not 404 and it never follows new deployments — and the rename also drops the custom-domain registration. So `kscw-wiedisync` → `wiedisync` and `kscw-spielplanung` → `wiedisync-spielplanung` left all three custom hostnames CNAME'd at frozen snapshots while CI happily deployed to the renamed projects: `GET /pages/projects/wiedisync` listed `domains: ['wiedisync.pages.dev']` only, and `GET /pages/projects/kscw-wiedisync` answered *project not found* while `kscw-wiedisync.pages.dev` still returned 200. Evidence the apex was stale by **releases**, not minutes: CSP `frame-src https://challenges.cloudflare.com` (missing the 2026-08-10 PDF-previewer `'self' blob:` fix) and an in-app changelog topping out at ~v1.75.x — so **v1.76.0, v1.76.1 and the entire v1.77.0 security release never reached a single member**, and neither did the PDF previewer fix that was logged as shipped. `kscw.ch` was never affected (`kscw-website` was never renamed). Fix per hostname: `POST …/pages/projects/<project>/domains` (goes `initializing` → `pending` and **stays** pending until DNS points at the project) then `PATCH …/dns_records/<id>` to `<project>.pages.dev` keeping `proxied: true` → `active` in ~1–2 min. Cut over 2026-08-11 with a DNS rollback snapshot taken first: `wiedisync.kscw.ch` → `wiedisync.pages.dev`; `spielplanung.wiedisync.kscw.ch` + `spielplanung-dev.kscw.ch` → `wiedisync-spielplanung.pages.dev`. **Zero downtime** (200 throughout); the entry chunk flip-flops old/new for a minute or two as PoPs converge — propagation, not a failed cutover. Verified after: 10/10 requests on the new bundle, all v1.78.0 markers present, CSP now carries `'self' blob:`, `assets:check:prod` 95/95, and both spielplanung hostnames on `index-BmqQpC41.js` instead of the frozen `index-Cjg6Pp5q.js`. ⚠⚠ **The lesson that matters: every "verified live" check in the last three releases was pointed at `<project>.pages.dev`, which is always correct — the custom domain was the broken thing and it is the only one members use.** ⚠ `assets:check:prod` cannot catch this; it proves internal consistency of whatever build is served, and it passed 95/95 while the apex was three releases behind. ⚠ One command separates "propagation, wait" from "detached, never clears": `npx wrangler pages project list` prints each project's custom domains — a hostname missing there is not attached. ⚠ Also corrected in INFRA.md: the 2026-08-05 note claiming `spielplanung-dev.kscw.ch` was "a second custom domain on the same project" was wrong about the mechanism (both spielplanung hostnames were CNAMEs to the frozen alias — which is the real reason their bundles md5-matched); it is true as of today, and the `spielplanung-dev` misnomer still mutates PROD data. ⚠ `CLAUDE.md → Domains` called `wiedisync.pages.dev` the dev app; it is the **prod**-branch alias (dev is `dev.wiedisync.pages.dev`), harmless for data because `api.ts` pins prod Directus by hostname.

- **2026-08-11** **Share links for events, trainings and games — the app had no per-item URLs at all (no migration, dev + prod, v1.78.0)** — asked as "could we have a sharing concept … like 'you can sign up for this, follow this link'". The blocker was not sharing: `/events`, `/trainings` and `/games` were **list-only routes**, an open detail modal was local component state, and even a notification tap resolved to the bare list (`NotificationPanel.tsx` `getNavigationPath`) — so there was no URL to paste anywhere, and no way to link a member to the item a push had just told them about. Now three routes (`/events/:eventId`, `/trainings/:trainingId`, `/games/:gameId`), a `ShareActivityButton` in each detail modal (native share sheet → clipboard fallback; an `AbortError` is a **cancel**, not a failure, so it must not fall through and toast "copied"), and notifications deep-link to the item. ⚠ **The linked item is fetched BY ID in its own query (`useDeepLinkedActivity`), never looked up in the page's list** — the list is always filtered (team chip, show-past, the games tab), so resolving a link against it breaks good links for reasons the recipient cannot see; identical in kind to the week-scoped closure list two entries down, but worse, because the sender's filters are never the recipient's. ⚠ The modal is **derived** (`selected ?? linked`), not written into state from fetched data — that is the React #310/#301 render-phase-setState trap, cf. [[react-301-render-phase-setstate]]. ⚠ **`AuthRoute` now carries the attempted path as `?next=` and `LoginPage` honours it** instead of a hardcoded `navigate('/')`: without it every link handed to somebody not already signed in **on that device** dead-ends on the home page, which is the normal case for a phone opening a link from a chat. `safeReturnPath()` is the single guard both the writer and the reader use — a second, subtly different copy is how open redirects get in — and rejects `//evil.com` (starts with `/`, so a naive "is it relative?" check passes it and the browser leaves the site), `/\evil.com`, absolute URLs and the four auth gates (login loop); 8 unit tests pin it. ⚠ **`/games/:gameId` sits behind `AuthRoute` even though `/games` is public** — the detail modal carries the RSVP roster, so the deep link must not widen what an anonymous visitor can read. ⚠ **Missing and forbidden return the SAME message**, or the link becomes an existence oracle for events a member was never invited to. ⚠⚠ **Not to be confused with `events.signup_url`**, the *guests'* door (OpnForm at forms.kscw.ch): a MEMBER who signs up through that leaves no `participations` row and the event card reads "0 going" while 40 people signed up — the two doors now sit in the same modal, deliberately labelled apart. Scope was **members-only** by decision; the non-member public page (`/e/:token` + a share token + a signups table, reusing `publicEventsScope()` and the `/f/:slug` Turnstile machinery, both of which already exist) was explicitly deferred, not forgotten. Gates: `npm run build` ✓, `npm run lint` **0 errors** (80 warnings, all pre-existing), 1288 unit on dev / 1278 on prod. ⚠ **Prod shipped as a cherry-pick (`9e126f50`), not a merge**: `origin/prod..origin/dev` still held 4 unreleased commits — `fix(bp-sync)` and the form-re-publish/duty-arrival fix (which carries **migration 305** + `kscw-hooks` + `scorer-reminders`) and `fix(dr)`'s `regenerate-baseline.mjs` — so a merge would have forced a prod schema migration and a Directus restart for a frontend-only feature. Those 4 remain on dev awaiting their own release. ⚠ **`CLAUDE.md` still calls the event-signup-forms feature "unreleased on dev" — that note is now stale**: `git diff --stat prod dev` shows `event-signup-form.js`, `EventSignupsModal.tsx` and friends **identical** on both branches. ⚠ The cherry-pick was done in a **stale `prod` worktree left by another session** under `/tmp/claude-1000/…/854bb77f-…/scratchpad/wt-prod` (it holds the branch, so `git checkout prod` in the main tree fatals) — it was clean and exactly at `origin/prod`, so it was used as-is rather than removed; the main tree never left `dev`, cf. [[lenovoserver-parallel-edits]].

- **2026-08-10** **A saved hall closure was invisible — the closure manager moves from a modal to its own page (no migration, dev + prod)** — reported as "after adding one it does not show in current?": a `KWI A/B/C` closure for the **13.12.2026 VB U20 tournament** wrote all three rows correctly (`hall_closures` 726/727/728, source `admin`) and then never appeared in "Current closures", so it read as a failed save. Root cause is scoping, not writing: `ClosureManager` took its list as a **prop**, and from the Hallenplan that prop is the page's own week-scoped query (`start_date <= Sunday AND end_date >= Monday`) — a December closure cannot match a August week, and the modal has no way to know. Closures now live at `/admin/hallenplan/closures` (member app) and `/admin/terminplanung/closures` (scheduling app) — one `ClosuresPage` mounted twice, owning its own query with an **Upcoming (default) / All** toggle that is never week-bound; a save refetches, flashes the new row for 5 s and toasts what landed ("Closure added: 13.12.2026, 3 halls"), so the write is visibly confirmed. Also: the list is a `<Table>` per the data-list rule (source column hides `<sm`, actions stack), the **To** picker is bounded by **From**, and choosing a sync-owned source now warns — `schulferien-sync` and `gcal-sync` each reconcile `.where('source', …)` against their own feed, so a hand-made closure filed as `gcal`/`school_holidays` is deleted on the next run. `ClosureManager.tsx` deleted; all **three** call sites navigate to the page (the third, `calendar/HallenplanView.tsx`, is easy to miss — grep before deleting a shared modal). ⚠ Context for the original question: the two tournaments were only in **`scheduling_global_blocks`** (the superadmin "Blocked dates (whole club)" panel), which nothing outside the Spielplanung reads — the calendar / Hallenplan / iCal / training auto-cancel all key off `hall_closures`, so the two are complementary records, not duplicates, and both are needed. Prod shipped as a **cherry-pick** (`4c125532`), not a merge — dev was 28 commits ahead carrying migrations 305-308 and the unreleased forms work. ⚠ A parallel session was pushing to prod throughout: the first push was rejected as behind, the rebase landed, and CF Pages then served **two** builds that were not mine before the third was — verified as always by crawling for a marker string (`admin/hallenplan/closures` in the `App-*` chunk, `terminplanung/closures` in `SchedulingApp-*`), never by the entry-hash flip, cf. [[cf-pages-hash-flip-not-your-build]]. `assets:check:prod` 404'd one lazy chunk on the first run and was clean 40 s later — propagation, not a broken deploy.

- **2026-08-10** **12 of 14 HU16 basketball players were flagged as guests on their own team — roster corrected on prod, duplicate ClubDesk chips removed (data-only: no migration, no code change)** — reported as "why so many guest/duplicates" from a screenshot of the ClubDesk `BB HU16` group, which held 17 × `(Spieler*in)` + 12 × `(Guest)` + 2 × `(Trainer*in)`, with all 12 Guest chips sitting on contacts that also carried `(Spieler*in)`. Root cause is one roster edit, not a sync bug: on **2026-07-11 12:56:18–12:56:54** the HU16 coach clicked the roster editor's `K / G` toggle once on 12 of the team's 14 rows (`directus_activity` 469828–469869, origin `wiedisync.kscw.ch`, revision deltas all `guest_level: 0 → 1`; the button cycles blank → G1 → G2 → G3, so that is one deliberate click per player). They are **not** guests — all 12 were core (`guest_level = 0`) on HU16 or HU14 in 2025/26, they are the squad, and their only other 2026/27 row is the `H-Classics 1LR` league umbrella. The 2026-07-15 mass group-assignment run then mapped `guest_level > 0` → `'<group> (Guest)'` and added that chip while the old `(Spieler*in)` stayed, because **ClubDesk group assignment is add-only** — 12 flips × add-only = the 12 duplicate pairs on screen. ⚠ **The real damage was ahead, not behind**: `trg_participations_guest_block` raises on a guest confirming a game for that team, so all 12 would have been unable to RSVP yes to their own fixtures the moment the 2026/27 ProBasket schedule lands — team 84 currently carries only last season's 16 games and 0 participations from the 12, which is why nobody had hit it yet. Fees were never at risk: `guestMemberIdSet` requires guest on **every** team and their umbrella row is core, so the register got `Gast = Nein` and the full rate. Fix: `UPDATE member_teams SET guest_level = 0` on the 12 rows (team 84, season 2026/27, narrow `WHERE` so a re-run is a no-op) → 14/14 core; then `clubdesk-remove-group.mjs` stripped the 12 `BB HU16 (Guest)` chips — **preview 12/12 previewed → commit 12/12 removed**, confirmed by a read-only `"ZZZ probe"` chip read (`chips=["BB HU16 (Spieler*in)", …]`, no Guest). ⚠ The raw-SQL update **bypasses the Directus revision trail** (the documented psql escape hatch), so this entry is its audit record. ⚠ Two ClubDesk runs failed back-to-back at exactly the 45 s `page.goto(…, networkidle)` timeout before the third succeeded; a diagnostic using the scripts' exact launch options then loaded the login page in 0.5 s, and `curl` + DNS were fine from the same image throughout — a transient stall at ClubDesk, **not** a broken selector or a blocked container, so retry before debugging. Club-wide the wrong-function check is now down to **1 row** (a single `VB D2`). ⚠ Left for a decision: 4 contacts (Klemenz 1001267, Liebert 1001255, Minder 1001064, Schölly 1001151) hold `BB HU16 (Spieler*in)` with **no 2026/27 HU16 roster row** — all four are `Kein Mitglied` + `wiedisync_active = false`, yet still billed `BB Jugend Meisterschaft` and still on the 2026/27 umbrella roster, which is precisely what hides them from every check: `missing` needs a roster row before it can expect a group, `strays` needs **zero** current-season rows, and the Sunday `clubdesk-group-cleanup.sh` auto-remove envelope inherits the stray definition. Also Brugger Julien (member 733, core HU16) carries no `clubdesk_id` at all and is invisible to ClubDesk.
- **2026-08-10** **v1.77.0 promoted to prod — register status, the otn_bb drop and the BB fee rise, in one release (migrations 302+303+304, prod)** — the first full `dev` → `prod` **merge** in a while rather than the recent run of cherry-picks: `origin/prod..origin/dev` was 12 commits and the merge result diffed **byte-identical to `origin/dev`** across the whole tree (the check that proves prod carried nothing of its own; the 5 conflicts — CHANGELOG, ChangelogPage, package.json, DEVLOG, ExplorerGrid — were all the earlier cherry-picks colliding with their originals and were resolved to dev). ⚠ **Deploy order was deliberately NOT the usual `deploy:prod` chain**: 303 DROPS a column, and per CLAUDE.md a drop is the reverse of an add — `ext:deploy` FIRST (so no live code still reads it), THEN the migration, THEN a container **restart** (a stale in-memory schema snapshot 500s *every* `/items/members/*` call, including reads that never named the column). Sequence run: `db:snapshot:prod` (384M, offsite) → `ext:deploy:prod` → `db:migrate:prod` (302, 303, 304) → `docker restart directus-kscw` → `db:setup-perms:prod` (**570 / 0 errors**) → `db:baseline:prod`. Verified on prod: 706/710 members carry a `register_status`, 670 an Eintritt, 34 an Austritt; `otn_bb` is gone from `information_schema`; all 7 BB rates read +10. ⚠⚠ **The drop had a live straggler the release's own sweep missed** — `finance.js` still selected `otn_bb`, so `GET /kscw/finance/members/:id/fee` returned **500** the moment 303 applied (`column "otn_bb" does not exist`), while `ext:deploy`'s smoke check passed because it probes `/kscw/public/teams`. This is the documented lesson recurring in a new shape: **the deploy smoke test only proves the bundle loaded — curl the endpoints the migration actually touches.** The parallel actor had already committed the fix (`bb4d3126` on dev, cherry-picked to prod as `100085bb`) by the time it surfaced; a second `ext:deploy:prod` shipped it and the route returned 200. Post-deploy sweep: 8/8 core collections 200 (`members`, `teams`, `games`, `trainings`, `events`, `participations`, `absences`, `notifications`), the new `POST /kscw/csp-report` 204s end-to-end and lands in the JSONL, **0 errors in the log for the whole deploy window**, `assets:check:prod` 95/95. ⚠ `db:smoke:prod` still cannot run from lenovoserver — the prod smoke-token slot in `.env.local` is **empty** (open since 2026-07-11), so the non-admin member path was checked by hand instead. Frontend: CF Pages served **v1.76.1** for the whole backend deploy and the v1.77.0 build landed after; confirmed by transitively crawling all 93 chunks for `explorerGridColRegisterStatus` rather than trusting the entry-hash flip — the hash HAD changed while the markers were still absent, i.e. [[cf-pages-hash-flip-not-your-build]] exactly as documented.
- **2026-08-10** **The fee increase ran the other way — the register was already right, and 113 members would have been over-billed (migrations 307+308, dev+prod)** — follow-up to the +CHF 10 entry below, and it inverts its premise. Before flagging anything for a ClubDesk overwrite I ran the REAL fee engine (`deriveMitgliederbeitrag`, imported into a throwaway script) over all **674** active linked prod members and diffed it against `clubdesk_export`. Result: **ClubDesk already held the new price** (31 Erwerbstätige at 520, 101 Jugend at 320, 47 Minis at 220, 14 1. Liga at 570, 13 Lernende at 420) — the club had raised the fees in the register and wiedisync + the website were the ones lagging. Exactly **11** rows were stale. ⚠ **The claim in the entry below that “ClubDesk keeps the old numbers” was inferred from the fill-only rule and was WRONG** — true for 11 members, not 255. Measure the values, do not reason from the sync rule. An ungated “wiedisync wins” would have rewritten **124** register cells to fix 11: the other 113 are deliberate club decisions — 51 un-surcharged bases, **24 waived zeroes**, 10 rows priced ABOVE their category, 28 per-person amounts (155, 270, 280, 430, 130). So the correction goes BOTH ways. **307** flags the 11 (wiedisync wins) and **308** pins the register's amount into `fee_base_override` for the other 113 (the register wins) — because the native 2026/27 dues run prices through `feeBreakdown()`, so without it the August invoices would have gone out **CHF 19'355 over** what the register says, including a full bill to 24 people the club had zeroed. ⚠⚠ **`fee_base_override` is the BASE, ClubDesk's cell is the TOTAL** — copying the total straight in makes the engine re-add the CHF 100 surcharge on top of a number that already contains it (540 → 640). 308 therefore writes `fee_surcharge_override=false` alongside, and stores a pure guest's base **110 ABOVE** the billed amount to absorb the guest reduction (8 of the 113). Every row was verified by re-running `feeBreakdown()` with the written values — 113/113 emit the register's amount — and again against live prod after the migration: **623 members bill exactly the register, 11 deliberately +10 above a stale one, 0 disagreements**. Code: `buildPushCsv`'s Mitgliederbeitrag cell stops being unconditionally fill-only and now honours a pending push that NAMES the field, via the same `registerCell` gate as the register triple; nothing writes that change entry automatically (not the hook, not a category edit), so **only a migration can authorise an overwrite** — 3 new tests pin all three arms. ⚠ The 11 corrections have NOT reached ClubDesk yet: they ride the next sync-up a human APPROVES, and until then those 11 are skipped by the Saturday sync-down (302's contract). ⚠ **Migration-number collision**: a parallel session claimed `305` two minutes before mine and both landed on dev — renumbered mine to 307/308 and fixed forward (dev carries both filenames in `kscw_migrations`; harmless because 307 re-asserts and 308 is guarded on `fee_base_override IS NULL`, proven by re-running them). Prod shipped as a **cherry-pick** (`efb658fc`), not a merge — dev carried the parallel session's unreleased forms work — done in a DETACHED worktree because their stale `prod` worktree still holds the branch. Data-only: no perms run, no SCHEMA rebaseline. Rollback if ever needed: `fee_base_override`/`fee_surcharge_override` → NULL for the 113 IDs listed in 308, `clubdesk_push_pending=false` for the 11 in 307.
- **2026-08-10** **Basketball fees +CHF 10 for 2026/27, and the passive fee that was two different numbers (migration 304, dev; prod pending approval)** — user decision: every BASKETBALL category rises by CHF 10 (Erwerbstätige 1. Liga 560→570, Erwerbstätige 510→520, Lernende/Studierende 1. Liga 460→470, Lernende/Studierende 410→420, Jugend Meisterschaft 310→320, Minis Turnier 210→220). Volleyball untouched. Liga, 1 legacy `BB 2 Trainings`). **kscw-website `76000a6`**: the Mitgliedschaft fee table + the registration form's category labels (de/en) carry the new amounts, and **Passivmitglied — club-wide, not a basketball category — now appears in BOTH sport tables** (it was listed only under basketball, so a volleyball reader had to look under the other sport to find their own fee); the BB-prefixed i18n keys were renamed to `membershipCategoryPassive*` and used by both. ⚠ **The passive fee was two numbers**: CHF 40 in the website table, the rate schedule and the push map, but **CHF 50** in the registration confirmation emails in all five locales (and in three dead `registrationBBFee*` i18n keys) — 40 is correct (user), the emails now say 40 and the dead keys are gone so the wrong number cannot be copied back in. That is the right default while the club bills 2026/27 from wiedisync, but it means the register and the invoice will disagree on paper until somebody deliberately re-pushes; the fill-only rule exists precisely to stop a sync from overwriting per-person manual amounts, so that would be a separate, explicit decision. **Prod NOT deployed**: needs `db:migrate:prod` (304) + `ext:deploy:prod`, no perms change, no SCHEMA rebaseline (data-only).
- **2026-08-10** **"Ehrenmitglied" is two facts and they disagreed — the mailing list followed the wrong one (no migration, dev; prod pending)** — follow-up to the register-status work, from checking the other half of the original question. ClubDesk holds honorary membership BOTH as a register Status and as the **Ehrenmitglieder group**, and on prod they diverge: **15 in the group, 12 by status, 10 both**. All 15 group members pay `Gratis`, and the 5 whose status reads `Aktivmitglied` include two on a current roster — i.e. honorary members who still play. ClubDesk's Status is single-valued and doubles as the **billing** axis, so it structurally cannot say "honorary AND active" and files those five as active. ⚠ Consequence, live until today: the club mailbox's Ehrenmitglieder chip resolved on **status**, so every mailing to the Ehrenmitglieder silently reached 12 and **missed 5**. The category must be changed in ClubDesk. Same for the group: adding Birrer + Zehnder to Ehrenmitglieder is manual there (CSV group import is a proven no-op). Note the new pending-push guard does **not** save it: the hook flags `clubdesk_push_pending` only for the register triple + iban/ahv, not for `beitragskategorie`, and two earlier down-sync passes write that column without the guard anyway. The honest answer was no: honorary membership existed ONLY as a ClubDesk register Status + a ClubDesk group, both mirrored read-only (the explorer's *Honorary*/*Former* columns are a regex over the scraped `gruppen` string), and "ehemalig" could only be approximated by flipping `kscw_membership_active` — one boolean that cannot say WHY somebody left, cannot express Ehrenmitglied at all, and carries no date. **Migration 302** adds `members.register_status` (ClubDesk's 7-value picklist **verbatim**, CHECK-enforced — the values are pushed into the legal register, where a re-spelling is a new picklist entry, not a synonym), plus real `eintritt`/`austritt` **date** columns, backfilled from the register mirror (dev: 526 Aktiv / 87 Passiv / 29 Kein Mitglied / 27 Zwischenjahr / 24 Ehemalig / **12 Ehrenmitglied**, 3 unlinked → NULL; 669 entry dates). ⚠ NULL is its own state — "the register never told us" — and is deliberately not conflated with 'Kein Mitglied'. **These are the first ClubDesk-OWNED cells an UPDATE push may write** (`CD_PUSH_HEADERS` had kept Status off update rows since the two-set split). The contract the user chose is *wiedisync wins until pushed, then ClubDesk*, and it is held by three separate pieces: the members hook flags `clubdesk_push_pending` **and records the field** in `clubdesk_push_changes`; the Saturday sync-down **skips pending rows** (without that, a Monday edit is silently reverted whenever nobody approved the push in between — the whole failure mode); and `registerCell()` sends a cell **only when the pending change names that exact field**, so an IBAN-triggered push never rewrites a status from a copy up to a week stale. Everything else echoes ClubDesk's own cell back, so an empty wiedisync value can never blank the register. Two-sided divergence surfaces as an ordinary Data Health **conflict** (all three joined `computeClubdeskDrift`), which is what makes "the register wins once the push landed" observable rather than merely intended. ⚠ **A unit test caught a real bug before it shipped**: Status and Austritt were resolved independently, so a member departed-but-not-yet-pushed (flagged for some unrelated field) would echo the register's `Aktivmitglied` while *filling* its empty Austritt from wiedisync — writing into ClubDesk exactly the contradictory pair `members_austritt_needs_departed_status` refuses here. The exit date is now gated on **the status cell the row actually sends**. The same coupling bit the migration itself: the CHECK fired mid-backfill on Timo Beyerlein, who is `Passivmitglied` **with** an Austritt of 30.07.2026 in the register (ClubDesk does not enforce the pairing), so the backfill drops an orphan date rather than inventing a departure. UI (`/admin/explore`): status dropdown + both dates, a status filter incl. **"not set"**, three grid columns. Picking a departed status prefills today's exit date (never overwriting one already set) and — behind one confirm that **names both flags** — switches off club membership and app access; picking an active one clears the date. Those two flags are danger-zone fields everywhere else, so writing them from here is a deliberate, documented exception to "one column, one editing surface". Perms: all three own-READABLE (`MEMBER_DERIVED_READ_FIELDS`) and **never** member-editable — a member who could set their own `register_status` could re-admit themselves — plus read for finance. Not in `MEMBER_VISIBLE_FIELDS`: club-wide would publish who is an Ehrenmitglied and who resigned when, to ~700 people. **Migration 303 drops the legacy `otn_bb`** (asked for separately).
