the SDK has **zero** runtime deps, so the lockfile diff is exactly one package. ⚠ The install also corrected a pre-existing drift: `package-lock.json` still carried `version: 1.63.1` against package.json's 1.68.0 — someone bumped the manifest across five releases without regenerating the lock. **Vulnerability sweep in the same pass — 4 highs → 2, and the 2 that remain are deliberate.** None of the four came from the SDK (it has no deps). `npm audit fix` (**lockfile-only — `package.json` byte-identical afterwards**) cleared transitive `brace-expansion` ×5 paths → 5.0.9 (GHSA-mh99-v99m-4gvg + GHSA-rgw5-rvv9-x895, both cvss 7.5 DoS, under archiver-utils/readdir-glob/rimraf/zip-stream) and `postcss` 8.5.22 → 8.5.25 (GHSA-6g55-p6wh-862q path traversal via attacker-controlled `sourceMappingURL`), and incidentally moved `react-router-dom` 7.18.1 → 7.18.2 within the declared `^7.15.0`. ⚠ **`react-router` + `react-router-dom` left flagged on purpose, and `--force` would have made things worse.** The advisory is GHSA-qwww-vcr4-c8h2, *"**RSC Mode** CSRF Bypass Allows Action Execution Before 400 Response"* — and this app has no RSC: plain `<BrowserRouter>` in a Vite SPA (`App.tsx:149`, `SchedulingApp.tsx:72`), no `react-router.config.*`, no `@react-router/*` server packages, no `createRequestHandler`/`ServerRouter`/RSC import anywhere. **There is also no forward fix**: the flagged range is `7.12.0 - 8.2.0` while `latest` is **7.18.2**, i.e. the newest published release is itself inside the range, so npm's only offer is a **downgrade to react-router-dom 7.11.0** — seven minors backwards, which npm's own `fixAvailable` labels `isSemVerMajor: true`. Taking it would trade seven minors of real fixes for a mitigation of a mode we don't run. Re-check when react-router ships >8.2.0. Gates ✓ after the fix (build, lint 0 errors / 82 pre-existing warnings, 1015 unit + 1 skipped, 122 scripts). No repo migration, no perms, no version bump — infra-only, invisible to members; the SDK half rides out on the next CF Pages push.

- **2026-08-05** **Data Explorer edited nine closed-value member columns as free text (dev+prod, no migration)** — reported as "birthday visibility i can modify it as text, but pretty sure on edit profile it is a dropdown". Root cause in `ExplorerMemberFields.tsx`: `detectKind()` chose the editor from the **value**, never the column — and a varchar holding `'hidden'` is indistinguishable from a name, while a NULL one carries no hint at all. Only three columns had ever been special-cased (`nationalitaet_codes`, `trainer_licences`, `federation_of_origin`); everything else with a closed set fell through to a text box, so a typo like `Hidden` saved cleanly and then silently stopped matching the `full`/`year_only`/`hidden` switch everywhere else. Now keyed off the column, ahead of the value heuristics: **7 dropdowns** (`birthdate_visibility`, `sex`, `anrede`, `language`, `consent_decision`, `transfer_status`) and **2 checkbox grids** (`role`, `position`) that were **JSON textareas** — where a typo 400s on `members_role_values_valid` and a valid-but-wrong string silently grants or drops access. ⚠ `sektion` + `beitragskategorie` deliberately stay **free text with a `<datalist>`**, not a gate: prod holds off-list values (`VB Schüler*in Meisterschaft mit Abzug`, `VB Studenten/Lehrlinge mit Abzug`, `BB 2 Trainings`) and gating them would make those rows uneditable. Same reasoning inside the dropdowns — an unrecognised stored value keeps its own entry labelled `(unrecognised)`, so opening the editor on such a row and saving something else cannot overwrite it. `role`'s list is the **CHECK constraint**, not the Directus field choices: those still omit `website_admin` (1 live holder) and `position` omits `staff_only` (28) — ⚠ **so Directus' own admin UI at /admin still offers the wrong sets**, a `directus_fields` metadata migration nobody has written. `position` imports `memberPositions.ts` (`getSelectablePositions` keeps any legacy value already on the record — 223 members hold `other`) rather than a second copy of the list. New `memberFieldOptions.ts` is its own module because a component file exporting constants is an ESLint **error** here (react-refresh/only-export-components). Every option list checked against the live prod distincts before shipping — all 706 members' current values are covered, nobody renders as `(unrecognised)`. **Two capitalisation bugs found on the follow-up pass** (user: "everything shall be capitalized in the UI"): the position i18n keys live in the **`teams`** namespace, not `auth` — the first cut would have rendered the bare key `positionSetter` in both display and edit mode, which tsc cannot catch since it is a runtime string lookup; and pre-existing, eight lines from a correct one, `tm.sport` printed raw (`volleyball`) in the member's teams table while the team card already used `capitalize()`. ⚠ The rule as written in CLAUDE.md enumerates strings you *author* — both of these were values rendered straight out of Postgres, which never pass through a locale file, so grepping the locales finds nothing; admin surfaces are where this bites because they print columns verbatim by design. Also noted, not acted on: `TableBrowser.tsx` + `RecordEditModal.tsx` + `SchemaViewer.tsx` are **dead code** (nothing mounts them) — they handle selects correctly from the Directus schema, which is why the bug only ever showed in the explorer; and `SCHEMA.sql` is **stale** — 281/282 are applied on prod but the baseline still carries the pre-BB `trainer_licences` regex, so a `db:baseline:prod` is owed. Gates ✓ (build, lint 0 errors, 1015 unit). ⚠ Frontend-only: no `ext:deploy`, no schema, no perms. No version bump — superadmin-only surface, and `ChangelogPage`'s audience is members.

Phase (`Vorlage_Jugend_allgemein` has exactly **38** date rows, 19.09→13.12, a byte-perfect match), while `Vorlage_Senior_innen` has **93**, 25.09.2026→**09.05.2027**, matching the Sperrdaten PDF's "Damen + Herren 1. Liga Interregional 19.09.26 – 09.05.27". So **Lions D1 (4445) and Herren 1 (1348) — the only two teams the Anleitung obliges to file by 17.08.2026 — were exporting 38 of 93 required rows.** Windows are now per league, verbatim from the PDF (incl. the doc's own 2026/2027 typos, flagged in comments), plus the **six** missing blackouts (Sport/Fasnachtsferien 30.01–14.02.27, Osterferien both cantonal variants, Final Four 17./18.04.27, Classics Final 25.04.27, Ostern 26.–30.04.27) — invisible until now only because all six fall past 13.12. `AUTOMATIC_BB_SOURCE_IDS` cut from 5 to `['4445','1348']`: the Anleitung names "Herren 1. Liga, Damen 1. Liga, alle interregionalen U14 bis U22 Ligen" and KSCW has **no** interregional junior team (DU12 is a Turnier, HU12/HU16 are regional); per-team export still reaches every team on demand (user's call). `basketballGroups.ts` regenerated from the **"Prov. Gruppeneinteilung"** sheet only — ⚠ the workbook's two sheets disagree **by design** (Klubübersicht's Kategorie is the registration category and is stale: it still files Seuzach-Stammheim D1 and Feldkirch D1 in D1LRA where Gruppeneinteilung has moved both to D2LRA). **"Sat 07.11.2026 is just empty"** was two defects stacking: `vbHallsByDate` discarded `start_time` and blocked a hall **all day** off one booked slot, and the page filtered cells to `free|game` then `return null`-ed an empty row — so a Saturday with volleyball in KWI A **and** B **and** C (verified on prod: all three at 13:30, plus C at 11:00) rendered header-only, and the `statusVbUsing`/`statusUnavailable` legend entries could never appear at all. Occupancy moved into a pure, unit-tested `hallOccupancy.ts` (⚠ a null `start_time` still blocks the whole day — never silently frees a hall; A+B↔A/B collision explicit; 30-min changeover so a 13:30–15:30 VB match occupies 13:00–16:00 and leaves the 11:00 and 16:00 pitches alone). **Latent export bug fixed in the same pass**: free pitches were emitted as one window from first to last, so a hall free at 11:00 and 18:30 but busy between declared **11:00–20:30** available to ProBasket — now grouped into maximal contiguous runs, capped at the template's three windows. **Migration 278** adds `basketball_slots` + `basketball_team_rules` + `game_scheduling_seasons.bb_slot_config`; rules are a **table, not a json map on the season** (a stringified-team-id key rots silently — exactly how `BB_GROUPS['DU10']` became unreachable). ⚠ Tier is an explicit seeded column, **never** `teams.league`: prod team 76 "Herren 2 H3" carries `league='H3LS'` but is registered H2LRA for 26/27, so a league-derived classifier gives it *soft* Ferien when the rules say hard. Seeded **9 of 11** teams — the two DU18 rows are deliberately absent because the sheet's "Spark"/"Fire" nicknames exist in **neither** the DB (`1xDU18`/`2xDU18`) **nor** the ProBasket register (`DU18 A`/`DU18 B`), prod `team_links` pairs the two squads the **opposite** way from the sheet, and the user ruled that 7182 is really **DU16**; the migration asserts the 9-row count and RAISEs rather than silently inserting 0 on a varchar join miss. **Migrations 279/280** add the Basketplan club registry (main + secondary contact) and give `game_scheduling_club_portals` a `sport` discriminator so one portal codebase serves both. ⚠ **That sharing exposed a live hazard in the volleyball half**: six queries in `game-scheduling.js` had no sport filter, and the worst was the club-portal **send** — a volleyball admin clicking Send would have mailed **every basketball opponent club** a volleyball invite carrying a basketball token; `clubPortalByToken` was token-only (tokens are globally unique) and the ensure lookup keyed `(season, club_id)` where `club_id` is only unique *within* a sport. Also restored the one-segment `terminplanung/:token` route the portal work had **replaced** rather than supplemented — no catch-all in that block, so a pasted **volleyball** opponent link had started rendering blank. `dry_run` now defaults to **true** server-side (fail-safe: a curl omitting the field cannot blast 63 clubs). **Nothing was sent** — the only `dry_run:false` in the tree sits behind a button's `onClick`. Basketplan contact scraping ships as an **opt-in script, not in the nightly cron**, selectors marked unverified (that page has not been read with credentials). **The "missing" mailbox was a stale premise, not a bug**: `basketball@spielplanung.kscw.ch` went live ~22:00 on 04.08 and both forwarded ProBasket documents are in `scheduling_emails` (31366/31367, with attachments); the 10-min sync cron shows 0 errors in 24h. ⚠ `SCHEDULING_MAILBOX_SYNC_DAYS` unset → 60-day IMAP window, so anything sent before ~06.06.2026 is invisible until one backfill run at 365. ⚠ Migrations 278–280 are numerically **earlier** than the already-applied 281/282 (the operator committed those in parallel) — safe, since the runner filters on `!applied.has(name)` and ignores ordering, and the two sets touch disjoint tables. Idempotency proven on dev: all three re-applied under `ON_ERROR_STOP=1`, and a 289-line schema snapshot diffed **empty**. Gates ✓ (build, lint 0 errors, 1015 unit, 122 scripts, `node --check`, i18n 357 keys × 5 locales with 0 missing). ⚠ Deploy is order-critical — chained `npm run deploy:*` only: 278–280 add columns the new endpoint code selects, and the `game-scheduling.js` sport filter reads the column 280 creates. **Prod sequence run 2026-08-05**: `db:snapshot:prod` → `db:migrate:prod` **standalone** (per [[migrate-silent-noop-gotcha]] — chaining it after the snapshot can print a false "up to date") applying 278/279/280 → `db:setup-perms:prod` **re-run after** the migrations so the two new-collection grants bind to tables that now exist (553 permissions, 0 errors; the reconcile printed no revokes/dedups on the second run, proving it idempotent) → `ext:deploy:prod` (`2d547534`). Verified on prod, not asserted: the 3 tables exist, `basketball_team_rules` = **9** rows (the migration's own seed assertion held), `sport` + `bb_slot_config` columns present, both new endpoints answer **403** (registered and gated, not 404), `assets:check:prod` 95/95 reachable, and both CF Pages projects serve the new build (member app `App-DQNPu6MA.js` carries the `terminplanung/bb` route, scheduling app's entry chunk carries the new `reason_volleyball` key — ⚠ the CF-built hashes differ from a local `dist/` because the build env supplies different `VITE_*`, so compare by CONTENT not by hash). ⚠ dev→prod was a real **merge** with 10 cherry-picked duplicates on the prod side; conflicts were exactly the 4 release/doc files (`package.json`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `ChangelogPage.tsx`, `DEVLOG.md`), every one of them "prod side empty / dev side adds" → resolved `--theirs` (see [[kscw-dev-prod-divergence]]). **Permission escalation closed the same day**: `Sport Admin → KSCW Admin` (`admin_access=true`, live since 2026-03-29) revoked along with 5 redundant Superuser rows and 432 duplicates — `directus_access` role-level rows went 19 groups → 13 clean rows, and only Administrator + Superuser retain `admin_access`. Philip Urech (430) `is_spielplaner` set true on prod; ⚠ his policies had been attached **by hand** on 04.08 and were classified stale by §12/§14, i.e. scheduled for silent deletion on the next deploy — see [[spielplaner-flag-not-policy]]. **Follow-up the same day — migrations 283 + 284 (dev+prod) close the constraint sheet completely (11/11 team rules, 5/5 partner pairs).** 278's refusal to seed the two DU18 rows was **over-cautious**: it blocked on the nicknames "Spark"/"Fire" being unfindable, but the sheet's own **parentheticals** are the key and map exactly onto the team names — `(1x)` → id 73 `1xDU18` (5697), `(2x)` → id 72 `2xDU18` (7182). ⚠ Team 72 keeps its misnomer: `full_name` is `KSC Wiedikon DU16`, `league` `DU16B`, and Basketplan fixtures for 7182 show it playing as DU16 — so it is seeded `category='youth'`, not `'u18'` (inert in practice: `u18` is only called out for the Friday 20:00 pitch and both teams are weekend-only). **The real thing the nickname block was hiding**: `basketball_team_rules` has **no partner column** — the sheet's "cannot play the same time as" and "adjacent games" columns live in `team_links`, and prod had the two DU18 pairs **the wrong way round** (72↔Rhinos / 73↔Lions where the sheet says 73↔Rhinos / 72↔Lions), had `H1↔HU18` and `H2↔HU14` **missing entirely**, and stored the rest as `diff`. ⚠ `diff` loses half the intent: every populated sheet row names the **same** partner in *both* the hard and soft column, which together means **back-to-back** (a shared coach), i.e. `adjacent` — which `highlightFor` treats as both a same-time conflict *and* a neighbouring-slot suggestion, whereas `diff` only holds them apart and never pulls them together. All five pairs are now `adjacent` (user decision: the sheet wins over the older links). 284 asserts the exact end-state string rather than trusting row counts, so a renamed/archived team fails loudly instead of half-applying; both migrations applied **twice** on dev to prove idempotency (283: 2 rows then 0; 284: 3 updates + 2 inserts then a full no-op). ⚠ Sheet-label traps codified in both migration headers: "Rhinos (D2)" is a typo for **Rhinos D3** (no KSCW D2 exists in any season), and "H2"/"H4" name **leagues, not team ordinals** — they are `Herren 2 H3` (76) and `Herren 3 (Unicorns) H4` (77).

- **2026-08-05** **BB officials reconciliation + `trainer_licences` learns the basketball ladder (migrations 281 + 282, dev+prod)** — asked as "check that all these people have the respective OTR/OTN in wiedisync, and compare with ClubDesk" against a fresh ClubDesk basketball export (240 contacts, 05.08 11:07). Reconciled **four** registers — that export, the `clubdesk_export` mirror (04.08 14:36), `members`, and `basketplan_people` (27.07) — 186 in exact agreement. **3 real gaps**, all one root cause and *not* a sync bug: Hermann/Morderet/Vijayakumara have no `license_nr` **and** are spelled differently in Basketplan (`Herrmann` two r's, `Mordret` no 'e', `Vijayakumaran`/`Shan Thosh`), so `basketplan-scrape-people.mjs`'s join — licence-nr **OR** exact first+last+birthdate — misses on both arms; birthdates match exactly, so the IDs are confirmed. 282 sets the flags **from Basketplan** (not ClubDesk: its `Offiziellen Lizenz` is a single-value picklist that can only ever show the higher rung — which is also why **50** members legitimately hold `otr1_bb`+`otr2_bb` while ClubDesk shows only OTR2, all correct and left alone) and fills `license_nr`, which is what stops the recurrence. ⚠ **Deliberately did NOT run `import-clubdesk-csv.mjs`** — it opens `TRUNCATE clubdesk_export RESTART IDENTITY`, and this export is BB-only (240/1152) **and omits `[Gruppen]`**, so it would have dropped the 912 VB rows and blanked the column `referee_vb`/`deriveGruppen`/the sync-up drift all read; Saturday's full-club scrape refreshes the mirror normally. ⚠ **Two offered values rejected as junk after validating them** (the reason to check, not trust): 3 AHV numbers — Giulio Cataldi `7570000000000` (not a Swiss AHV at all, they begin 756) and Ella **and** Juli Sidi Friede **both** `7560000000000` (failed check digit, same number for two people) — and Felix Stauch's phone `79598279`, 8 digits, one short even with the leading 0 restored. 27 both-differ fields stay untouched (fill-only), incl. **5 birthdate mismatches**, 3 of them dd/mm transpositions. **Migration 281**: `trainer_licences` CHECK widened to `^(JS|C|B|A|T1|T2|T3)…` — Swiss Basketball grades coaches "Trainer 1/2/3", a *separate federation's* ladder, so the 3 ClubDesk cells resolved to NULL and were silently skipped (never corrupted). Collapsing T2→B was **rejected** (user): unverified equivalence, and the push would render a BB coach's grade in volleyball wording. ⚠ Parsing lives in **four** places and all four moved: SQL in the migration's trigger, SQL in `import-clubdesk-csv.mjs`, JS in `clubdesk-update.js`, TS in `src/utils/trainerLicences.ts`. ⚠ The subtle bit: `Trainer` is on the token skip-list precisely so `"Trainer B"` yields B, so `"Trainer <digit>"` must be **lifted to a `T<n>` token BEFORE tokenizing** or the digit is orphaned — verified round-trip in both implementations (`"Trainer 2+"` → T2, the `+` shorthand dropped; `"Trainer 1, B"` → `B,T1`; `"Trainer B"` still B; `"Basketball"` still nothing). Profile picker is now sport-aware (VB sees C/B/A, BB sees Trainer 1/2/3, J+S always) **unioned with what the member already holds**, so a stored cross-ladder code can never be hidden; every other consumer (admin explorer filter + field editor, ProfilePage) derives from the shared constant and picked it up free. ⚠ **Applied via the psql escape hatch, not `db:migrate`** — the runner is git-blind and would have swept in the operator's uncommitted 279/280 WIP; both registered in `kscw_migrations` with the runner's own sha so `--status` stays truthful. ⚠ ClubDesk **Gruppen cannot be pushed** (UPDATE rows never send it; a create-row carrying it lands with empty groups — proven), so the **34** licence holders missing from `BB Offiziellen` are a manual UI job, filtered by Wiedisync ID under "Alle Kontakte" (several are Passiv/Kein Mitglied); future ClubDesk mutations to go through Playwright per the user. **Latent defect found while answering "why does UPDATE not send Offiziellen Lizenz?"** and fixed in the same commit: `deriveOffiziellenLizenz` tested `otr1_bb` **before** `otr2_bb` (and `otn1` before `otn2`), so a member holding both rungs derived the **lower** one. **43 members hold both** on prod — correctly, since Basketplan records both `otr1_since` and `otr2_since` and wiedisync models the rungs as independent booleans, while ClubDesk's picklist is single-valued and must choose. It was latent only because all 43 are already linked and UPDATE rows never carry the column, so only a CREATE could have shipped the downgrade — but it is precisely what would have rewritten 43 correct OTR2 cells the day the field joined the UPDATE set. Now highest-rung-first, with the reasoning pinned in a comment and 4 tests. The answer to the question itself: the omission is a deliberate boundary (`clubdesk-update.js:307`) — ClubDesk stays authoritative on existing contacts for Gruppen/Status/Offiziellen Lizenz/Sektion/Schiedsrichter/Telefon Mobil; the 2026-07-08 spike proved an empty mapped cell is a no-op on import, but keeping the columns out of the header set is kept as the *structural* guarantee ("one probe on one field type is no licence to send status cells at existing contacts"). Adding it fill-only like Beitragskategorie was offered and deferred. Verified on the dev clone before prod: `'t2, js'` → `JS,T2`, `'B,T2,JS'` → `JS,B,T2`, `'T4'` → CHECK violation. Gates ✓ (build, lint 0 errors, 996 unit, 122 scripts, `node --check`). ⚠ Endpoint + script code → needs `ext:deploy` **and** `clubdesk:deploy` (the importer lives in the separate `/opt/clubdesk-sync` bind-mount, not covered by `ext:deploy`).

- **2026-08-05** **Hallenfinder Excel export (dev+prod, v1.67.0, no migration)** — asked as "here let me export an excel of this". The page answers *which halls are free* and then strands the answer on screen: on a phone it renders **4 of 19** fields, and address, PLZ, Stadtkreis, Stadtquartier, Schulkreis and the caretaker contact all sit behind a `hidden sm:`/`md:` breakpoint — precisely the fields needed to act on a hall. New `src/modules/hallenfinder/hallenfinderExport.ts` (lazy `import('exceljs')`, same shape as `assignmentExport`, reusing its `downloadBytes`). **Three sheets.** *Halls*: every field, with dimensions shipped **both ways** — the city's own `sizeLabel` verbatim (Swiss decimal comma) *and* the parsed metres as real numbers, which is the only way Excel can filter "length ≥ 36"; ⚠ an unmeasured hall writes **blank, not 0** (a 0 sorts as "smallest hall" and asserts a measurement the city has not published); links written as real hyperlinks; rows free every non-holiday week tinted green; autofilter + frozen header. *Courts*: one row per partition, since a triple hall is one row on the page but three bookable spaces — sheet **omitted entirely** when nothing is partitioned. *Search*: the filters, season and data date, because availability is a claim about a moment and a filterless, dateless list of "free halls" is unusable a fortnight on. English-only per the export convention (`i18n.getFixedT('en', 'hallenfinder')`), dates Swiss `dd.mm.yyyy` **inside** the file but ISO in the **filename** so a folder sorts chronologically. ⚠ `gsw` has no `hallenfinder` namespace at all (falls back to English) — button labels added to en/de/fr/it only, matching the existing shape rather than introducing a lone gsw key. Also memoised `filters`, which was a fresh object every render (it now feeds the export callback's deps and the react-query key). **Verified against live prod data before claiming value**: of 30 halls in the widest search, address/PLZ/contact/Kreis/Quartier/Schulkreis are **30/30 populated**, `sizeLabel`+length+width 30/30, height 24/30 — ⚠ and **partitions are 0/30 across every hall today**, so the Courts sheet is currently inert (it appears as the monthly detail pass fills migration 269's columns). ⚠ The 6 height-less halls are exactly those whose city string ends `x 0,00 m` — the city writes 0,00 for "unknown", the parser correctly yields NULL, so the Size column shows `0,00` while the Height column is blank; left as-is because the page already renders the label verbatim and editing the city's own string would be worse. **7 new unit tests build a real workbook and read it back with exceljs** (numbers stay numbers, nulls stay blank not 0, links are hyperlinks, Courts appears only when earned, empty result set yields a file not a throw). Gates ✓ (build, 1015 unit, eslint clean on the touched files — ⚠ the repo-wide lint shows 1 error in `useBbClubPortal.ts`, an **untracked WIP file**, not from this work). ⚠ Frontend-only: no `ext:deploy`, no schema, no perms. Prod by cherry-pick (`45ebe1bc`), landing just after the user's v1.66.0 release, hence the v1.67.0 bump covering this **and** the still-unreleased VIS on-demand check.

- **2026-08-05** **VIS check goes weekly, and a first/last name split was hiding a player who was in the roster all along (dev+prod, no migration)** — two follow-ups to the on-demand check. (1) **Cadence**: the guard in `/opt/vis-sync/vis-sync.sh` moved from `date +%d = 01` to **`date +%u = 1`** (Mondays; next run 10.08, verified by walking the next 8 days). The monthly cadence rested on a stated "30 federation rosters is a heavy read" — measured the same day at **~3.7s** for 24 federations / 464 members, so the rationale was simply false; script + crontab comments now carry the number so it cannot come back. Backups `vis-sync.sh.bak-20260805-weekly` + `/root/crontab.bak-20260805-vis-weekly`. ⚠ `vis-sync.sh` exists **only on the VPS**, not in the repo. (2) **Matching**: reported as "Paula Fiorella Farina is 243491 but the system can’t find it". Not a missing VIS record — a **split mismatch**: wiedisync holds `Paula Fiorella` / `Farina`, VIS #243491 (ARG, fed 9) holds `Paula` / `Fiorella Farina`. Same three tokens, opposite side of the first/last boundary, so the exact `lastname|firstname` key missed **and** the prefix fallback could not fire, because that one requires the **surname to be exact**. Probed VIS directly before touching code: 243491 *is* in the ARG roster, is not in ITA or SUI, and ARG holds exactly 2 names containing "farina". New **step 2** in the cascade — the whole name as a sorted **token bag**, accepted only when exactly one VIS player carries that bag (⚠ hence a `Set` per key, not a number: the bag deliberately collapses variants, so two different people can share one and a tie must be refused, not guessed). ⚠ Splits **before** stripping punctuation — `norm()` would fuse "Fiorella Farina" into one token and defeat the point. Applied to **both mirrors** in the same commit as their headers demand; the `.mjs` was shipped to the VPS by **single-file scp**, deliberately NOT `scripts:deploy` (git-blind, would have rsynced unrelated WIP migrations sitting in `directus/scripts/`). Verified the two paths agree: endpoint 12/452 on dev and prod, host script `--dry-run` on prod **also 12/452** with `✓ Farina, Paula Fiorella (AR) — VIS #243491`; prod row 729 now `in_vis=t, vis_player_no=243491`. **Then measured whether a manual link is needed at all**: of the 452 still not found, **0** are a token-subset of exactly one VIS player (so the "VIS holds extra given names" gap affects nobody today), and the 40 surname-only near-hits are **different people** (`Linda Imhof`→`Stefan Imhof`, `Livia Wyss`→`Nadia Wyss`) — i.e. precisely the false positives a looser rule would invent. Name matching is now as good as it can safely get; a manual `vis_player_no` override remains unbuilt and would need a migration, because the sweep overwrites that column on every run (now weekly, so a hand-edit in Directus survives at most 7 days). ⚠ Confirmed with the user: VIS is volleyball-only — button hidden on the basketball tab, `bb_admin` excluded from the endpoint gate. Prod by cherry-pick (`38349be4`) + `ext:deploy:prod`; no schema, no perms.

- **2026-08-05** **`/admin/transfers` Refresh only ever reloaded a month-old VIS answer — real on-demand VIS check (dev+prod 2026-08-05, no migration)** — asked as "what does Refresh do? it defo does not run the VIS check because it is stale to 01.08". Correct on both counts. That button is `refetch()` on the page's `members` query (`TransfersPage.tsx:1406`) and nothing else — it re-reads stored columns, and does not even refetch the other two queries on the page (`vis_federations`, the team junction, which keep their own `staleTime`). `members.in_vis` / `vis_player_no` / `in_vis_checked_at` (migration 240) had **exactly one writer**: `vis-player-check.mjs`, gated inside `/opt/vis-sync/vis-sync.sh` by `if [ "$(date +%d)" = "01" ]` — so for 30 days of every 31 the page showed a frozen answer that no amount of pressing Refresh could change, which is precisely how it read as broken. New `POST|GET /kscw/admin/vis-player-check` (`kscw-endpoints/src/vis-player-check.js`) + a "Check VIS now" button beside Refresh. ⚠ **The endpoint is a deliberate MIRROR of the .mjs, not a refactor of it**: the script reaches Postgres through `sudo docker exec … psql`, which does not exist inside the Directus container, and the extension must not import across the `scripts/` bind-mount (a separate deploy unit — CLAUDE.md §4, and the exact coupling that produced the 2026-06-19 `public/team` 500s). The ISO→FIVB map, the cohort SELECT, the name-matching cascade and the write rule are duplicated; both files now carry a keep-in-step header. Shape is **202 + poll** (in-flight guard, one run process-wide; a second caller gets 409) — ⚠ *not* because it is slow: measured on the dev clone at **3.8 s** for 24 federations / 464 members (SUI 1093 players, ITA 4651, USA 5786), which quietly **falsifies the monthly cron's own stated rationale** ("30 federation rosters is a heavy read" — it is not; noted in INFRA.md for whenever the cadence is revisited). The async shape is for the tail: one HTTP round trip per federation against a third party we do not control, behind a CF tunnel that cuts a held request at ~100 s. Raw-knex writes bypass Directus' activity trail, so the trigger is `writeUserLog`-ed **at start** (the run outlives the request; "who pressed it" is worth keeping even if it then fails) and every exit path lands a `sync_runs` heartbeat (`source: 'vis_player_check'`). Both 409s carry a `code` (`vis_check_running` / `vis_credentials_missing`) added to `EXPECTED_ERROR_CODES` — a second admin pressing the button is an expected state, not a Sentry bug. Gate is `superGate` incl. **`vb_admin`** but deliberately **not `bb_admin`**: VIS is FIVB's index, so the button is hidden on the basketball tab entirely. Page also now **dates the numbers** it shows (`trVisLastChecked`, newest `in_vis_checked_at` across all members, not the filtered cohort) and both buttons carry a `title` saying which one asks the database and which one asks FIVB — the confusion that prompted this was avoidable in the UI, not just in the backend. ⚠ **Needs `VIS_USER`/`VIS_PASS` in the container env → an env change → a container RECREATE, not `docker restart`** (`ext:deploy` only restarts; same trap as KSCW_WEBSITE_URL on 2026-07-29). Appended to both `.env` files from `/opt/vis-sync/vis.env` server-side (`.bak-20260805-vis` kept); dev recreated + verified (`printenv`, `configured:true`). Verified end-to-end on dev against live VIS, not asserted: 202 → immediate 409 on a second POST → `{checked:464, inVis:11, notFound:453, unmapped:[], federations:24}` → `in_vis_checked_at` max = 2026-08-05 10:12 in the DB, `sync_runs` row `ok / 464 rows / 3817 ms`, 203 members correctly left NULL (out of cohort). Gates ✓ (build, lint 0 errors, `node --check`). ⚠ Endpoint + frontend code, no schema → no ordering hazard. Shipped to prod by **cherry-pick** (`cd81366c`/`2e87ca43`/`d8f9eb6c`), not a dev→prod merge — dev was carrying unrelated basketball work that was not ready to promote. Prod sequence was `ext:deploy:prod` → container **recreate** (env only lands on create) → one run: `{checked:464, inVis:11, notFound:453, unmapped:[], federations:24}` in 3631 ms, `in_vis_checked_at` max 05.08.2026 10:22 (was 01.08), `user_logs` actor row + `sync_runs` heartbeat both present.

- **2026-08-05** **Cup fixtures were leaking into Terminplanung — a Cup R1 tie became an invite + a phantom 2nd game (dev+prod, no migration)** — reported as "D1 has Appenzeller Bären to be set but that's a cup game, no need to set up the game and in particular not 2 games". Both halves were real. The tie is SVRZ #417931 *Mobiliar Volley Cup, Runde 1, Spiel 28* (Appenzeller Bären 1 – KSC Wiedikon D1, `open`, placeholder 01.09.2026 00:00). ⚠ **`svrz_games.league_short` cannot detect a cup** — it holds the *team's* league category (`2L`), so a cup tie is byte-identical to a league game there; the competition only appears in `league_name` (`#7244 | Mobiliar Volley Cup | ♀`), which no Terminplanung reader filtered. Consequences on prod: `ensure-from-svrz` auto-minted invite **#202 "Appenzeller Bären 1"** for D1 (source `svrz`, `email_sent_at` NULL — nothing ever reached them, 0 bookings), and the card showed **2 games** for a single tie: the away leg was the real fixture, the home leg was `buildFixtureLegs`' empty-side fallback firing per-side instead of per-pairing. A first pass using `leagueCategory.name NOT LIKE '%Liga%'` **under-counted** — the real predicate found **7** cup ties for 2026, 5 of them still `open`, incl. two Züri Cup rows with an **empty `home_team_name`** (opponent not drawn yet → an invite with a blank name was queued to appear). Fixes: `excludeCupFixtures()` (`league_name !~* '(cup|pokal|coupe|coppa)'`, same net as sv-sync's `CUP_RE`, NULL spelled out because `!~*` is NULL-propagating) gates all four fixture-*discovery* reads — `opponentSvrzFixtures`, `resolveSyncedOpponents`, `import-from-svrz`, derbies; by-id lookups deliberately untouched so historical bookings still resolve. `buildFixtureLegs`' empty-leg fallback is now keyed on the whole pairing's fixtures (`oppGames`, **not** `oppBookings` — a manual opponent with only an away booking still needs its home leg, that leg is what `ManualBookingForm` offers as the home target); an empty side renders `noGameThisSide` (5 locales). Cup games still reach the member calendar via sv-sync (`/indoor/games?includeCup=true`) — this is scheduling-only. Verified on real data, not just the build: the predicate keeps 2020's 109 open league fixtures and drops only the 5 cup rows; prod `svrz-clubs` returns `clubs: []` for D1/D4/H1/H3. Invite #202 deleted on prod + the dev clone with a `NOT EXISTS(bookings)` guard — ⚠ the first attempt returned `DELETE 0` because the stored name carries a **trailing space** (`'Appenzeller Bären 1 '`, 20 chars, straight from SVRZ). D1 is back to 9 opponents, all league, all `booked`. Gates ✓ (build, lint 0 errors, 850 unit, 114 scripts, `node --check`). ⚠ Endpoint + frontend code → needed `ext:deploy` on both envs; no schema, no ordering hazard.

- **2026-08-05** **`/set-password` 400'd and the app blamed the reset link — password rules unmirrored, emailed token unread (dev+prod, v1.65.0, no migration)** — asked as "did Anja Jimenez try a password change and it didn't work?". She did, **five times** on 04.08 between 16:59 and 17:06, and every `POST /kscw/set-password` returned **400**. ⚠ **The error log was empty for all of it** — those 400s are early `return res.status(400)` guards that never reach `logEndpointError`, and `/set-password` sits in `EXPECTED_VALIDATION_ENDPOINTS` (`api.ts:677`) so the frontend suppresses them too. Root cause: `validatePassword` (`index.js:116`) requires ≥8 chars **AND** a letter **AND** a digit-or-special, while `SetPasswordPage` checked only `length < 8` — so a letters-only password passed the form and died at the server. **Proven by elimination against prod, not inferred**: her four `email_verifications` rows (305/306/308/310) are still present with `verified = t` and were unexpired at each attempt (10-min TTL vs. 36–85 s gaps), and the success path deletes every row for the address (`index.js:1468`) — so the request never got past the password check, which runs *first*. Confirmed live: a letters-only password returns exactly that rule's text, 400. The damage was the **message**: the catch-all mapped any code-less 400 to `resetError` = *"Der Link ist ungültig oder abgelaufen. Bitte fordere einen neuen Link an."* — so she requested two fresh reset emails (16:57, 17:00) chasing a link that was never involved. Fixes: (1) `validatePassword` now returns `{ error, code }` (`password_too_short` / `password_weak` / `password_too_common`) and the 3 call sites send it verbatim; (2) new `src/lib/passwordRules.ts` mirrors the cheap rules client-side (translated, pre-flight) with the rule text printed under the field, + 8 unit tests pinning the mirror; (3) `SetPasswordPage`, `SetPasswordForm` and both `SignUpPage` handlers map the codes. ⚠ **Adding the code broke a live branch** — `SignUpPage`'s invite-redeem caught password rules via `status === 400 && body.error && !apiErr.code` ("show verbatim"); with a code present that now falls through to the generic `inviteRedeemFailed`, so the code mapping had to be added *ahead* of it in the same commit. **Second, independent bug found on the way**: `password-reset.js:218` mails `${FRONTEND_URL}/set-password?token=…`, but nothing in `src/` ever read `?token` — the backend's mode 2 was **unreachable from the app** and the emailed link silently degraded to the OTP form. Corroborated by data: `password_reset_tokens` has held **2 rows ever**, hers (id 3) still unconsumed. `SetPasswordPage` now opens straight on the password step when a token is present (and skips the auto-OTP — a token is its own proof of address) and posts `{ password, token }`. **Third, a different member, same page**: Philip Urech (member 430) failed at 16:50 with `no_account` — he typed `urechphilip@gmail.com` while his record carries `philip.urech@bluewin.ch`; `password-request` falls back to `members.vm_email`/`email` but neither holds that gmail, so nothing could link them. He was told "no account found → **Sign up**", which for an existing member would have minted a duplicate; the hint now leads with "try the address the club has on file" and demotes signup. ⚠ Frontend + endpoint code → needs `ext:deploy` on both envs; **no schema, so no ordering hazard**. Gates ✓ (build, lint 0 errors, 858 unit incl. 8 new, 114 scripts, `node --check`).
- **2026-08-04** **ClubDesk sync-down and sync-up are now mutually exclusive (dev+prod, no migration, no bump)** — asked as "nothing blocks me from syncing down then up immediately, right?" and the answer was **correct**: `POST /clubdesk-member-sync` guarded only `down_state`, `POST /clubdesk-member-sync/up` only `up_state` — different columns of the same singleton row — and the two dispatcher crons hold **separate** claim locks (`.member-dispatch-$DB.lock` / `.up-dispatch-$DB.lock`), so both claimed in the same minute. The UI could not have stopped it either: `ClubdeskSyncPage` renders the button and the modal as independent components with private state. ⚠ They never *collided* on ClubDesk itself — both scrapes serialise on the shared blocking `$DIR/.sync.lock` (one session per account) — so this was never a corruption bug; the two real hazards were **ordering**. (a) The up payload (stale-link guard, blank-risk drift, would-duplicate name match, echo-back) is computed against `clubdesk_export` = the **last completed** sync-down, at the moment `/up` is called, and is then **frozen** — so firing both together builds the push against the *pre-down* snapshot and the refresh the operator just asked for cannot help it. That is exactly the "contact deleted since the last sync-down → unknown `[Id]` hard-aborts the whole import" abort the up dispatcher's own error text warns about. (b) The up dispatcher takes `.sync.lock` **per scrape** (preview-update, preview-create, commit-create, commit-update), so a down run can slot in **between the dry-run preview and the commit**. Fix is symmetric: whichever direction is queued/running now 409s the other (`code: 'up_in_progress'` / `'down_in_progress'`), plus `up_state` on the status GET. ⚠ `GET /up-preview` short-circuits to an empty `{blocked_by_down}` **before** any `clubdesk_export` read — not tidiness: the down import reloads the snapshot as `BEGIN; TRUNCATE clubdesk_export; \copy …; COMMIT`, so every read below it would block on that `ACCESS EXCLUSIVE` lock and hang the modal on a spinner for the whole run. (That same MVCC/`TRUNCATE`-in-transaction shape is also why a concurrent preview could never read a *half-loaded* table — the tearing risk was never real, the wait was.) UI: the down button polls the status row (20 s) and greys out with a hint; the modal gets a `blocked` phase with retry, reachable **both** from the preview and from the push (a down can start after the modal is already open). ⚠ All three catch blocks check **`code` before `state`** — the block carries the *other* direction's `queued`/`running`, so a bare state check blames the wrong sync. Liveness moved to a ref so closing mid-retry discards the response. 4 keys × en/de/fr/it; **gsw deliberately skipped** (it carries none of the sibling ClubDesk sync keys — adding only the new ones renders a half-Swiss-German panel; it falls back exactly as `clubdeskSyncInProgress` already does). ⚠ Lint caught a real one: extracting `loadPreview` put a synchronous `setPhase` in the effect body — the phase transition belongs to the caller. Endpoint code → needs `ext:deploy` on both envs; no schema, so no ordering hazard. Gates ✓ (build, lint 0 errors, 850 unit, 114 scripts, `node --check`).
- **2026-08-04** **Mailbox picker: OR within a section, live chip counts, membership sub-audiences (dev only, no migration)** — the drill builder ANDed every chip in a clause, which is only right ACROSS the rows. Every section except Roles is a **dimension** (a person holds exactly one value), so intersecting two chips from the same row asked a question that can never be answered: `D1`+`D2` meant "on both rosters" where the operator had just picked 39 people, and `Aktivmitglied`+`Passivmitglied` is empty **by definition** — which would have shipped the new membership sub-chips broken on day one. Now **OR within a section, AND across sections**; the cross-section narrowing the builder exists for is untouched (Sections ▸ Volleyball + Roles ▸ Coaches still = volleyball coaches). ⚠ **What it gives up**: Roles is NOT a dimension (coach/scorer/referee are independent booleans), so "scorers who are also referees" is no longer expressible in one clause — an analytics question, not a mailing one, and paying for it with an always-empty result in the other five rows was the worse trade. **No wire-format change** — `clauses` stays `string[][]`, `parseClauses` untouched; only the combination inside a clause moved into the pure, tested `combineClauseSets` (+10 cases; these functions decide who a mass mail reaches and a mistake is invisible in the result — a send to the wrong 300 still reports success). ⚠ The former-members guard moved off `srcs.length > 1` onto **section count**, because same-section keys now union and a union of register contacts with members would be well-defined — only the cross-section case is impossible (today `former` is alone in its section, so it rejects exactly what the length check did). **New `POST /admin/mailbox/group-counts`**: for every chip, how big the audience would **become** if added to the draft — *not* an intersection count, since a chip from a section already in the draft unions and its number goes **up**. ⚠⚠ Backed by a 60s member-set index built from the **canonical `resolveMemberAudience`**, one call per key (~50), NOT by a hand-rolled ~5-query `GROUP BY` index: the fast version would be a **second definition of who is in an audience**, and the number painted on the chip would drift from the dry-run that actually gates Send — `audience.js` was extracted precisely so announcements and the mailbox could not disagree. **New chips**: Active/Passive/Honorary/Gap year (one `register_status` each, same join + `kscw_membership_active` gate as `all_members`) + Guest players (`member_teams.guest_level > 0`, ⚠ deliberately **not** register-gated — 3 guests are ClubDesk `Kein Beitrag` ex-members who still train, and they are exactly who the chip is for; ⚠ not the same "guest" as `game_guests`, see [[game-guests-rsvp-gate]]). Composer now opens scoped to the **current season**, seeded after the catalogue loads (which season is current is only known then) and only when >1 season exists. ⚠ A season cannot scope a section/qualification/register audience and the server **rejects** that clause, so picking one **drops the season and says so** (toast) rather than building a filter that cannot resolve; `SEASON_SCOPED_PREFIXES` is now mirrored in `MailboxPanel.tsx` — keep in step. **Verified live on dev against the real DB, not asserted**: baseline is internally consistent (550+89+11+21 = **671** = `all`); AND across sections narrows (coaches 30→**15** under Volleyball, scorers 141→129); OR within a section unions (Active+Passive = **639**, Active+Honorary = 561); and the headline case D1(18)+D2(21) reports **39** on the chip *and* resolves to **39 recipients in the `/bulk` dry run* — chip arithmetic and send arithmetic agree, which is the invariant that matters. **Follow-ups the same day, also dev-only**: (1) **Scorers/referees/officials now resolve from the ClubDesk GROUP, not the `qual:*` licence columns** — "who does this for us" and "who holds the licence" are different questions and the picker answered the second. Measured on the dev clone: officials licence **148** vs `BB Offiziellen` **117**, i.e. that chip was mailing **31 people who do not officiate**; scorers 141 vs 140, BB referees 8 vs 7, and VB referees **already identical at 13** because ClubDesk has fed `referee_vb` from the group rather than `offiziellen_lizenz` all along (see [[scorer-vb-vm-clubdesk-conflict]]). The group is a strict **subset** of the licence in every case, so this only ever narrows. New `audience_type: 'clubdesk_group'`; chip keys `qual:*` → `cdgroup:*` (4 labels re-keyed ×5 locales, dead `mailboxGroup_qual_*` removed). ⚠ **Groups live ONLY on `clubdesk_export.gruppen_bracketed`** — `clubdesk_people.gruppen` is empty for all 1151 rows — so this joins a *different* table than the register-status audiences. ⚠ Matched as an **exact element after splitting on ','**, never a LIKE, or `BB Offiziellen` would silently absorb a future `BB Offiziellen 2`; group names carry ClubDesk's own spelling (asterisks and all) so a rename there empties the chip — the count is the check. ⚠⚠ **The trade**: ClubDesk Gruppen are maintained BY HAND (CSV import cannot write them, [[clubdesk-group-assignment]]), so a new scorer nobody adds to the group will not be mailed — curated-but-can-lag replacing automatic-but-over-broad, deliberately. (2) **Season row moved** from the top of the picker to directly above players/roles/teams — the only rows it can scope — with an inline hint saying so; leading with a chip that most rows below it *reject* read as though it scoped everything. Verified live: all four `cdgroup:*` counts match the raw SQL exactly (140/13/7/117) and no `qual:*` key remains in the catalogue. Gates ✓ (build, lint 0 errors, 850 unit, 114 scripts). **Prod pending approval.**
- **2026-08-04** **DMARC/SPF hygiene on the sending domains (DNS only, Cloudflare zone `kscw.ch`)** — found while answering "could we move mail to AWS completely" (answer: no — SES has no IMAP/mailboxes, AWS's only mailbox product is per-seat WorkMail, and `wiedisync.kscw.ch` is *already* Migadu-hosted so named addresses need no migration at all). Two defects on `noreply.kscw.ch`, the Directus transactional identity (password resets, invitations, OTP, scorer reminders, scheduling invites, expense mail): SPF read `include:amazonses.com~all` — **missing space**, so the include target parsed as the literal domain `amazonses.com~all` → **PermError**, with no `all` mechanism left at all (latent, not live: SES uses the custom MAIL FROM `bounce.noreply.kscw.ch`, whose record was correct, and SPF evaluates the envelope domain); and `_dmarc.noreply.kscw.ch` carried a stray **`│` (U+2502, stored escaped as `\226\148\130`)** copy-pasted from a terminal table — a bare token with no `=`, which strict parsers reject as a malformed record. Both fixed. ⚠ **The finding that mattered more**: `spielplanung.kscw.ch` is `p=quarantine` with **no `rua=`** — enforcement with zero telemetry, i.e. exactly the failure the code comment at `scheduling-mailbox.js:112-116` already warns about ("replies fail SPF and are silently quarantined… **Nothing surfaces in a log**"). `rua=mailto:admin@wiedisync.kscw.ch` added there and on `wiedisync.kscw.ch` (which now carries 671-recipient group sends at `p=none`). ⚠ Cross-domain reporting needs **RFC 7489 §7.1 external-destination authorisation** or Google/Microsoft/Yahoo simply send nothing — `{spielplanung,noreply}.kscw.ch._report._dmarc.wiedisync.kscw.ch` TXT `v=DMARC1` added; `_dmarc.wiedisync.kscw.ch` needs none (own domain). All 6 verified on **both** authoritative NS. Reports go to a dedicated **`dmarc@wiedisync.kscw.ch`** (created in the Migadu UI same day — there is no Migadu *admin* API in the vault, only per-mailbox IMAP passwords, so it could not be scripted). ⚠ It is on **wiedisync.kscw.ch specifically** so the `_report._dmarc.wiedisync.kscw.ch` authorisations already cover it — a `dmarc@mail.kscw.ch` would have needed three further records first, and without them Google/Microsoft/Yahoo send **nothing, with no error**. Briefly pointed at `admin@wiedisync.kscw.ch`, which would have dripped daily XML into the IMAP-synced club mailbox several admins read. ⚠ Leave `wiedisync.kscw.ch` at `p=none` until a few weeks of reports read clean; raising it first is how a club quarantines its own mail. Canary-first procedure used deliberately: the CF API returns TXT content **with** surrounding quotes, so a naive write could have produced `""v=spf1 …""` on a live sending domain.
- **2026-08-04** **Mailbox composer polish — footer unpinned, "All" dropped from chip labels, team chips bucketed by sport × gender (dev+prod, no bump)** — three reading complaints about the group-send composer, all cosmetic, none touching audience resolution. (1) The Cancel/Send bar was `sticky bottom-0` inside `CardContent`: pinned to the viewport it hung *over* the card scrolling behind it and read as page furniture rather than as this card's own footer. Back into normal flow (`-mx-6 -mb-6 mt-6`, same bordered styling) — the sticky was added defensively when the composer still lived in a `sm:max-w-xl` dialog whose Send fell below an unscrollable fold; as its **own full-height screen** (`composeFullScreen`, since the 2026-08-03 build) scrolling to the end reaches Send anyway, so the guard outlived its bug. (2) Eight chip labels lost their "All" prefix in **all 5 locales** (`All coaches` → `Coaches`, `Alle Schreiber*innen` → `Schreiber*innen`, `Tous les arbitres (…)` → `Arbitres (…)`, …): under a row already headed "Roles & functions" the word carried no information, and eight chips opening on the same token made the row harder to scan, not safer. ⚠ Labels only — **no audience was removed**, `mailboxGroup_all` still resolves the same `all_members` register audience, now reading "Members". (3) The Teams row was one flat name-sorted list of **29** chips against at most 8 anywhere else; now split into sport × gender buckets with sub-headings (volleyball first, women → men → mixed, alphabetical inside). That needed `gender` carried through `teamAudienceCounts()` (`audience.js`) and `GET /admin/mailbox/groups` → hence an `ext:deploy` on both envs; it is **display-only** and reaches no resolver. Unknown sport/gender sorts last under "Other teams" rather than vanishing, and `bucketLabel` falls back to the raw value, so a sport added in Directus shows as itself instead of a missing i18n key. New keys `mailboxTeamsSport_volleyball|_basketball` + `mailboxTeamsOther` (5 locales); gender labels reuse the existing `gender_m|_f|_mixed`. ⚠ The `teamBuckets` `useMemo` sits **above** the `if (!groups)` early return — moving it below is a conditional hook. Gates ✓ (build, lint 0 errors, 850 unit, 104 scripts). No version bump: label + layout polish, per the versioning discipline.
- **2026-08-04** **Game panel's one roster button branched by role and hid the RSVP list from staff (frontend only, v1.63.3, dev+prod)** — `GameDetailModal` had a **single** button, labelled `participationRoster` ("View roster"), whose *destination* depended on who you were: assigned scorer → scorer `RosterModal`, `isTeamStaff` → `PreGameRosterModal` (match sheet), everyone else → `ParticipationRosterModal`. ⚠ `isTeamStaff` starts with `adminSeesContact = hasAdminAccessToTeam(kscwTeamId)`, which is **true for every team** for a superadmin — so the group most likely to ask "who has answered?" (the team's own coach/TR, plus all admins) could not reach that view from a game **at all**, while reading a button that names it. Fix: those roles now get **two** buttons — `pregameTitle` ("Match sheet", the existing destination, now honestly labelled) + `participationRoster` (the RSVP list, same modal the games-list Users icon opens); non-staff keep the single button, unchanged. `ParticipationRosterModal` moved out of the ternary's `else` and is mounted unconditionally — safe because **all five** of its `useCollection` calls are gated on `open`, so a closed instance issues no queries. No new i18n keys (both labels already exist in all 5 locales). Gates ✓ (build, lint 0 errors, 850 unit).
- **2026-08-04** **Called-up players could not RSVP — and game-card RSVP had been dead app-wide since 10.06 (frontend only, v1.63.2, dev+prod)** — reported as "I called up H3 for an H1 cup game but I can't RSVP". Migration 271's call-up worked perfectly; it was **never wired into the RSVP gate**. `canParticipateIn` (`AuthProvider.tsx:412`) is `memberTeamIds.includes(teamId) || coachTeamIds.includes(teamId)` — **team**-scoped, and a guest by definition has no `member_teams` row on the fixture's team, which is the entire point of borrowing them. So `useUserVisibleGameIds` correctly ORs guest games into the *visibility* filters (the fixture reaches their calendar/home/list) while the Yes/Maybe/No block never renders. `git show a59d3bdf` touched neither `GameCard` nor the modal's gate. **Prod data confirmed it end-to-end before any code changed**: game 572 (`vb_418057`, 17.09.2026, Volley S9 vs KSC Wiedikon H1, `kscw_team`=H1) has 1 `game_guest_teams` row (H3 = team 92, by Luca Canepa) fanned out to **25 `game_guests`, every one `on_game_team_roster = f`**. ⚠ **The backend was already willing** — `participations` create is self-scoped (`OWN_MEMBER`, `setup-permissions.mjs:1144`), and *both* the guest-RSVP filter hook (`kscw-hooks/src/index.js:4873-4920`) and `trg_participations_guest_block` key off **`member_teams.guest_level`**, for which a migration-271 guest has no row at all → `level === 0` → early `return payload`. The UI was withholding buttons for a write the server would have accepted, so this needed no migration, no `ext:deploy`, no perms run. ⚠ Note the **two unrelated senses of "guest"** that collide here: `member_teams.guest_level > 0` = "trains with us, may not play league games" (blocked from game RSVP on purpose), vs. `game_guests` = "borrowed for this one fixture" (invited precisely to answer it) — the modal's `isGuestIn(kscwTeamId)` branch is the former and must stay. **Adjacent bug found while fixing it, bigger than the report**: `GameCard` passed `game.kscw_team` **raw** to `canParticipateIn` / `isStaffOnly` / `isGuestIn` (only `teamIdForPerms` used `relId`), but *every* surface fetches `fields: ['*','kscw_team.*',…]` — so all three compared an **object** against `string[]` and silently answered `false` from **e640bea9 (10.06)** onward: no RSVP buttons on any game card (you had to open the panel), coach replies never filed under staff, `guest_level` players never held back. `git log -S` dates the raw call to ab2f622c (10.03), i.e. it was correct until the expansion landed 3 months later — a **latent type-lie**: `Game['kscw_team']` is declared `string`, so `tsc` cannot see it. Normalised via `relId` once at the top. `GameCard` is mounted only by `GamesPage` + `EmbedGamesPage`, which bounds the blast radius. **Also**: `useUserVisibleGameIds` moved off its private `useState`+`fetchAllItems` effect onto `useCollection` (React Query) — the page filter, all N cards and the panel now share **one** query key and therefore one round-trip instead of one request per card. **Third fix, the late-loading RSVP rectangles**: `GameDetailModal` rendered `<ParticipationSummary>` with no `participations` prop → its own fetch per panel, *and* its loading placeholder was a one-character `…`, so the three bricks arrived a round-trip late **and reflowed everything under them**. `GamesPage`/`HomePage` now hand over the batch they already hold (`participationsByGame` / `getParticipations`), and `bars` mode renders a same-footprint pulsing skeleton for surfaces with no batch (calendar). ⚠ Passing `participations` sets `skipFetch`, which also disables that component's internal `useRealtime('participations')` refetch — safe **only because** both handing surfaces already refetch on that channel themselves (`refetchCombined` / `useBulkParticipations`); hand prefetched rows to it from a surface that does not, and the counters go stale after an RSVP. Gates ✓ (build, lint 0 errors, 850 unit).
- **2026-08-04** **Two scrollbars on every page — Radix's hidden form inputs escaping `main`'s clip (frontend only, dev+prod, no bump)** — reported as "why can I scroll so low in edit profile": a dead void below Save, on mobile *and* desktop, plus a second scrollbar. Root cause is **not** in the profile form. Radix primitives (`Switch`, `Select`, `RadioGroup`, `LanguageSelect`) each render a visually-hidden native control with **`position: absolute`** for form participation. `main` was `position: static`, so those boxes had **no positioned ancestor** → their containing block was the **initial containing block**, which means ancestor `overflow` never clips them and they stretch `<html>`'s scrollable height to wherever the lowest one sits. Fix is one word: `relative` on `main` in `Layout.tsx`, making it their containing block. ⚠ **The measurement that actually localised it**: `main.scrollHeight` alone looks *correct* (1894 = 16 pt + 1782 content + 96 `pb-24`) — the overflow is on `documentElement`, not `main`, so probing the scroll container proves nothing. The decisive bisect: forcing `main{overflow:hidden}` left `docScrollHeight` at 1313 (so it is NOT main's own overflow propagating), while hiding main's child dropped it to 852 — i.e. content that escapes the clip. Deepest offender was the Privacy card's last `Switch` hidden `<input type=checkbox>` at y=1313 → 461px of void on a 393×852 phone, 329px at 1600×1000 (the gap scales with how far down the page the last switch/select falls, so every page differed). Verified `docOverflow: 0` on /profile/edit, /profile, /home, /calendar at both viewports with `main` still scrolling normally. ⚠ Both `<main>` branches need it — the `/admin/explore` full-bleed branch is `overflow-hidden` and clips identically. Gates: lint 0 errors, 850 unit ✓; `npm run build` was verified in a **detached worktree at HEAD + this diff only**, because the shared working tree carried unrelated in-progress `MailboxPanel.tsx` edits that fail `tsc` (see [[lenovoserver-parallel-edits]]).
- **2026-08-04** **Identity-document upload was dead for 6 days — a type-less `<Button>` inside a `<form>` (frontend only, v1.63.1, dev+prod)** — two members reported "upload from mobile does nothing"; the error logs were clean, and that was the *finding*, not a dead end. **`512e17f7` (2026-07-28 18:19) moved `IdentityDocumentSection` from a sibling of `<ProfileEditForm>` into the form itself via the new `beforeActions` slot.** shadcn's `Button` sets no `type`, so the HTML default **`submit`** applies — outside a form that is inert, inside one every button in the section became a submit. Tapping "Upload document" therefore opened the OS file picker **and** submitted the profile form, whose `onSaved` navigates to `/profile`; the section unmounted while the picker was still open and the picked photo landed on a dead `onChange`. ⚠ **The POST never left the browser**, so there was no server-side trace of any kind — the only log lines were the benign `no_document` GET 404s the entry below had just declassified. Confirmed by data, not inference: prod holds **exactly one** `identity_documents` row (member 442, 2026-07-28 **16:51**) — 88 minutes *before* the regression commit — and zero since. Fix: `type="button"` on all 7 buttons + `preventDefault()` on Enter in the password field (same implicit-submit trap, would have saved+navigated instead of unlocking the key). ⚠ **Rule for this file, pinned by `IdentityDocumentSection.test.ts`**: every `<Button>`/`<button>` in the identity components must carry `type="button"` — the test scans source via `?raw` (vitest runs in the `node` env here, no DOM) after stripping comments, because the header comment explaining the rule writes `<Button>` in prose. `ProfileEditForm` itself audited clean (all 11 buttons already typed). Also closed the **observability** hole that made the reports unfalsifiable: the upload's raw `fetch` and the crypto before it reported nowhere (only the two `kscwApi` legs self-log) → `captureApiError` now wraps the handler. **Crop/rotate editor** added alongside (`react-easy-crop`, new dep): full-screen, portalled to `<body>` **on purpose** so an overlay can never repeat the same class of bug from inside the form; zoom/90° rotate/aspect presets (ID-1 85.6×54mm, 4:3, 3:4). ⚠ Crop maths uses the **rotated bounding box** — `croppedAreaPixels` is expressed in that space, so the scratch canvas must be sized `rotatedSize()`, not a diagonal square, or the crop lands off-target; canvas is white-filled first (a crop overhanging the photo goes black under JPEG). Cropping happens **before** `encryptDocument()`, so the trimmed background never reaches the store. ⚠ PDFs and images the browser cannot decode (iPhone **HEIC** on Android Chrome — `image/*` is not a promise a decoder exists) skip the editor and upload as-is rather than dead-ending on a black frame; the file input clears in `onChange`, not after upload, or re-picking the *same* photo after a cancel fires no change event. Strings in all 5 locales. Gates ✓ (build, lint 0 errors, 850 unit + 5 new).
- **2026-08-03** **"No document on file" stopped being reported as an error (frontend only, dev+prod)** — `/identity/document/:id` answers `404 + code no_document` when a member has never uploaded one, which is **most** members. `kscwApi` treated that as a failure: a Sentry event plus a `console.error` on **every** `/profile/edit` visit. Two costs, and the second is the one that matters — it reads as `api_error` on a page that is working correctly, and the volume helps trip the **`kscw-sentry-tunnel` worker's 60/min per-IP cap**, so the resulting `429`s drop *real* errors that happen in the same burst. A noisy non-error was therefore degrading error reporting generally. ⚠ `EXPECTED_VALIDATION_ENDPOINTS` already carves out exactly this class but matches on **exact path**, and `/identity/document/8` carries a record id — so it could never match. Added `EXPECTED_ERROR_CODES`, the same carve-out keyed on the **parsed response `code`** instead of the path; extend that set for any future id-bearing endpoint whose 4xx means "asked a question, answer is none". Still `throw`s, so every caller's `catch` keeps driving the UI unchanged. No version bump (no user-visible behaviour change, per the versioning discipline).
- **2026-08-03** **SES bounce/complaint handling — the platform's first suppression list (migration 277, dev+prod)** — nothing consumed SES delivery events, so a dead address was retried on every send forever. Survivable while every audience came from the active register; **not** survivable once the mailbox gained a 376-contact former-members audience with departures back to 2018. ⚠ The risk is the **shared identity**, not the failed delivery: bounces/complaints accrue against the SES identity that also carries password resets, signup invites, scheduling mail and expense reimbursements, and SES suspends on reputation, not per campaign. New `email_suppressions` (partial unique on `released_at IS NULL` → a retried SNS notification is a no-op via **targetless** `ON CONFLICT`; `released_at` un-suppresses without losing why), `POST /kscw/ses/notify` (SNS webhook), `email-suppression.js`. Consumed by **both** send paths — mailbox group send (surfaces a `suppressed` count in the preview so it is visible, not silent) and the announcements fanout, the higher-volume sender. ⚠⚠ **The webhook is unauthenticated by necessity** (SNS cannot present a bearer token) and is authenticated **solely** by RSA-verifying Amazon's signature — that check is all that stands between the open internet and "suppress any address you like", which would stop club mail one address at a time with no error anywhere. The cert URL is matched `^sns\.<region>\.amazonaws\.com$`, **not** `endsWith('amazonaws.com')` — `evil-amazonaws.com` passes the naive form and is the classic way SNS verification is got wrong. Set `SES_SNS_TOPIC_ARN` to pin the topic (a valid signature only proves *some* AWS account signed it). ⚠ Only **Permanent** bounces suppress — a transient bounce is a full mailbox, and suppressing those would quietly erode the club's own list; complaints always suppress. ⚠ `loadSuppressed` **fails OPEN**: a broken suppression table must never stop the GV invitation. Forgery verified rejected on dev **and** prod (unsigned / attacker cert host / forged SubscriptionConfirmation → all 403, 0 rows written). **AWS wiring completed and verified end-to-end the same day** (SNS topic `kscw-ses-events` in **eu-central-1** → SES Bounce+Complaint event destinations → HTTPS subscription, confirmed; steps in the `ses-notify.js` header). Proven on **prod** against `bounce@simulator.amazonses.com`: `processed Bounce: 1 new suppression(s)`, row written with `reason=bounce, subtype=General, smtp; 550 5.1.1 user unknown`. ⚠ **Idempotency is proven in production, not just in test** — SNS delivers **at-least-once**, and it duly delivered the same notification twice; the second logged `0 new suppression(s)`, so the partial unique + targetless `ON CONFLICT` held against real duplicate traffic. That also retires the earlier "paired 200s" mystery: those were ordinary SNS duplicates, not a broken subscription. `SES_SNS_TOPIC_ARN` **is** pinned on prod and the wrong-topic guard is confirmed live (`rejected message from unexpected topic …:1:other`). Test row deleted afterwards — both suppression tables are back to 0. ⚠ **Dev has no `SES_SNS_TOPIC_ARN`**, so on dev a validly-signed message from *any* AWS account would be accepted; harmless today (no SNS subscription points at dev, dev sends no member mail, and the DB is scrubbed nightly) but pin it before dev is ever subscribed — and note that env changes need a container **recreate**, not a restart.
- **2026-08-03** **Mailbox audience picker → chips, multi-select, sektion + former members (no migration)** — dropdown replaced by toggle chips in rows (Everyone / Sections / Players / Roles / Teams / Former members), each carrying its live count, because the catalogue is the useful part: an operator must *see* that "all scorers" exists and how big it is. Multi-select unions the selections and dedupes **once over the combined set** (per-group dedup would mail a coach of D1 twice when both chips are on); members resolve before ClubDesk contacts so a person in both keeps their opt-out. ⚠ **Sektion ≠ sport**: the sport groups walk active rosters and so reach players only — VB 200 vs sektion 279, BB 258 vs 282 — so offering only the roster version silently dropped 79 volleyball people from "email volleyball", and the **KSCW section (110)** belonged to no sport chip at all. Both are now chips, relabelled. ⚠ Unknown group keys reject the **whole** request rather than resolving the rest — silently dropping a chip would send to a smaller audience than the operator just confirmed. **Guests**: 38 of 41 guests on active teams already carry a paying fee category *and* an active register status, so "guests are included if they pay" needed no code — the only 3 excluded are `Kein Beitrag` ex-members, out of the register audiences but still in their own team's chip so a coach can reach them.
- **2026-08-03** **16 stale `kscw_membership_active` flags cleared via `/clubdesk-deactivate` (prod, data-only)** — ClubDesk recorded them 'Ehemaliges Mitglied' with real Austritt dates (2018 → 06.2026); all had zero rosters, zero staff roles, no upcoming participations. Ran the club's own deactivation endpoint 16× rather than hand SQL, so every guard was re-verified server-side and each write is audited: 16/16 `success`, 0 rosters dropped, **scorer_vb 149 → 141** (8 ex-members leave the "all Schreiber" audience — without this a scorer-course mail reaches people who left in 2018). ⚠ **3 deliberately NOT deactivated** — guest players on 2026/27 active teams (`guest_level=1`, one logged in 02.08); the flag means "active participant", the register means "member", so they keep team mail and are excluded from GV audiences by status alone. ⚠ **Audit gap found**: all 16 `user_logs` rows landed `user = NULL` because `writeUserLog` keys the actor on `members.id` and `admin@kscw.ch` is a Directus-only account with no member row — pre-existing in `/clubdesk-deactivate`, affects any admin action through that account, worth fixing against the actor-capture rule.
- **2026-08-03** **"All members" = the ClubDesk register, not the `kscw_membership_active` flag (no migration)** — user spec: *"all active members + honorary members … such an email would be for GV"*. The honorary half needed **no code**: all 11 Ehrenmitglieder are already `members` rows with `kscw_membership_active=true` (8 mailable). The flag alone, though, gives 695 and includes **19 people ClubDesk records as 'Ehemaliges Mitglied'** — all `Kein Beitrag`, all with a real Austritt date (2018 → 06.2026) — who would have been invited to a general assembly they left the club before. `all_members` now joins `clubdesk_people` and requires `status IN (Aktivmitglied, Passivmitglied, Ehrenmitglied, Zwischenjahr)` → **671 (658 mailable)**; user picked this over strict Aktiv+Ehren (561) because Passiv/Zwischenjahr are members for GV purposes. ⚠ **The drift runs BOTH ways** — 4 contacts ClubDesk calls 'Kein Mitglied' hold active roster spots *and* real fee categories, so a naive status filter would have dropped real participants; they are correctly out of the *register* audience but must NOT be "fixed" into members. ⚠ Only `members` row without a ClubDesk link is `KSCW System` (470) — the join excludes it for free. ⚠ 3 of the 19 are **guest players** (`member_teams.guest_level=1` on a 2026/27 active team, one logged in 02.08) — ex-members who still play; their `kscw_membership_active` is arguably CORRECT (the flag means "active participant", the register means "member"), so they keep team-scoped mail and are excluded from the GV group by status alone. That distinction is the whole reason the fix landed in the resolver rather than in the data.
- **2026-08-03** **Club mailbox group send — ClubDesk-style mass mail to teams/subgroups (v1.62.0, no migration)** — asked for as "send email from club, manage contacts and groups". Feasibility answer first: **SES was never the constraint** (671 distinct member addresses; production mode eu-central-1 clears the whole register in ~75s at even the conservative tier), and `/admin/announcements` already targeted every group named. The genuine gaps were that announcements are *news posts*, not *email* — no attachments, replies land in a personal inbox, no Sent record — so the send moved into the club mailbox where it belongs. New `POST /kscw/admin/mailbox/bulk` + `GET …/groups`, registered on the `/admin/mailbox` family **only** (the Spielplanung gate grants `is_spielplaner`, which must never imply mailing the club). ⚠ **One message per recipient, never one with N addresses in `To`** — a 149-address header discloses every member's address to every other member and can't be personalised; the cost is N sends, absorbed by a pooled transport (`pool:true, maxConnections:5, rateLimit:10/s`, well under SES's ceiling) instead of the reply handler's per-send transport. Audience comes from the **shared** resolver — `resolveMemberAudience` was lifted out of the announcements fanout into `kscw-endpoints/src/audience.js` and the hook now imports it, so "all Schreiber" cannot mean two different sets (extraction verified line-by-line against the old inline block: only param threading + log wording differ, zero query-logic change; the hook passes `announcements #<id>` as the label so skipped-fanout warnings keep naming the post). `dry_run` is mandatory in the UI, not a nicety — CLAUDE.md forbids test-mailing real members, so the preview is the only safe way to see reach; it returns count + the noEmail/optedOut/duplicate breakdown (**the gap is the point**: "team of 14 → 11 emails" reads as a bug until you see 3 have no address) and first-name+initial samples, never addresses. Also: dedup by lowercased email (693 member addresses are 671 distinct — shared family inboxes would otherwise get N copies), `email_notify_announcements` honoured, RFC 2369 `List-Unsubscribe` mailto, `{{vorname}}`/`{{nachname}}` merge fields substituted **after** sanitisation so a name can't inject markup, one Sent-folder append + one `scheduling_emails` row (not N), `writeUserLog` actor trail. ⚠ The i18n merge hint passes the tokens as interpolation **values** — a literal `{{vorname}}` inside a locale string is eaten by i18next before it renders. ⚠ **New `audience_type: 'all_members'`** — verifying the picker's counts against prod caught the same class of bug as the gate fix above: `'all'` resolves on `wiedisync_active`, so a mailbox group labelled "All members" would have reached **204 of 695**. Right for an announcement (a post in an app you must be able to open), wrong for email. Added as its own type gating on `kscw_membership_active` (→ 695, 682 mailable) rather than loosening `'all'`, which would silently widen every existing `audience_type='all'` announcement — that widening is a product decision, not a refactor. ⚠ Preview state is keyed by its group rather than paired with a `loading` flag: the obvious version writes state synchronously inside the effect and trips the cascading-render lint rule (a real one — see [[react-301-render-phase-setstate]]). Stale comments corrected in passing: `AdminMailboxPage.tsx` claimed `vorstand`/`VorstandRoute` (the guard is `GlobalAdminRoute` = admin/superuser, and vorstand was explicitly rejected 2026-07-16), and the module header said THREE accounts when `vis_transfers` makes four. Gates ✓ (build, lint 0 errors, 845 unit, 83 scripts). **Not exercised against a live send** — dry-run only, per the no-mass-test rule; first real send should be a single-member group.
- **2026-08-03** **Announcement `fn:`/`qual:` audience tokens were silently under-sending (no migration, hook-only)** — came out of a feasibility review of ClubDesk-style mass mail to teams/subgroups. `/admin/announcements` already covers every group asked for (teams, sport, `fn:coach`, `qual:scorer_vb`, `qual:referee_vb|bb`, `role:vorstand`), so the question was never "can we" — but `membersWithRoleTokens` gated **all three token namespaces** on `wiedisync_active`, i.e. on having logged into the app at least once. Only 204 of 705 members have. Measured cost on prod before the fix: **scorer_vb 149 → 106 reached (43 dropped), referee_bb 9 → 2 (7 dropped), referee_vb 13 → 11, fn:coach 34 → 30** — every send reported success, so this was invisible. Gate is now **per-namespace, not per-audience-type**: `role:*` keeps `wiedisync_active` (it IS the app-permission column, and widening it would turn `role:user`'s 617 rows into a de-facto all-members blast that `audience_type='all'` already provides), while `fn:*`/`qual:*` move to `kscw_membership_active`, matching what the teams/sport branches have always used — a Schreiber is a Schreiber whether or not they use the app. ⚠ All 43+7+2+4 newly-reached members have an email AND `email_notify_announcements=true`, so this is a real delivery increase, not a paper one — verified on prod before shipping. ⚠ Not tested by sending (CLAUDE.md forbids mass-mailing real users); verified by running the resolver's SQL against prod read-only. ⚠ `event-notify.js` has the **opposite** shape for the same tokens — no activity gate at all, so event invites can reach ex-members; deliberately left alone (different collection, invite semantics, out of scope) but worth a look. Gates ✓ (build, eslint, 845 unit, 83 scripts).
- **2026-08-03** **Trainerausbildung — a coaching qualification on the member record, two-way synced with ClubDesk (migrations 274–276, dev+prod, no version bump)** — asked for as "Trainer Licence, multiple choice between J+S, C, B, A, NULL is fine". The cardinality was the whole design question: **J+S (Jugend+Sport) is the federal track and C/B/A is the federation trainer ladder**, so they are orthogonal — a J+S-qualified Trainer B is ordinary and the column had to be a SET, not a rung. Stored exactly like `nationalitaet_codes` (223): an ordered comma-separated list in one varchar, CHECK-guarded `^(JS|C|B|A)(,…)*$`, normalized to canonical JS,C,B,A by trigger so equality and display are stable however it was typed. ⚠ The normalizer deliberately **preserves unknown tokens** rather than dropping them — silently swallowing a typo is worse than the CHECK's loud 400 (verified: `'b, js ,C'` → `'JS,C,B'`, `'D'` → violation, `'  '` → NULL, all inside a rolled-back tx on the dev clone before anything shipped). ⚠ Registered in Directus as **`interface: 'input'`, NOT `select-multiple-checkbox`**: the multi-select interfaces require `special='cast-csv'`, which makes the items API hand back an ARRAY while every raw-knex reader in kscw-endpoints still sees the CSV string — the exact dual shape `nationalitaet_codes` avoided by staying a plain string, and this column is read from both sides too. Surfaces: profile-edit toggle chips (member-editable — self-asserted like the scorer/referee flags, since the club has no machine source), its own card on the profile, `/admin/explore` (grouped under Licences, inline checkbox editor, chip filter), i18n ×5. Permissions: joins `MEMBER_EDITABLE_FIELDS` **and** `MEMBER_VISIBLE_FIELDS` — same tier as the licence booleans, a sporting credential rather than PII; Public deliberately excluded (verified on prod: Member read+update `t`, TR read `t`, Public `f`). ⚠ **`setup-perms:dev` is still 413/124 on the keyless licence**, and the consequence was confirmed concretely rather than assumed: the KSCW Member field list on **dev** still lacks the column, so a normal member cannot use it there — admins can (Sport Admin/Vorstand hold `fields = '*'`). Prod reconciled clean at **537/0**. **Then ClubDesk gained a "Trainer Lizenz" column mid-session** and the scope grew a second half (**275**). ⚠⚠ It was created as an `Auswahl` **picklist** (`J+S¦A¦B¦C`) — single-valued, which would have forced a lossy collapse rule (highest rung? J+S first?) on a field whose entire point is that you can hold both. Flagged it; the user switched the field to **Text** before anything was written, and the sync became lossless. UP = `trainerLicenceCell` renders the set in the club's own wording (`JS,B` → `J+S, B`), echo-protected exactly like Federation of Origin (an unanswered member sends ClubDesk's own cell back, never a blank).
