DOWN = parsed back to codes, **fill-only** — members declare this in their own profile, so a stale register value must never overwrite their answer. ⚠ Parsing exists in **two** implementations by necessity — SQL for the down-sync (the register lands in the staging table before any JS runs) and JS for drift + tests — and both must lift `'J+S'` out **before** matching bare rungs, or its `+` splits it into junk; rungs match as whole letters so the `a` in "Trainer" and the `b` in "Basketball" cannot forge a qualification. Both parsers were proven against the same 14 real spellings (`j+s / b`, `Jugend+Sport, Trainer A`, `Stufe C`, `Basketball Trainer` → skip, `keine` → skip); every output satisfies 274's CHECK. Drift compares **code SETS, not rendered strings** — the cell is hand-editable so `J+S, B` / `B, J+S` / `j+s / b` are one answer and must not report as a conflict. The admin change-email renders the field twice server-side (German cell for ClubDesk, reader's language for the email) and ⚠ **never translates J+S in any locale** — it is a federal programme name, not a description. ⚠ Adding a push column shifted **every hardcoded CSV index** in the existing push tests (update set +1 from 12, create set +1 from 13) plus the staging column count 66→67 — 15 assertions re-pinned; the shape pins did their job. **276** is a comment-only fix-forward: 274's COMMENT said "ClubDesk has no counterpart column", which 275 falsified the same day, and an applied migration's sha cannot be edited (the 273 lesson). ⚠ Two self-inflicted process notes: an edit wrote **3 NUL bytes** into `clubdesk-update.js` where spaces were intended, which made `grep` silently report *zero* matches on a file that plainly contained them — `file` said `data`, not `JavaScript source`; `node --check` is now the reflex after any endpoint edit. And the working tree carries the user's untracked assets, so every commit staged explicit paths, never `-A`. Gates: build ✓, lint 0 errors, 845 unit (26 new), 83 script. Deployed dev then prod in schema-first order (274+275 → merge/push → `ext:deploy:prod` → `setup-perms:prod` → `db:baseline:prod`); the drift query was smoke-run read-only against prod (703 rows) to prove the new column references resolve before trusting the endpoint.
- **2026-08-03** **Live scoring, part 2 — the board actually publishes, plus history/final/pulse/banner (migration 273, dev+prod, v1.61.0)** — closes the "nothing feeds it" gap from part 1 and all four optionals in one pass. **Board side (`openvolley/escoresheet/ledbox-bridge`, NOT the `kscw-ledbox-public` mirror — I edited the mirror first and reverted; the mirror is a stripped snapshot with no `test/` and no multi-sport)**: `livePush.proposal.js` → `livePush.js`, wired into **`appliance.js`**, not `bridge.js` as the roadmap said — `bridge.js` has no `manualSource` at all (it is the LAN-relay-only path). Attached to the **SourceManager**, not the scoring source, so a linked LAN match publishes as well as the hand-driven board. ⚠⚠ **`BasketballSource.getState()` carries team fouls in `subs_a`/`subs_b`** and always reports `sets_won_* = 0` — an internal board convention, so livePush translates to the explicit `fouls_a`/`fouls_b` on the wire and zeroes subs. This retro-justifies 272's extra columns: without them the payload would have had to lie. `over` is derived from **match state, not the transient `match-end` event** (lastEvent is cleared on the next apply(), so an event-derived flag shows Final for one push then silently reverts to live), and per sport — `BEACH.setsToWin` = 2, volleyball 3, basketball its own `over` — imported from the sources' own rule constants so it cannot drift. wiedisync's `periodLabel` was realigned to the board's `OT1` (not a bare `OT`) so the phone and the LED panel never disagree. **Migration 273 `live_history`**: append-only log the board POSTs on the → final transition (tracked in memory; ⚠ restarting the appliance on a finished board can double-archive). Publisher gets **create and NOT update/delete** — ⚠ but it CAN read it, because the Public grant applies to authenticated requests too and Directus has no deny rule; the append-only guarantee is the absent update/delete, which IS effective (verified 403/403 on dev). ⚠ **Migration 273 was already applied when I tried to improve its header comment — reverted, because the runner rejects a sha change on an applied migration**; the corrected wording lives in setup-permissions.mjs instead. **Realtime**: `WEBSOCKETS_ENABLED=true` was **already set on both** containers and .env files — no recreate needed (the risky item evaporated). ⚠ `curl` gets **404** on `wss://directus*.kscw.ch/websocket` while a real browser gets a clean open, and the container itself returns `101` — that is the documented Cloudflare non-browser-UA firewall, NOT a broken endpoint. Don't chase it. **Frontend**: FinalSummary (winner + result, draw-safe), RecentMatches as a `<Table>` per the data-list rule (sorted on the SERVER clock `finished_at`, so a board with a wrong clock cannot reorder it), a score bump keyed on the score so it needs no state or timers, and a LiveNowBanner on /games — ⚠ deliberately worded "a match is live", never "your game is live", because `live_scores` has **no `games` FK** and binding a row to a fixture would sometimes be wrong. Verified end-to-end twice against directus-dev by running the real appliance against a mock board: score/serve/timeouts/set-end/period all landed, then a full 3-set match archived exactly one history row. ⚠ The e2e first "failed" because **`next-set` swaps ends** — scoring the same physical side alternates teams. Gates: build ✓, lint 0 errors, 819 unit, ledbox 31 appliance + 35 livepush assertions.
- **2026-08-03** **Live scoreboard `/live` wired up and made multi-sport (migration 272, dev+prod, v1.60.0)** — the page, `useLiveMatch` and the setup doc already existed from the scaffold but the route was never registered, so `/live` 404'd and the Directus side did not exist at all. Now: **migration 272** creates `live_scores` (manual string PK = channel, one row per physical board, seeded `kscw`) with the Directus collection/field registration inline — deliberately a migration and **not** hand-built in the admin UI, or dev/prod drift and a `SCHEMA.sql` fresh install misses it. Permissions are declarative in `setup-permissions.mjs` (§5 `setPermRead(PUBLIC_POLICY, 'live_scores')`, new §5b "KSCW LedBox Publisher" policy = create+read+update on that collection only) and were **applied on dev by a targeted API script rather than a full `db:setup-perms:dev` run** — dev is still keyless, so the full run fails on the filtered-permission licence and would have been noise. Service user `ledbox-board@kscw.ch` holds the policy via `directus_access` with a static token; verified scoped — `PATCH live_scores` 200, `DELETE` 403, `GET /items/members` 403, anonymous public read 200. **Multi-sport** is one row, not three tables: `sport` selects the layout, plus `period`, `over`, and — beyond the brief — **`fouls_a`/`fouls_b`**, because "team fouls + bonus" had no column to read from and reusing `subs_*` would have made the board's payload lie. `serving_team` is **reused as the basketball possession arrow** (identical left/right semantics, so no extra column). `over` is kept distinct from `status`: the former is the firmware's flag, the latter the publication lifecycle the page renders, and `status` wins. ⚠ `over` is fine unquoted as a Postgres column name (probed on PG16 before committing to it) despite being a SQL-standard reserved word. `CHECK`s pin `sport`/`status`/`serving_team` — the board's push is best-effort, so a rejected write stalls `/live` and never touches scoring. Frontend split into `VolleyballBoard` (volleyball + beach) and `BasketballBoard` behind a `Scoreboard` dispatcher, sharing `TeamIdentity` and the pure helpers in `scoreboard.ts` (kept non-JSX so react-refresh sees the component files as component-only). Beach drops the substitution counter and stacks the pair — the board publishes "Müller / Meier" in one name field. An unknown `sport` normalises to volleyball rather than blanking the page. ⚠ Two fixes came out of actually rendering it at 400px: the bonus badge beside the team chip truncated `BCZN` → `BC…`, so it moved to its own line under the meta, and the possession chevron was too faint at `text-muted-foreground` h-5. Route is **public** (no `AuthRoute`) and sits in `publicItems` next to `/games` — spectators in the hall have no session, which is also why the ~3s poll stays the baseline and realtime stays a members-only enhancement. i18n `live` namespace in all 5 locales + the `nav.live` label. Verified headless against `directus-dev` in all three sports plus the idle state, light and dark, zero console errors. **Deployed to prod the same session** (snapshot → 272 → targeted perms → own service token → `db:baseline:prod` → merge), verified anonymously end-to-end on `wiedisync.kscw.ch/live` and left idle. Permissions went out by targeted script first (rbw was locked), then — once unlocked — **a full `db:setup-perms:prod` reconcile confirmed the declarative §5/§5b blocks reproduce exactly this state**: 535 grants / 0 errors, `directus_permissions` **572 rows before and after** (a true no-op apart from the 4 new live_scores rows, which also proves 271's perms were already on prod), publisher token still 200/403 afterwards. ⚠ **Dev** keeps the targeted-script-only caveat — the full run still dies on the keyless licence. ⚠ **`db:smoke:prod` deliberately not run**: prod has no member smoke token in the vault and minting one would mutate a real member's data ([[kscw-deploy-tokens]] says don't) — the `directus_permissions` query above is the sanctioned substitute. ⚠⚠ **Security finding, documented not fixed**: Directus policies are additive and the Public policy applies to authenticated requests too, so the board token also inherits every public grant — on prod that is `members` limited to `id/first_name/last_name/photo`. It is **not** the anonymous view: migration 116's name-privacy hook abbreviates the surname only when `!currentUser`, so a token-bearing request reads `Oscar Bizard` where anonymous reads `Oscar B.` (phone/email/birthdate/AHV stay hidden either way — those gates don't test for anonymity). There is no deny rule to subtract it; the fix would be to make that hook minimise for any user with **no linked `members` row**, which changes behaviour for every service account and is therefore left as its own decision. ⚠ The dev publisher token does not survive the nightly clone (prod's `directus_users` overwrite it) — re-pin after a refresh. Gates: build ✓, lint 0 errors, 819 unit; prod smoke substituted by the perms query as above.
- **2026-08-01** **Game guest invitations — open one fixture to another team or to named players (migration 271, v1.59.0)** — asked for as "a cup game for H1 can be opened to H3, and H3 get it on their home/calendar like it was for them". The only existing way to do this was a `member_teams` row, i.e. a season-wide team membership to answer a one-Saturday question (it drags trainings, absences, attendance stats and the ClubDesk Gruppe with it). New `game_guest_teams` (the coach's intent, one chip per opened team) materializes by trigger into `game_guests` (one row per person, `via_team` = which opening produced it, NULL = invited by name and therefore surviving that opening being closed). **Materialized on purpose** — the announcement_recipients pattern: every consumer asks the flat question "is member X invited to game Y?", and a live 3-level alias walk would collide with the policy walk (the M2M-deep-filter silent-`[]` trap). ⚠ **`participations` READ changed on BOTH sides and is the review-worthy part** — a guest shares no `member_teams` row with the team whose game they play, so `SAME_TEAM_AS_ME` left the roster half-blind in both directions; two new Member branches + one LEADER branch fix it, each `_and`-ed with `activity_type='game'` so a one-Saturday loan never opens that person's trainings and events. `absences` deliberately stays narrow (the roster needs the answer, not the reason). Three metadata-only o2m aliases (`games.guests`, `members.game_guests`, `teams.games`) exist only to make those filters expressible (same device as 032/033). Guests are folded into the deadline reminder, the tomorrow reminder and the absence auto-decline via a shared `GAME_SQUAD_JOIN` fragment, into the iCal feed when the URL carries the personal token, and into `squadRows` — **without that last one a guest RSVPs "confirmed", vanishes from the Einsatzliste and is then rejected as an invalid `added` player on the match sheet**. Deliberately NOT in the auto-confirm sweep: being lent to a game is not consent to play it. ⚠ A team opening's per-person rows are written by a **Postgres trigger**, which never touches the items API — so `game_guests.items.create` does not fire for them and the notification hangs off `game_guest_teams.items.create` re-reading what the trigger wrote. Frontend: `useUserVisibleGameIds` (junction-first) OR'd into home/calendar/games; on the games page only the IMPLICIT "my teams" scope carries invitations, an explicit team filter is answered literally. Guests merge into the one roster list with a sky-blue origin badge — ⚠ distinct from the existing amber `guest_level` badge, which means the OPPOSITE thing (trains with us, may **not** play league games; `trg_participations_guest_block` still enforces that, and a game guest passes it because they have no `member_teams` row on the game's team at all). Clash handling is warn-only per user decision. Verified before deploy by running 271 + six trigger scenarios inside one rolled-back transaction on the dev clone (materialize 31→30 with the 1 overlap skipped, own-roster never leaks in, late roster addition invited, individual invite not downgraded, un-invite withdraws the RSVP, closing an opening keeps the named individual). Gates: build ✓, lint 0 errors, 819 unit, 83 script.
- **2026-08-01** **Cancelled calendar entries were rendered as live ones — the game-day auto-cancel looked broken but was not (v1.58.0, dev+prod, no migration)** — reported as "why do I have trainings on days when there are games". The sweep is fine: H3's 23.09 and 28.09 trainings are `cancelled=true` with marker games 409 (the H1 derby, H3 away) and 541 (VBC Swiss, away), i.e. **migration 261's pass 4 correctly covers away fixtures**. The gap was one layer up — `CalendarEntry` had **no cancellation field at all**: `trainingToEntry` folded it into `description` as a hardcoded German `Abgesagt:` prefix (served to all five locales) and not one of the six views read it, so a dead training was pixel-identical to a live one sitting next to the fixture that killed it. ⚠ The **subscription feed was never wrong** (`ical-feed.js` has prefixed `[ABGESAGT]` + `STATUS:CANCELLED` since it was written) — only the in-app calendar, which is why this survived so long. Added `cancelled` to the entry model (trainings, events, and games at `status='cancelled'` — **not** `postponed`, which still gets played), set it in `useCalendarData`, and rendered it through a shared `cancelledClasses()` in `entryStyle.ts` across MonthGrid / WeekGrid / MobileMonthView / MobileWeekGrid / the day-overflow modal / the home ticker. ⚠ The strike goes on the **whole row, not the title**: every grid hides the title below `lg`, so a title-only strike leaves a bare unstruck "18:00" — the same lie. The auto-cancels set no `cancel_reason` (pass 4 only writes `cancelled` + the marker), so the detail modal said a bare "Cancelled" → now falls back to `cancelledGameDay` ("Cancelled — game day") when `auto_shortened_by_game` is set; the generic Details row is suppressed for trainings because `renderTrainingDetails` was already printing the same string twice. Client-side `.ics` download now matches the feed. Also **home-page layout shift**: `UpcomingTicker` returned `null` while loading and expanded on arrival, shoving the page down — it waits on the member's teams (its scope) and only then its own 7-collection fetch, and an empty `teamIds` meant both "no teams" and "not known yet". New `scopeLoading` prop + a skeleton with the real pill's class string (⚠ bars padded to `h-5` = text-sm's 20px line box, else the skeleton is 4px short and it still jumps). **Verified live on the dev app** via a Playwright harness (screenshots in scratchpad): 23.09 + 28.09 struck at opacity 0.6, all other H3 trainings normal; home anchor y=640 across 73 samples = no shift. ⚠ Harness notes for next time — the `dev:login` proxy grants a session but it does **not** survive `page.goto` (bounces to /login), Playwright matches routes **last-registered-first** (register the bearer injector BEFORE the `/auth/refresh` stub or the stub is shadowed), `admin@kscw.ch` has **no linked `members` row** so `fetchMember()` returns null and the app logs itself out (pin the self-lookup response to a real member), and the nightly dev scrub NULLs phone/address so `isProfileComplete` is false and the onboarding gate covers the whole app. Confirmed `app_settings.profile_review` is still `enabled=false` on **both** DBs — the 270 campaign has not started. Frontend-only: no migration, no `ext:deploy`, no setup-perms. Gates: build ✓, lint 0 errors, 819 unit.
- **2026-08-01** **Cup games were never ingested — Swiss Volley's `/indoor/games` hides them behind `includeCup` (dev+prod, no migration, no version bump)** — asked as "check that cup games are ingested in the whole db, not only for rankings". They were not, and had not been since the PocketBase cutover: the feed serves league fixtures only unless `?includeCup=true` (or `=1`) is set, and `sv-sync.js` fetched the bare URL. Verified live 2026-08-01: **158 fixtures plain vs 159 with the flag**, the extra one being H3's Züri Cup R2 away at VBC Stäfa (04.11.2026, `gameId 418390`), absent from `games`. The 19 cup rows that *are* in the DB are fossils of the **2026-03-29 PB→Directus import** (`date_updated` NULL on all 19) — which is why last season's Züri Cup final (H1 vs Voléro Legends, 21.03.2026) sat at `scheduled`, 0:0: the sync could not see it to close it out. **Backfilled by hand on prod the same day** from the SVRZ match page (`id 387`: 0:3, 22:25 / 9:25 / 17:25, both referees, and the neutral venue KZO Wetzikon written to `away_hall_json` — GameCard and GameDetailModal both fall back to it regardless of `type`, so it renders on a `home` row). Ran inside a transaction with `SET LOCAL kscw.skip_games_notify='on'`; the trigger's own `result_available` past-date guard (>3 days) would have suppressed it anyway — verified 0 notification rows for the game before and after. Impact of the gap is not cosmetic — a cup **home** game produced no calendar entry, no RSVP, no Hallenplan virtual slot (so the hall read free and the game-day training auto-cancel never fired) and no scorer row; 8 of last season's 19 were home games. ⚠ **Flipping the flag alone would have shipped a second bug**: a cup payload INVERTS the caption layout — `league.caption`="Züri Cup" names the competition while `group.caption`="Runde 3, Spiel 2" is just the pairing, whereas a league fixture keeps its full name on `group` ("Frauen 2. Liga") and only a code on `league` ("2L"). The group-first mapping would therefore have stored a label with **no "Cup" in it**, and every downstream classifier keys off exactly that string: `isCupGame()` (cup home games are the playing team's OWN scorer duty — the 2026-07-09 club ruling; a mislabelled row would summon another team), `detectCupMatch()` (Spielplanung gold/silver chips), the GamesPage cup section, and the "league only" attendance stats. New exported `deriveLeagueLabel(g)` prefixes the competition for cups only (and never double-prefixes a group caption that already names it), reproducing the legacy `"Züri Cup — Runde 1, Spiel 12"` shape. **Rankings were never affected** (`/indoor/ranking` returns the same 17 groups with or without the flag — cups have no standings), and **basketball is fine**: `bp-sync` pulls each team's whole Basketplan XML (`findTeamById.do`), which is competition-agnostic (the `-ClassicsF`/`-ClassicsM` rows prove it). Dev run after deploy: `created 1, updated 0, skipped 160` — zero churn on the league fixtures, i.e. the label refactor is behaviour-identical for them. 5 new unit tests (819 pass).
- **2026-08-01** **Three features, two migrations (269 + 270), two of them shipping DARK (v1.57.0, dev+prod)** — (1) **Hallenfinder dimensions + photos (269).** The finder told a coach *when* a city hall is free but nothing about whether it is usable — the stock runs from 14×6.5 m Gymnastikräume to 44×22 m Doppelhallen with ceilings from 2.7 m to 9 m. Every `details.php?einrichtung=<id>` page already publishes `Gesamtfläche` as `L x B x H`, the per-partition sizes, a photo for about half of them, and the rental contact. New pure parser `hallenfinder/parse-details.mjs` (5 `node --test` tests against 3 real saved pages) + `hallenfinder-details.mjs` (emit-SQL, no DB access by design — the id list is passed in) + `hallenfinder-details-run.sh` (`npm run hallenfinder:details:dev|prod`). Runs **monthly**, not nightly: floor plans never change, and folding it into the nightly job would multiply requests at the city's server by ~100 for nothing — `hallenfinder-sync.sh` gained a day-of-month-1 pass (`HALLENFINDER_DETAILS=1` forces it). Backfill: **104/104 halls have dimensions, 47 have a photo, 102 a contact address, 7 report height `0,00`**. ⚠ `0,00` is *unmeasured*, not zero — the parser maps it to NULL so a height filter can never drop those 7 as "too low". ⚠ **`hall_type` deliberately untouched**: it is name-derived (`hallenfinder-scrape.mjs`) and is what the UI's type filter matches on, while the detail page's authoritative Hallentyp uses a different vocabulary ("Einfachhalle" where the derived value says "sporthalle") — it lands in its own `hall_type_label`. Photos are **hotlinked, never mirrored** (City of Zürich owns them; the page is coach/admin-only), which needs `https://www.ssd-sporthallen.stadt-zuerich.ch` in the `img-src` CSP in `public/_headers` or every thumbnail fails silently. ⚠ A first cut also derived a "fits a volleyball/basketball court" verdict from L×W×H; **removed on user instruction — show the dimensions exactly as the city writes them** (`size_label` verbatim, Swiss decimal comma), because a threshold is our guess dressed as a fact. ⚠ `hallenfinder:deploy` installs into the SHARED `/opt/hallenfinder-sync/`, which the **prod** cron runs — it must not go out before 269 is on prod. (2) **Annual pre-licence data check (270), ships DISABLED.** `members.profile_verified_at` + a new `app_settings.value` text column (the table was key+enabled only, nowhere to put a cutoff); a campaign is `key='profile_review', enabled=true, value='<ISO date>'` and a member is due when their stamp is missing or older — next season is two field edits, not a deploy. Second hard gate in `Layout.tsx` reusing 262's machinery, **only when `isProfileComplete`** so the two gates cannot stack. ⚠ **`verify` is NOT a variant of `onboarding`**: that mode *hides* the read-only ClubDesk block, and the fee category + licence living in there are exactly what the campaign asks people to check. It is a **confirmation, not a completeness check** — the core contact set stays required (262's rule) but IBAN/AHV do not, because plenty of members legitimately have neither and requiring them would permanently trap the people with nothing to fix. ⚠ `profile_verified_at` added to `MEMBER_EDITABLE_FIELDS` — self-asserted by design (only the member can assert "I looked and it's correct"), and **without the grant Directus silently strips the field from their save and the gate reappears at every login for all ~200 members**. Only verifiable on prod (dev setup-perms still fails on the keyless licence). (3) **Support-the-developer link, also DARK.** `/support` page (not a modal — the entry point is inside the MoreSheet drawer and a vaul Drawer on top of it would stack), reachable from the options accordion + the changelog footer. Rails: a **Payrexx** paylink (CHF + TWINT native, ~1.35–1.65%) and TWINT person-to-person by phone number. ⚠ Buy Me a Coffee was chosen first and dropped — its six creator currencies do not include CHF, so Swiss donors would eat FX on top of ~8% fees; Ko-fi has the same TWINT gap. ⚠ No Swiss QR-bill: the payload legally requires the creditor's street address, i.e. publishing Luca's home address to ~200 members. Hidden for under-18 accounts (fails closed on an unknown birthdate) and while impersonating; kill switch `app_settings.donate_enabled` where **no row = on**, so it needs no seed data. Both constants (`PAYREXX_URL`, `TWINT_NUMBER`) are empty → `donateConfigured()` false → no menu row, no changelog link, `/support` redirects home. Gates: build ✓, lint 0 errors, 814 unit, 83 script tests.

Georgiev, coach of D1, Austritt 19.05.2022), of which 3 BB juniors (Berra/Jaroch/Lee, July-2026 Austritt + `Kein Mitglied` + on rosters) stay held pending their coaches. ⚠ **A 464-row dry-run was aborted on the mapping screenshot**: `Kein Beitrag` showed under "Nicht erkannte Werte" (picklist value not yet saved) — committing would have zeroed 418 Mitgliederbeiträge while writing no category at all. **Always read the mapping shot, not just neu/veraendert.** Post-commit attribution against the 29.07 baseline confirmed **zero clobber of the ClubDesk admin's parallel work** (his 8 season re-categorisations, 20 Bemerkungen, 7 Betrag-Bezahlt entries all sit outside the 4 columns these batches write). ⚠ **One over-reach, caught by the treasurer's worklist sheet and reverted the same hour**: Stephanie Huwiler (1000724) carries a stale 2022 Austritt but is on a *Pause* and guests for D1 — she has no `member_teams` row, so the roster guard couldn't see her and the batch made her Ehemalige. Restored to `Aktivmitglied` + `VB Erwerbstätige` (Betrag left at the 0 a human had already set) + Austritt cleared. **Lesson: the roster/staff guard only sees people wiedisync knows about — a ClubDesk-only or roster-less member is invisible to it, so a human worklist is still the authority on who actually left.** Cross-checked all 40 CD-Ids on the sheet against both push files afterwards: every other row matches the sheet's verdict. Not a staff-resolution bug: `exportRows` appended the waitlist + staff blocks inside **`if (statusFilter === null)`**, while the on-screen sections render on `waitlistedParts.length > 0` / `visibleStaffMembers.length > 0` — i.e. under **every** filter. So exporting with "Confirmed" (or any) filter produced a sheet with the staff and waitlist blocks missing entirely while both were still visible in the modal behind it. Uhlmann) → the export had been taken under a Confirmed filter → the gate, not the staff pipeline. Both blocks now append unconditionally (the export mirrors the modal; the status filter narrows the **roster block** only), and `positionSummary` — documented as "the same population that exportRows covers" — had the identical gate and was drifting from it for the same reason. ⚠ `exportMeta.totalCount` stays `exportRows.length` (rows in the sheet, not rows matching the filter), which is what it already meant under the "All" filter; the header line therefore reads e.g. "Filter: Confirmed (39)" for 33 confirmed + 4 staff + 2 waitlisted. - **2026-07-30** **Staff RSVP on per-day events was stored as player RSVP — plus a row-boundary PDF pager (v1.56.0, frontend-only + a 6-row prod backfill)** — asked as "what about the staff (RSVP for Trainingsweekend)". Root cause: **`SessionParticipationSheet` hardcoded `is_staff: false`**, and it is the ONLY per-day RSVP path (`EventDetailModal` renders nothing else; the card's "Per day" button opens it) — so every staff answer on a per-day event became a player row. Live proof on prod: all 80 rows of event 2 were `is_staff=f`, including the two D1 coaches who had actually answered. The failure is a **double invisibility**: the roster modal's Staff section reads only `is_staff=true` rows → "Not responded" for people who answered, while their rows were ALSO dropped from the player tally (`playerParticipations` requires `member ∈ roster of invited teams`, and staff-only leaders have no `member_teams` row) — yet `ParticipationSummary` fetches **unrestricted**, so the card counted them as confirmed players. Card ≠ modal, which is the exact drift class the v4.6.7 dedupe comments were written to prevent. ⚠ The one writer that *did* pass the flag (`EventCard.setAll`) derived it from **`event.teams[0]`** — as did `EventDetailModal:43-44` — so on a two-team event a coach of the second team was a "player" anyway; replaced by **`isStaffOnlyForTeams(teamIds)`** on AuthProvider (staff of ≥1 invited team, on none of their rosters). **Staff rows are now fully editable** (pencil, status, note, the Overall tab's all-days fan-out, per-day count badge): writes resolve through the same `sessionTargets`, but over a **combined pool** (`participations` + `staffParticipationRows`) — the staff fetch is a separate query precisely because the roster fetch is `member IN <roster ids>`, and without merging it every save would miss the existing row and duplicate-insert into 246's partial unique. Also session-scoped `getStaffMemberStatus` (an unscoped `.find` rendered Saturday's answer on Sunday's tab — 27088c87's bug, still live on the staff side), **"Staff present" now counts distinct members** (rows would double a coach who said yes to both days), staff export rows carry the per-day breakdown and scope to the day tab, and the late-signin **fine prompt is suppressed for staff** (they are not on the roster the fine would be issued against). **PDF**: the pager rasterizes one tall PNG and sliced it at a fixed page height, cutting the last row of every page across the fold; rows now carry `data-export-row` and `nextSliceEnd()` ends each page on the last row boundary that fits, hard-cutting only if a single row exceeds a page (loop must advance). Slice heights rounded — a canvas truncates a fractional height and drifted the read offset per page. 8 unit tests on the pager. ⚠ **Prod data backfill, done same day**: 6 rows on event 2 (members 61 Kanagalingam, 527 Müller, 528 Georgiev × 2 sessions) flipped to `is_staff=true` — verified inert against all three `participations` triggers first (chat-sync keys on `status` and ignores `is_staff`, the auto-marker needs a status change, the guest block is game-only). Between the backfill and the deploy the OLD modal code showed "4 Staff present" for 2 people (row-counting); the card was already correct (it dedupes by member before the staff split). Gates: build ✓, lint 0 errors, 180 util tests.
- **2026-07-29** **Overall tab of a per-day event is now editable — one pick writes every day (v1.55.0, frontend-only)** — follow-up to the session-less-orphan fix below, which left the Overall tab read-only (count badge, no pencil) so a leader still had to walk each day tab repeating the same answer. Now the pencil renders there too and fans the write out across `eventSessions` (`Promise.all`, same shape as `EventCardSessionParticipation.setAll`); each day keeps its OWN row, only the days that disagree are written. Decision logic extracted to **`src/utils/participationSessions.ts`** (`resolveSessionTargets` / `statusWrites` / `noteWrites` / `uniformValue` / `allConfirmed`) — pure, node-env, **24 unit tests** (repo has no `@testing-library/react`, so the testable surface has to be pure). ⚠ Two traps handled: (1) the prefill must be **blank when days disagree** — a status from one day would pretend to speak for all, and a note only one day carries would get copied onto every day; (2) since a mixed member's note box renders blank, the note `onBlur` now writes **only when `value !== defaultValue`**, otherwise merely opening the pencil and clicking away would blank every day's note. `allConfirmed(targets)` replaces the old `currentStatus !== 'confirmed'` guard on the late-signin fine prompt (identical for the single-target case). New i18n `allDays` + `editAllDays` in all 5 locales.
- **2026-07-29** **Roster-modal RSVP edits on per-day events wrote session-less orphans (frontend-only, no migration)** — reported as "I did change to confirmed but it didn't refresh, it looks still not answered", followed by `POST /items/participations 400` — `Value for field "activity_type, activity_id, member" … has to be unique` (prod JSONL, member 186 / event 2 Trainingsweekend, two attempts 8s apart). `ParticipationRosterModal.handleStatusChange` + `handleNoteChange` **never wrote `session_id` on create**, but on a per-day event `session_id` is part of the row's identity: the leader edits from a *session tab*, the create landed as a session-less row, and every per-day view keys off `session_id` (`sessionParticipationByMember` literally `continue`s on `!p.session_id`) → the roster still read "Not responded" → second pick → 400 on migration 246's `(activity_type, activity_id, member) WHERE session_id IS NULL` partial unique. **Migration 246 is the messenger, not the cause** — before it this silently inserted a duplicate every click. Second defect in the same handlers: the lookup `participations.find(p => p.member === …)` was unscoped, and while `regularParticipations` *is* session-filtered the **club-wide fetch is not**, so on a club-wide per-day event editing Friday could have updated Saturday's row. Fix = `activeSessionId` + a scoped `findParticipation()`, both creates carry `session_id`. ⚠ **Pre-existing orphans are NOT auto-healed**: 3 rows on event 2 (members 7 Ghoul confirmed, 18 Sadikovic declined, 186 Donatsch confirmed) are invisible to the per-day roster yet still counted by `ParticipationSummary` — needs a manual materialize-per-session + delete. Grep guard for the broken shape: a `create({ … activity_type: 'event' …})` with no `session_id` spread. The Overall tab stays read-only (count badge, no pencil) — editing all days at once is still unbuilt.
- **2026-07-29** **Every M2M edit form 400s since migration 245 — junction PKs now round-trip (frontend-only, no migration)** — reported as "moved the respond-by date up 2 days and it errored": editing **any** field of an existing event failed with `Value for field "events_id, teams_id" in collection "events_teams" has to be unique.` (`RECORD_NOT_UNIQUE`, one hit in the prod JSONL, event 2 / Trainingsweekend). Nothing to do with `respond_by` — **Directus treats an M2M junction object without its own primary key as a CREATE**, upserts the whole array, and only deletes the now-unreferenced rows *afterwards*, so re-sending an unchanged link as `{ teams_id: 92 }` INSERTs a second `(event, 92)` row while the original is still there. That silently produced duplicate junction rows for years (245 deleted a pile of them); the moment 245 added the composite pair uniques the same write aborts the whole PATCH. **The index is right — the write pattern was always wrong**, so this is 245 exposing a latent bug, not regressing one. Fix = `m2mUpdatePayload(field, relatedIds, existingJunctionRows)` in `src/lib/api.ts`: kept links go back as `{ id: <junction pk>, teams_id }` (no-op update), only genuinely new ones stay PK-less, omitted ones still get deleted by Directus. Reproduced **and** verified against dev Directus by curl before/after (old payload → 400 `RECORD_NOT_UNIQUE`; new payload → 200; add+drop+keep mix → correct insert/delete/keep), plus 11 unit tests. ⚠ **The read side is half the fix**: the junction PK is only returned if `fields` names it — `teams.id` *next to* `teams.teams_id`, `coach.id` next to `coach.members_id`; a `teams.teams_id.*` expand does NOT include it and the helper silently degrades back to duplicate-inserts. Four call sites, all broken the same way: EventForm (`events_teams` + `events_members`), FormBuilder (`forms_teams`), SlotEditor via `teamToM2M` (`hall_slots_teams`), and the team staff editors ManageStaffModal/MemberRow (`teams_coaches`/`teams_responsibles` — there it bites on the *second* change of a session, the first one's new row being the duplicate, so those two also read the saved junctions back off the PATCH response via the new optional `fields` arg on `updateRecord`). `captain` deliberately left alone — M2O, no junction. Grep guard for the broken shape: `map((id) => ({ teams_id` / `({ members_id`.

'dm' : 'dm_request'`, so teammates whose shared team existed only on the new-season roster silently got an **approval-gated DM request instead of a direct DM** — measured on prod the day it was found: **82 members and 29 teams** had a 2026/27 roster row with no 2025/26 counterpart. Now: **`src/utils/season.ts`** (frontend) + **`kscw-endpoints/src/season.js`** — only two JS copies, because `kscw-hooks` **imports across the extension boundary** (it already does that for error-log/sentry/email-template/push-i18n/signup-invites/bb-docs/scorer-roster, so no third copy is needed; both extensions rsync together). Migrated: dateHelpers, formatSeason, RecurringTrainingModal, useFines, and endpoints index(×2)/event-notify/forms/clubdesk-update/identity-document/ical-feed/game-scheduling(×2)/messaging-helpers + hooks index(×2)/slot-cascade. ⚠ **Five sites deliberately NOT aligned, now annotated `— deliberate` in-code so the next audit doesn't "fix" them**: `jsExport.ts` (Sep 1 — J+S reports the Sep–Aug activity year, off-season must name the season that just *finished*), `gcal-sync.js` (Sep 1 — a churn floor for closure rows, not a season label), `bp-sync.js` (Sep 1 — **Basketplan's** calendar, not ours), and the matched pair `bb-docs.js` + `AnmeldungenPage.tsx` (Jul 1 + Sep 1 age reference — licence age-band rule; change both or neither). Verified `grep` finds **zero** residual inline cutovers outside the two modules, and both extension module graphs still `import()` cleanly (a bad path here kills all `/kscw/*`). Gates: build ✓, lint 0 errors, 778 unit.

- **2026-07-29** **One season cutover, not two — Postgres moved onto Jun 1 (migration 268, dev+prod)** — follow-up to the season-floor work below, which surfaced that `kscw_current_season_start()` (migration 069) still used a **Sep 1** cutover while every JS caller had long since moved to **Jun 1** (`getCurrentSeason()` in `src/utils/dateHelpers.ts`, `currentSeasonLong()` in the scheduling app, and the local copies in `event-notify.js` / `forms.js` / `clubdesk-update.js`). Across Jun–Aug the two disagreed **by a whole season** — on 2026-07-29 JS said 2026/27 (start 2026-09-01), PG said 2025/26 (start 2025-09-01) — and the function's own COMMENT still claimed it mirrored the JS. 268 changes the cutover month only (`v_month <= 8` → `<= 5`); the return stays "Sep 1 of the current season". ⚠ **A naive month swap would have opened a hole in the fines counter**: with the Jun 1 cutover, `kscw_fine_window_start('season')` anchored on Sep 1 returns a date in the *future* from Jun 1 to Aug 31, so every offense issued over the summer would sort BEFORE its own window start and never be counted (a fine issued 2026-07-10 would sit outside the 2026/27 window forever). The 'season' branch therefore anchors on the **Jun 1 rollover** (`season_start − 3 months`) — stable within the season, derived from the same single source; every other branch is byte-identical to 069. - **2026-07-27** **Whole-schema integrity batch — 74 verified findings, migrations 245–256 (dev+prod, deployed 2026-07-27)** — a 16-agent adversarial review of all 118 public tables (DDL + live-prod data checks + code cross-reference; full report `.planning/db-review-2026-07-27.md`, gitignored — contains member names) found one live outage, live data corruption, and a structural FK/uniqueness hole across the core tables; all of it fixed in one pass, implemented by 13 parallel agents + 3 adversarial diff-reviewers. **The outage (EVT-01, critical)**: 4 orphaned `events_teams` rows with `events_id=NULL` made the `NOT IN (subquery)` in `public-events.js`/`ical-feed.js` never-true — **kscw.ch and every calendar subscription served ZERO club-wide events**. Fixed at both layers: 245 purges the NULL junction rows (the tombstone class was actively growing, 17→24 `hall_slots_teams` NULLs in 5 days), sets junction FK columns NOT NULL, flips `one_deselect_action='delete'` on ALL junctions (021 only did two), and adds composite pair-unique indexes; the feeds are rewritten to `NOT EXISTS` (+ cancelled events now excluded from the public feed / `STATUS:CANCELLED` in iCal, EVT-09). **RSVP identity (246)**: 251 orphaned participations purged (nothing ever cleaned RSVPs when slot-cascade hard-deletes trainings), 11 recurred duplicate pairs deduped, then partial unique indexes `participations_activity_member_uq`/`_session_uq` + `activity_type`/`status` CHECKs; all 21 hook writers + `game-auto-confirm-sweep` now end in **targetless** `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` (inert pre-migration, arbitrates on either partial index post — no named target needed); AFTER DELETE purge triggers close the orphan class forever — ⚠ named `trg_*_0_purge_polymorphic` because **Postgres fires same-event triggers alphabetically** and they must run before `trg_*_notify` or they'd eat the deletion notices the notify triggers just wrote. **games/trainings get their first FKs (247)**: 17 on games alone (kscw_team, hall, the whole duty matrix → SET NULL, history survives); live orphans repaired first (games 392/393 → deleted team 91, 4 deleted duty members on games 57/130, 28 dangling `trainings.hall_slot`, 2 rankings rows), and **DU23-2's 15 detached 2025/26 games re-attached** (sv-sync had NULLed `kscw_team` when the team went inactive — GAMES-04); sv-sync now never downgrades `kscw_team` and both syncs **preserve an app-cancelled game** (the feed silently resurrected it nightly; un-cancel now notifies via new `trg_games_notify` branch + `game_reinstated` i18n key ×5, 250). 250 also makes the derby convention structural: partial unique `(game_id, kscw_team)` — two rows per derby pass, a third is a hard error — plus `svrz_number`/`hall_events.uid` unique and `games.status` CHECK. **members (248)**: duplicate person **333/585 merged** (585 had no login/roster; sole reference — invoice 30427 — repointed; ⚠ **ClubDesk still holds both contacts, 1001031 needs manual register cleanup**), `license_nr='0'` placeholders NULLed + format CHECK (⚠ leading zeros are REAL licence numbers — '038514'), partial uniques on `license_nr`/`vm_email`/`user`, FKs `members.user`→directus_users / `requested_team`→teams, `position`/`role` json→jsonb — ⚠ the `role` ALTER required **dropping + recreating `stats_members`/`stats_club_overview` around it** (Postgres refuses ALTER TYPE on view-referenced columns; `security_invoker=true` restored, caught by the adversarial migration review as the one apply-blocker). **Dead weight**: `hall_events_halls` dropped (252 — 0 rows ever; gcal-sync's `record.hall` write targeted a column that doesn't exist = latent nightly-sync crash, deleted; virtualSlots' dead branch removed; 3 legacy setup scripts cleaned), `members_sektion_backup_20260715` dropped (253), 7 duplicate indexes dropped + `user_logs` finally indexed (249 — the audit page was seq-scanning 370k rows ×3 per view; ~221k tuples/scan measured), `user_logs.user` CASCADE→SET NULL (audit rows now outlive the actor), `team_requests.member`→CASCADE (was blocking 36 member deletions), `fines` CASCADE→RESTRICT. **Scheduling (251)**: dead columns `slots.booking/.game`, `bookings.game` dropped (⚠ review caught a live reader the audit missed), `season` varchar→integer + FK, status CHECKs, sequence rename. **Finance (254)**: camt `clubdesk_guess` now survives the ClubDesk mirror's delete+reinsert via a `match_clubdesk_id` snapshot + importer re-link step (new `link_lost` status), team-entries got the closed-fiscal-year guard, `referee_expenses` float4→numeric(10,2)+FKs, payout/invoice status CHECKs, CHF currency defaults. **absences (255)**: NULL `end_date` now trigger-normalizes to indefinite (the residual hole 236 left). **members admin form (256)**: 14 Directus field groups over all ~100 fields + 6 unregistered columns registered + 24 `COMMENT ON COLUMN` — the review's verdict on the "messy" members table was **organize, don't split** (of 14 suspect columns only `last_online_at` was dead, and it got its writer instead: auth.login now stamps it; EVT-04/07/08 also fixed — absence auto-decline now covers club-wide + invited events (was auto-CONFIRMING absent members), Zurich date derivation, per-session declines for per_day events). Gates: build ✓, lint 0 errors, test:unit 716 ✓ (~45 new), test:scripts 78 ✓; **all 12 migrations applied + effect-verified on the dev DB clone**. ⚠ **Deploy is order-critical: schema BEFORE extensions** (251/254 change columns the live endpoint code reads) — chained `npm run deploy:*` only, never standalone `ext:deploy`. - **2026-07-27** **The DU20 girls were deleted out of ClubDesk by our own cleanup cron — restored from backup, and the rule that did it corrected (scripts + prod data, no migration)** — from *"can you check in an old backup the DU20 and younger volleyball player in clubdesk?"*. **What the backups said**: the team is `VB DU1719` in both surviving copies (`~/export_all.csv`, the 19.06 ClubDesk UI export, and `/opt/clubdesk-sync/backups/clubdesk-full-20260706-1855.sql`, a `clubdesk_export` dump) and was renamed **`VB DU20`** for 2026/27 (DU23-1+2 likewise merged into `VB DU23`). The 06.07 backup holds **32 players + 2 coaches**; live ClubDesk held **2 players**. Nothing younger exists in volleyball — DU1719/DU20 is the youngest VB group in all three snapshots, and boys' `VB HU20` was untouched (23). One extra name, **Meret Rausch (2006)**, is in the 19.06 export but not 06.07 and has no ClubDesk row at all today. **What happened**: the **16.07 07:25–07:34 catch-up run** of `clubdesk-group-cleanup.sh` removed 48 chips, **29 of them `VB DU20 (Spieler*in)`** (+ Eline Geranguillet from `VB DU23`, + 18 across D1–D4/H1–H3/Legends/HU20/DU23/BB) — `group-cleanup-commit.log` names every one. It was allowed to, by two independent gaps: (a) **wiedisync's DU20 roster was never entered** — team 68 (and the inactive 31) had **0** `member_teams` rows and 31 of the 32 girls were on no team at all, so every one of them was a "stray"; and (b) the auto-remove envelope's departure test was **`wiedisync_active = false`**, which does not mean "left the club" — it means **"has never activated a wiedisync login"**, true for ~500 of 709 members and near-universal among juniors. ⚠ Since ClubDesk was the only place the roster existed, the 06.07 backup was the **only surviving copy**. **Restore (prod, user-scoped to DU20 only)**: all **29** re-assigned as `VB DU20 (Spieler*in)` via `clubdesk-scrape-groups.mjs … commit` (`tally {"assigned":29}`, log `/opt/clubdesk-sync/du20-restore.log`), then **verified read-only** by re-reading the chips out of the contact detail view with `clubdesk-remove-group.mjs … preview` against a group they are not in (`skip_not_in_group`, `chips=["VB DU20 (Spieler*in)"]` for Andreossi/Kunath/Wyss). Group back to 31 players (the 29 + Blattner and Habibian, who never lost it) + 2 coaches. **⚠ A silent-no-op bug in the add scraper, found by the 3-row preview**: `okEnabled:false` on rows 2 and 3, and the screenshot showed why — on every row **after the first**, the click that should select the grid row only restores focus (same class of bug as `setFilter`'s re-focus note), so the dialog opens with an EMPTY **`Kontakt: Pflichtfeld`** and a permanently disabled OK. A commit run would have assigned row 1 and skipped the other 28 while reporting cleanly. Worse, the stale variant would file the group under the **previous** contact. Fixed with `kontaktValue()` (anchored on the `Kontakt:` label, not a y-band) + `kontaktMatches()` (diacritic-insensitive, one-token — ClubDesk name drift is normal, a stale dialog shares no token) inside a 3× re-select loop, new status `skip_no_selection`; the retry fired on all 28 subsequent rows and every one then committed with its own name in `kontakt`. ⚠ This bug has been latent since the tool shipped 2026-07-15 (the 234/236 initial run predates it or masked it behind commit-mode's longer settle) — **any past preview-mode result showing one success then skips was this, not the data**. **Rule fix** (`clubdesk-group-cleanup.sh`): "left the club" now means the **register** says so — `clubdesk_export.status IN ('Kein Mitglied','Ehemaliges Mitglied','Verstorben')` or a non-empty `austritt` — or `members.kscw_membership_active = false`; the coach/TR "Lasse pattern" branch is unchanged, and plain active players stay untouched for the manual card. Simulated against prod before deploying: **old rule would re-cut 27 of the 29, new rule 0**; today's stray set (3) is unaffected by either. ⚠ Membership status is the only thing this may key on — never app activation. **Roster** (user: *both*): **31** `member_teams` rows inserted (team 68, season 2026/27, `guest_level 0`) — the 29 plus Blattner + Habibian so the roster matches the group exactly; that alone also removes them from the stray set. Only the two messaging triggers fire on insert (team-conversation membership) — no mail, no push, and 27 of the 29 have no login anyway. Also added **Muriel Schmid** (member 388) to `teams_coaches` for DU20 (row 113) — she holds the `VB DU20 (Trainer*in)` chip but wiedisync knew only Nadine Beilstein; coach chips are outside the cleanup's player-group scope, so this was consistency, not exposure. **Deliberately NOT done**: Eline Geranguillet (her lost chip was `VB DU23`, not this group — user's call to leave her) and the other 18 removals from the same run. ⚠ **`clubdesk_export` stays stale until the Sat 22:00 down-sync**, so until then Data Health lists those 29 as "missing their team's ClubDesk group" — do not re-run the add on that basis. ⚠ Prod only — there is no dev ClubDesk instance. Both scripts deployed to `/opt/clubdesk-sync/` (md5-verified) and committed in `3728ab09`; worklists carrying uuids deleted from the VPS afterwards. Rollback: `DELETE FROM member_teams WHERE team=68 AND season='2026/27'`, `DELETE FROM teams_coaches WHERE id=113`, and the chips come off with `clubdesk-remove-group.mjs`.

- **2026-07-27** **Transfers — show Volleymanager's side of the federation-of-origin question (frontend-only, prod)** — from *"also show what is their FoO in VM"*. Volleymanager stores NO federation of origin — `sv_vm_check.federation` is the Swiss Volley REGIONAL association (SVRZ/…) of the *current* licence club, per the 2026-07-25 backfill finding — so the closest it has is now shown verbatim in the "Licence validated" column of the actionable VB worklist: `VM: <nationality> · plays as <nationality_code>` (citizenship as a German country name, plus the licence's IOC alpha-3 PLAYING nationality; `SUI` on a foreign citizen means Swiss-equivalent *eligibility*, i.e. often that the transfer side is already settled — not Swiss citizenship). Display-only with an explanatory `title` hint; never fed back into `federation_of_origin`. Verbatim on purpose: mapping the German name / IOC code through our own country tables would let a mapping bug misreport the register being compared against. Reuses the existing deterministic `vmByMember` match (licence number → email), so exactly the vbNeeds cohort is covered — unmatched members keep showing "Not in Volleymanager". Two columns added to the `sv_vm_check` fields list; no permission change (Sport Admin already holds full CRUD on the collection). i18n ×5 (`trVmOrigin` / `trVmOriginBoth` / `trVmOriginHint` — all five locales carry the transfers block). Gate: `build` ✓, `lint` 0 errors on the file. Prod direct (user instruction); no version bump (minor admin-only addition per versioning discipline).

- **2026-07-27** **VB referees page — add and remove referees on the page itself (frontend-only, dev)** — from *"here also let me add and remove referees"*. `/admin/vb-referees` only listed members already flagged `referee_vb`; the flag itself had to be flipped in the member explorer. Now a single-select member search above the table (active members not already referees, client-side filtered so nullable `referee_vb` needs no `_null`-aware filter) sets `referee_vb=true` via the items API — Sport Admin holds full-field `members` update, so no permission change. A per-row trash button confirms (danger dialog), **deletes the member's `vb_referee_duty` rows first, then clears the flag** — in that order, because a lingering duty row would keep counting its team as covered in the coverage check, and the reverse failure mode (duties gone, flag still set) is at least visible as "referee with no assignment". ⚠ The confirm dialog warns about the set-true-only syncs: both the ClubDesk cron (feeds `referee_vb` from the SR *group*) and the VM Monday sync re-flag a member who still holds a referee licence there, so a removal only sticks once the source register is updated too. Empty-state copy updated (add via search, not "set the flag in the explorer"); i18n en+de (gsw/fr/it have no vbRef block at all — English fallback, part of the known 743-key gap). Gate: `build` ✓, `lint` 0 on the file. Dev only (`21974997`), pending prod merge approval.

- **2026-07-27** **ClubDesk backfill — the new "Gast" Y/N column filled from the wiedisync roster (one-off import, no code change)** — from *"i've added a 'Gast' Y/N column on clubdesk, can you please fill it up with the guest_level > 0 from wiedisync?"*. ClubDesk has no source for guest status: the roster lives in `member_teams.guest_level`, so the register's new checkbox could only ever be filled from here. **Definition — pure guests, not every guest row (user's call).** Season 2026/27 has **41** members with a `guest_level > 0` row, but 14 of them are ALSO a core player on another team (core on H1, guesting on H2) — those are full members, and the club's own fee logic (`guestMemberIdSet` in `clubdesk-update.js`, which drives the reduced Mitgliederbeitrag) already defines a guest as *guest somewhere AND core nowhere*. The backfill uses that same rule → **27 Ja**. Marking the other 14 as Gast would have contradicted the category they are billed under. **Scope — full reconcile:** every wiedisync-linked, non-muted member gets an explicit `Ja` or `Nein` (**707 rows**, 27 Ja / 680 Nein), not just the guests, so the column carries no ambiguity between "not a guest" and "never answered". Contacts with no wiedisync member (ehemalige, non-members) were left alone — wiedisync only asserts about people it knows. Delivered as an `[Id];Gast` CSV through the normal import wizard (`clubdesk-scrape-import.mjs`, preview → commit under the shared `.sync.lock`), NOT through `/up`: the sync-up push is drift-gated and `Gast` is in neither `CD_PUSH_HEADERS` nor `clubdesk_export`, so nothing would have flagged it as changed. Guards held — **0 "Neue Kontakte"**, so no unknown `[Id]` (which hard-aborts the whole import) and no duplicate contact; mapping dialog confirmed `Spalten-Name Gast → Feld Gast`, `Gültige Werte: Ja, Nein`, **zero unrecognised values**. Round-trip verified by re-previewing the same CSV after the commit: **707 "Unveränderte Kontakte (entsprechen dem aktuellen Stand in ClubDesk)"**. CSV + Playwright screenshots deleted from the VPS afterwards (the shots frame the contact grid → address/e-mail PII). ⚠ Prod only — there is no dev ClubDesk instance.

- **2026-07-27** **…and made it a sync instead of a snapshot — Gast joins the push + drift set (migration 244, endpoint + scripts)** — the backfill above was a one-off import, i.e. a snapshot: guest status is per-season, so it would have silently rotted at the next roster turnover with nothing to catch it. **Push side**: `'Gast'` joins `CD_PUSH_CONTACT_HEADERS`, so every UPDATE *and* CREATE row now carries it (15 contact columns / 26 create columns). New `gastCell(isGuest)` is **total** — a non-guest asserts `'Nein'` rather than sending an empty cell, which also makes the field structurally exempt from the echo-back / blank-risk machinery (there is no empty wiedisync value that could blank the register, so the guard has nothing to guard). ⚠ The flag comes from **`guestMemberIdSet`, never a bare `guest_level > 0`** — a guest on one team who is a core player on another is a full member and is billed as one by `deriveMitgliederbeitrag`, so the looser rule would have the same CSV contradict itself. `/up` now resolves `m.is_guest` **once over the whole push** (`pushMembers`) instead of only over the CREATE set, so an update row and a create row cannot be resolved against different definitions. **Drift side**: migration **244** adds `clubdesk_export.gast` (the 064 `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` is a no-op on existing DBs, so a staging column needs its own ALTER — the gap `js_id`/195 and `wiedisync_id` already hit), `import-clubdesk-csv.mjs` maps `'Gast' → gast` in `HEADER_TO_COL` + `TARGET_COLS` (66 staged columns), and `computeClubdeskDrift` compares the register's cell against the live roster using **the very same `guestMemberIdSet` + `gastCell` the push uses** — a drift verdict that could disagree with the pushed cell would re-flag the member on every refresh and never converge. A member who starts or stops being a guest now surfaces as an ordinary CONFLICT the admin flags → pushes. ⚠ **Direction is one-way by design**: there is deliberately NO write-back pass into `members` — wiedisync owns guest status outright, so letting ClubDesk's copy flow back would let a stale register overwrite the live roster. The staging column exists only to answer "what does the register currently say". ⚠ `clubdesk_export` is a raw staging table, **not** a Directus collection (no `directus_fields` row, no items-API exposure), so 244 touches no permission row and `setup-permissions.mjs` is untouched. ⚠ **Until the next sync-down stages the column, `cd.gast` is NULL for all 707** → Data Health shows one *aggregated* `gast` fills line (fills are aggregated per field exactly like `sex`; per-member rows are conflicts-only and `clubdesk-sync-status` keys its `drift` verdict on conflicts, so no member is falsely marked drifted). Running a **Sync down** after deploy clears it, since the register already holds the values. **Also fixed — the summary-count bug found during the backfill's own verification**: `countFor(/(Veränderte|Geänderte) Kontakte/i)` in `clubdesk-scrape-import.mjs` matched the substring inside **"Unveränderte Kontakte (entsprechen dem aktuellen Stand in ClubDesk)"**. ClubDesk OMITS a line whose count is zero, so an import that changes nothing has no "Veränderte" line to find, matched the "Unveränderte" one instead, and reported every unchanged row as changed — the verification run printed `veraendert=707 unveraendert=707` off a dialog whose only two lines were "Insgesamt: 707" and "Unveränderte: 707". Now `/(?<!Un)(Veränderte|Geänderte) Kontakte/i`. Harmless to the up-dispatcher's gate (`scrape_ok` only needs a numeric `total` + no `error`), but the sync-up modal renders that number at an admin as "changed contacts". ⚠ The regex lives inside a `page.evaluate` closure, so it has no unit test — verified against the five label variants in a standalone node run. `gast` added to `MEMBER_FIELD_LABELS` (the sync-up modal's change chips print whatever field name the drift flag wrote — it is a drift field, not a `members` column). Gate: `build` ✓, `lint` 0 errors, `test:unit` 681 passing (+3 new push cases), `test:scripts` 75/75. ⚠ **Deploy order is schema FIRST** (`db:migrate` → `scripts:deploy` → `ext:deploy`): the endpoint selects `cd.gast`, so shipping it ahead of 244 reproduces the 2026-06-19 `column … does not exist` 500s.

- **2026-07-27** **Prod data fix — 8 members whose federation of origin was seeded from their passport, not their licence (no code change)** — from *"Thamy had 'SVRZ' written as note to all player whose FoO is actually CH"*. Migration 239 seeded `members.federation_of_origin` from **nationality**, which is a guess, not a fact: a German passport does not mean the DVV issued the first licence. Thamy had already done the research and recorded it in `transfer_note` — **"SVRZ" (Swiss Volley Region Zürich) on exactly 8 rows, and those 8 are the entire non-empty note corpus** — but the note is free text that nothing reads, so all 8 sat in the DVV/AFG/GB worklists as members we owed an international transfer request for. 7 of the 8 hold a Swiss licence number. Set `federation_of_origin='CH'` for ids **63 Kicinski, 77 Meinen, 107 Trilby (DE)**, **123 Baldauf (DE)**, **196 Fayazi, 251 Ibrahimkhil, 398 Shinwari (AF)**, **378 Sawyer (GB)** → CH 485→493, DE 44→40, AF 6→3, GB 2→1. ⚠ **`in_vis`/`in_vis_checked_at` reset to NULL on the same rows**: the stored `false` was "not found in the *DVV/AFG/GB* index" — a claim about a register these members were never in — so keeping it would print a stale verdict under the new grouping. The monthly `vis-player-check` re-runs them against SUI (where, per 2026-07-26, ~99.7% read "not found" anyway). ⚠ **Thamy's note is deliberately kept** — it is the evidence for the change, and the note column renders in the Swiss group too. ⚠ **ClubDesk still holds the old value** (Deutschland / Afghanistan / Großbritannien): a raw-SQL write sets no `clubdesk_push_pending`, and the flag is a deliberate superadmin action (`/clubdesk-drift/flag`), so the correction reaches the register when someone runs **Data health → ClubDesk drift → flag → sync up**, not silently. **Same correction, second pass — the Polish group, minus the one member VIS actually knows.** *"all the polish people put FoO in CH"* → **"all the polish except kacper"**. PZPS held 4 members and the nationality set was identical (no PL-national grouped elsewhere), but **531 Kacper Krawczyński has `in_vis=true` and `vis_player_no=215744`** — VIS lists him as a PZPS player, and he holds no Swiss licence number, which is positive evidence his federation of origin really is Poland. The asymmetry decides it: marking a genuinely PZPS-licensed player as Swiss makes the club skip an ITC he needs (an eligibility problem), while leaving a Swiss-origin player under PZPS costs one unnecessary letter. Flagged before touching him; confirmed. **266 Kalaga, 331 Napasnikava, 416 Szelwach → CH** (all three not-found in the PZPS index and holding Swiss licence numbers 110951 / 322200 / 322202); **531 left at PL**. PL 4→1, CH 493→496. ⚠ **Unlike the SVRZ eight, these three carry no recorded evidence in `transfer_note`** — the reason is the club admin's own knowledge, so this DEVLOG line is the only provenance. Rollback: `UPDATE members SET federation_of_origin=<old>, in_vis=false, in_vis_checked_at='2026-07-26 17:40:07.894075+00' WHERE id=<id>` with old = DE for 63/77/107/123, AF for 196/251/398, GB for 378, PL for 266/331/416. Prod only — dev is re-cloned from prod nightly.

- **2026-07-26** **ClubDesk data-update email — the coded fields were rendered by the member's browser, in the member's language (endpoint + frontend, no migration)** — from a screenshot of the live email: *"Federation of Origin please written properly. then why 'Schweiz'? or 'Deutschland'? these should be in EN (and for FoO → CH flag + Swiss Volley) and pushed in de to clubdesk"*. Three visible defects, one root cause. **(1) The field-label table had no `federation_of_origin` row**, so `buildChangesTable` fell through to `labels[c.field] || c.field` and printed the raw column name at a human; added in all 5 locales (+`iban`, the same gap one field over). **(2) The two sides of a row came from different places** — `old_value` was a label rendered client-side by `federationLabel()` ("🇩🇪 DVV"), `new_value` was rebuilt server-side as a German *country* name ("Schweiz") — so one row compared a federation against a country, and `sex` read `m → männlich`. **(3) `nationalitaet` compared a full list against the German primary-only mirror** (`members.nationalitaet` is the trigger-derived name of the FIRST code, migration 223), so an unchanged `DE,CH` reported as *"Deutschland, Schweiz → Deutschland"* — an email claiming a member dropped a nationality they still hold. **Fix — the coded fields travel as CODES and are rendered TWICE server-side from the same raw value**: `pushValue()` → German, ClubDesk's picklist spelling (persisted to `clubdesk_push_changes`, shown in the sync-up modal, and what the register gets — `nationalityCell()` joins `loadCountryPushNames`'s `name_de_clubdesk`, NOT the display German, or GB would preview as "Vereinigtes Königreich" against a cell holding "Großbritannien"); `displayValue(loc)` → the READER's language, re-rendered per locale bucket inside the send loop. New `kscw-endpoints/src/federations.js` owns the display side (`federationDisplay` → "🇨🇭 Swiss Volley", `countryCodesDisplay`, `countryDisplay`, `sexDisplay`, `parseCodeList`); `federationCell`/`nationalityCell` in `clubdesk-update.js` stay the push side. ⚠ **The federation maps mirror `src/utils/federations.ts` and must be added to in both** — the picker naming a body while the email names a country is exactly the bug being fixed. Country names come from `country_codes.name_de/name_en` for de/gsw/en and CLDR for fr/it (small-ICU degrades to English, not to German). ⚠ **`fedSport` had to move above the diff** — the federation is sport-specific (FIPAV vs FIP), so the `member_teams` fetch now runs before the changes are rendered. **The picker had the same hole**: `primarySport` collapses BOTH "plays two sports" AND "has no team yet" into `'both'`, and `federationName()` returns `''` without a sport — so those members were asked "which federation licensed you?" over a list of plain country names. New `federationNames()` names both bodies ("FIPAV / FIP", "Swiss Volley / Swiss Basketball") in the picker AND the email rather than neither. Frontend `clubdeskFields` now diffs codes (`serializeCountryCodes(storedNationalityCodes())` vs the selection, raw `federation_of_origin`), which is also what kills defect (3) at the source; `federationLabel()`/`formatCountryCodes`/`countryLabel` dropped from the form. The sync-up modal's change chips were showing the same raw column names — `MEMBER_FIELD_LABELS` moved out of `ExplorerMemberFields.tsx` into `memberFieldLabels.ts` (a component file exporting a constant is a `react-refresh/only-export-components` **error**, not a warning) and both surfaces read it. Separately, **fr/it locale cleanup**: 53 values were byte-identical German (`male: 'Männlich'`, `mr: 'Herr'`, `plz: 'PLZ'`, `invalidPlz`, `active: 'Aktivmitglied'`, …) — worse than absent, since a MISSING key falls back to **English** (`fallbackLng: {gsw:['de','en'], default:['en']}`) while a present German one renders German. Brand names / acronyms / true cognates (Volleyball, PDF, `#`, Saldo, Adresse-fr, Saison-fr) deliberately left. ⚠ **The bigger fr/it gap is untouched: 743 keys per locale are absent entirely** (admin 354, finance 252, + 4 whole missing namespace files) and render in English. Gate: `build` ✓, `lint` 0 errors, `test:unit` 56 files / 679 passing incl. 14 new `federations.test.js` cases + 2 push-shape cases. ⚠ **Needs `ext:deploy` to take effect** — the email fix is endpoint code; the frontend half ships on push.

- **2026-07-26** **Transfers page — Swiss Volley is a federation too, and guests are not licensed (migration 243, frontend + `vis-player-check.mjs`)** — from *"Switzerland (Swiss Volley) is also a federation with their players, so use it to group the Swiss players"* + *"guests will not have a licence so take them out"*. **(1) The `settled` bucket was doing two unrelated jobs and is now split.** `bucketOf` returned `settled` for both `'NONE'` and `'CH'`, which reduced ~483 members (migration 239 seeded CH from nationality) to a single tally with no rows, no contact and no VIS state. But **Swiss Volley IS a federation in VIS with its own player index** (`vis_federations` vis_no **189 / SUI** — it was already in the directory, seeded by 241, just never used), so the same grouping the DVV/FIPAV cohorts get is available for our own players. New `swiss` bucket → its own section under **🇨🇭 Swiss Volley**, with the VIS column and the Swiss Volley contact. `settled` now means `'NONE'` only — no federation exists to group those under. ⚠ **The "needs no transfer" pill deliberately still counts BOTH** (`active.settled + active.swiss.length`): the claim is "these many members need no transfer" and excluding the Swiss because they also appear below would make it false. ⚠ **No transfer status control and no prepared letter in the Swiss group** — the letter asks a federation to grant an ITC *to Swiss Volley*, so addressed to Swiss Volley it would ask them to transfer players they already licensed; the contact row stays, because "who do we write to about a Swiss player missing from VIS" is still a real question. ⚠ **Volleyball tab only**, same reason the letter is: VIS/`vis_federations` are FIVB's, so on the BB tab this would file Swiss basketballers under a volleyball federation behind an "In VIS" column that cannot speak to them — they stay in the tally there. ⚠ **Collapsed, and the body is UNMOUNTED while collapsed** (`open={swissOpen}` controlled + `{swissOpen && body}`, not bare `<details>`): a native `<details>` only *hides* its content, so ~483 rows × a text input would have been mounted on every page load for a table nobody opened. The header still carries the count + the in-VIS/not-found/not-checked split, so the summary reads without expanding. `renderTable(groups, withStatus: boolean)` became `renderTable(groups, mode: 'needs'|'clarify'|'swiss')` — three cohorts no longer fit a boolean. **(2) Guests are out, everywhere.** A `member_teams` row with `guest_level > 0` is somebody who trains without being licensed by the club — no licence, so no eligibility to establish, nothing to find in VIS and no transfer anyone owes; they were on a worklist that could never be worked. `sportsByMember` now builds from **non-guest** junction rows only (the same rule `buildScorerTeams` already applies to duty eligibility), so a guest-only member has no sport and drops off both tabs — while a member who is a full player on **any** team still counts as one. ⚠ Guest-only members would otherwise have been absorbed into the existing `trHiddenNoTeam` line and reported as a *data gap*; the hidden count is now split (`hidden.noTeam` / `hidden.guestOnly` + `trHiddenGuests`) because "on no team" is something to fix and "guest only" is the correct answer. **(3) `vis-player-check.mjs` follows the page**: `CH: 'SUI'` added to `ISO2FIVB`, the `NOT IN ('CH','NONE')` filter narrowed to `<> 'NONE'`, and an `EXISTS … coalesce(guest_level,0)=0` guard added — otherwise the Swiss group would render "Not checked" on every row forever and guests would keep being looked up. ⚠ **This reverses migration 240's stated premise** ("CH-origin members are skipped"), which is why **243** exists at all: it is a **COMMENT-only** migration correcting `members.in_vis`'s column comment (240/241 are applied and sha-pinned, so they cannot be edited). `in_vis` semantics are unchanged for the Swiss — a `false` there **blocks nothing** (no international transfer applies) and reads "no player of that name in Swiss Volley's index", which is why the badge hints are swapped (`trSwissInVisYesHint`/`trSwissInVisNoHint`) and the "Open in VIS" CTA is dropped in that group. ⚠ Swiss Volley's is the largest roster the job pulls, but it is one fetch per run, matched locally like every other federation. i18n ×5 locales (`trSwissTitle/Description`, `trSwissInVisYesHint/NoHint`, `trHiddenGuests`). Gate: `build` ✓, `lint` 0 errors on the file, `test:scripts` 75/75, `test:unit` 652/653 (the one failure is `useTour` timing out at 5.00s under full-suite load — passes standalone in 4.2s on this branch AND on the base commit; the two erroring extension test files want `knex`/`fast-xml-parser`, which are not installed in this clone). **Deployed 2026-07-26, dev+prod**: 243 applied on both (prod 246/0 pending, dev 245→246), `db:baseline:prod` regenerated `SCHEMA.sql` (comment-only diff, `f27272b3`), `scripts:deploy` shipped the new `vis-player-check.mjs` to both bind-mounts; the frontend deployed itself on push. `db:setup-perms:prod` was **skipped deliberately** — 243 touches no permission row, so the declarative script would reproduce what is already live and the clear-then-recreate window is not worth spending on prod (which also aborted `db:smoke:prod`, and prod has no member smoke token anyway). ⚠ **The first live prod run reveals VIS's SUI index is not Swiss Volley's licence register**: it returns **1093 players** total — nowhere near Swiss Volley's domestic base — and of 318 checked Swiss members exactly **1** matched (Baumgartner, Hanna — VIS #241904), against 3 in-VIS overall out of 414 checked. So the Swiss group's "In VIS" column reads "Not found" for ~99.7% of rows. That is *semantically* correct and blocks nothing (no international transfer applies to a Swiss player), but the column carries no signal there and is worth reconsidering. The remaining 166 unchecked CH rows are the guest-only / no-team members the new exclusion correctly skips. ⚠ **No pixel verification.**

- **2026-07-25** **Transfers page — one letter per federation, team-scoped rows, table de-bloated (frontend only, dev+prod, folded into v1.47.0)** — from *"if no sport/no team: no show in the transfers"* + *"have a consolidate email per federation"*. **(1) `inSport` now requires a real `member_teams` row.** It used to return true for a member with NO team so nothing could hide, surfacing them under BOTH sports — but the register carries enough team-less people (ehemalige, passive, parents) that they buried the cohort the page exists for: on prod **31 of 130** "needs a transfer" members are on no team (DVV group 24 → 16). ⚠ Dropping rows from a compliance worklist silently is the one unacceptable outcome, so the count is stated in the page header (`trHiddenNoTeam`) — add a team and they return. There is no `clarify` cohort on prod at all (0 rows), so that half of the page is currently dead weight. **(2) Consolidated request per federation.** `visRequestText` went from one member to `TransferMember[]`, addressed once per federation group to everyone with `in_vis !== true` — not-found AND never-checked together, because both need the identical thing and two letters would ask the same people the same question twice. ⚠ **Volleyball tab ONLY**: `vis_federations` is FIVB's directory, so on the BB tab this would write to a national *volleyball* federation about a basketball player; the contact row still renders (a lead, per `trVisBasketballHint`), the letter does not. ⚠ **Body + subject are module-level English literals outside the component — they have no access to `t()`**, so the letter is structurally incapable of picking up the admin's UI locale (the exports-are-always-English rule; the recipient is a foreign federation and FIVB's working language is English). ⚠ **`mailto:` length guard**: the 16-name DE letter encodes to **2239 chars**, past the ~2048 Windows hands a mail client, and some clients TRUNCATE silently — a letter missing its last players that *looks* complete. Over `MAILTO_MAX=1800` the body is dropped from the href (recipient + subject only) and the hint tells the admin to paste; 5-name groups still prefill. ⚠ `prettyFederationName` title-cases VIS's ALL-CAPS names for the letter, with **no "short token = acronym" rule** — the obvious heuristic, and wrong for this data: all 69 rows hold long-form names only, so `VOLLEYBALL NEW ZEALAND INC.` / `SRI LANKA …` would emit "NEW"/"SRI"; connectors (DE/OF/E/DEL) lowercase mid-string, genuine acronyms go in `FED_KEEP_UPPER` by name. **(3) Table de-bloated.** The per-row VIS cell carried the federation name + email + copy + a collapsible letter — **identical on every row of a group**, ~120px of boilerplate that pushed the note field off screen (the user's screenshot showed ~300px rows). All of it moved to a per-group `federationBar`, which is also the only place a consolidated ask can exist; the row keeps badge + checked-date + `#player_no` + Open-in-VIS. Columns 9 → 7 (licence nr + category merged; birthdate into the member cell, where it belongs with the identity a federation matches on), and the three stacked grey banners above the tables merged into one. Group headers gained a per-federation VIS split so an admin sees which groups still need a letter without opening any. **Federation contact coverage verified on prod: 69/69 rows have an email, incl. all 30 ISOs our members' origins point to** — nothing to look up. i18n ×5 locales (`trColLicence`, `trHiddenNoTeam`, `trVisMoreAddresses`, `trBulkRequestTitle/Compose/TooLong`; retired `trNoTeam`, `trRequestTitle`, `trVisMultipleAddresses`). Gate: `build` ✓, `lint` 0 errors. ⚠ **No pixel verification** — dev's member app is dead on the keyless-license issue and the local env/token read was classifier-blocked, so the visual check is the CF Pages preview. No schema or extension change; CF Pages auto-deploys both branches.

- **2026-07-25** **Hallenfinder — free City of Zürich sport halls for the season (migration 239, dev+prod, v1.48.0)** — from *"get a plan of ALL Halle of Zürich, and when there's free space for the whole winter (Sept–March)"*.
