Schulferien reuse the existing `ssd_schulferien` open-data CSV (same source as `schulferien-sync`). **Migration 239** adds PRIVATE `city_halls` + `city_hall_availability` (grant `supabase_admin` only; read exclusively via the role-gated endpoint — the `sync_runs`/045 pattern → **no `setup-permissions.mjs` change**, no `directus_collections` registration). `GET /kscw/hallenfinder/search` derives free-every-non-holiday-week for any weekday/start/duration/district/type from the cached per-date jsonb (`requireAuth`; public non-sensitive data, nav gates the roles). Nightly refresh via `hallenfinder-sync.sh` (clubdesk-style container→psql) + root crontab; manual `npm run hallenfinder:sync:dev|prod`. Frontend `src/modules/hallenfinder` (page+hook+route, nav in both builders, EN/DE i18n). ⚠ **Migration collided at 236** with origin/dev's `236-absences` (dev advanced to v1.47.0 mid-build) → renumbered **239** + rebased onto origin/dev. ⚠ `deploy-extensions.sh` ships **origin/dev, not the worktree** — the endpoint only lands after push. Gate: `build` ✓, `lint` ✓, `test:scripts` 75/75 (parser fixture + Schulferien range tests). ⚠ Nav link is **admin-gated** (beside Hall slots) while route+endpoint allow **admin/coach/leader** — v1 simplification. dev+prod.
- **2026-07-25** **Indefinite absences were invisible to auto-sign-out — end_date is now a hard invariant (migration 233, dev+prod pending deploy)** — reported: *"D4 Celina Paulsson has an indefinite absence but on the training of 17.08 she is in"*, framed as *"absences we did 'not blocking' should still block participation"*. **The `blocking` flag is a red herring** — it only gates the game-scheduling availability calc (`game-scheduling.js`, `AND a.blocking IS NOT FALSE`) and a few display filters; it is referenced NOWHERE in the auto-decline path. The real bug: **`absences.end_date` is NULLABLE and every coverage evaluation keys purely on it** (`a.end_date::date >= <activity date>`) — `autoDeclineForAbsence` (bails at `if (!startDate || !endDate) return`), `applyTrainingAutoRSVP` / `games.items.create` / `events.items.create`, `reEvalActivityAutoDeclines`, the `participations.items.create` filter, and the frontend `absenceCoversActivity()` overlay — while **none consults the `indefinite` boolean**. The app forms paper over this by writing the sentinel `end_date='2099-12-31'` when *indefinite* is ticked, but any row created another way — **Directus admin UI (staff tick indefinite, leave end_date blank), raw items API, older/imported data** — lands with `end_date=NULL`, for which `NULL >= date` is never true → the member is invisible to auto-decline, and worse, when a fresh training generates inside the window `applyTrainingAutoRSVP` AUTO-CONFIRMS them (exactly Celina/17.08). **Fix keeps the existing end_date-keyed design and enforces its invariant** rather than threading `indefinite` through a dozen queries: **233** adds a `BEFORE INSERT/UPDATE` trigger `trg_absences_normalize_indefinite` (`indefinite IS TRUE ⇒ end_date := '2099-12-31'`, catches admin UI + API + imports + hooks — the 001-triggers pattern), backfills existing indefinite rows to the sentinel, then re-runs the migration-038 hard-override so participations wrongly left confirmed/tentative/waitlisted flip to declined (038's marker-preserving trigger keeps `auto_declined_by`; already-declined rows skipped → idempotent). Frontend `absenceCoversActivity` hardened to treat `indefinite` as open-ended and null-safe on `end_date`. ⚠ **This also makes indefinite *blocking* absences correctly block scheduling** (they silently didn't when end_date was NULL) — correct, and non-blocking indefinite absences (the injury/maternity case) still don't touch scheduling. ⚠ Deploy is **schema-first** (`db:deploy:*` before `ext:deploy:*`) though no extension code changed; the migration is the load-bearing part and the participation backfill runs inside it. Gate: `npm run build` ✓, `eslint` on touched file 0 errors, `test:unit` 130/130 in `src/utils`. ⚠ Could not verify against live DB from this session (no API/SSH creds in the clone) — root cause established from code; the backfill + trigger make it self-correcting on deploy.
- **2026-07-25** **Transfers page + FIVB VIS read + a fourth mailbox (migrations 234–238, dev+prod, v1.47.0)** — from *"add a Transfers page where i can see the potential transfers and deal with them"*. **`federation_of_origin` semantics were corrected TWICE and the second one mattered.** 223 documented it as "last licensed with"; 227 fixed that to "first"; then the user cited Swiss Volley / FIVB Sports Regulations verbatim — *"the association that obtained a license from the person **at the age of 14**"*. ⚠ That is a genuinely different question for anyone who moved country as a child (Italy at 8, Swiss Volley at 14 → origin **Switzerland**, not Italy), and it is **exactly Swiss Volley's transfer trigger** — "licensed abroad at 14, egal ob der Spieler seit längerem in der Schweiz wohnt, nur Amateur ist, keinen Vertrag hat", at every level incl. RL/JL. Asking the wrong question would have built the wrong worklist. All 5 locales + the public form reworded. **The 'NONE' option was reworded too, and that collapsed a distinction I had defended**: 234 gave `transfer_status` a `'not_needed'` value arguing the member's answer and the club's conclusion were different statements — true only while "None" read "never licensed before", under which an Italian who played only **CSI/UISP/PGS** (CONI *promotion bodies*, NOT FIVB/FIBA members — no licence exists to transfer) might reasonably answer "Italy". Once the option said *"never licensed with a national federation"* they answer NONE, NONE means "nothing to chase", and **235** narrowed the enum to `NULL|pending|done` with "needs no transfer" DERIVED (`NONE` or `CH`), never stored. **234** adds `transfer_status`/`transfer_done_at`/`transfer_done_by_name`/`transfer_note`. **The page** (`/admin/transfers`, nav in BOTH `useNavItems` + `MoreSheet`) tabs by sport, groups by federation of origin, and separates *actionable* (foreign FoO) from *to clarify* (**111** members with a non-Swiss nationality and no answer — nationality is a hint about **whom to ask**, never the trigger). ⚠ Gated **AdminRoute, not SuperAdminRoute** like its Data-Health neighbour: this is per-sport casework for the sport TK, whom SuperAdminRoute excludes — and AdminRoute is exactly the set already holding `members` read+update, so gate and grant line up. ⚠ The four columns are staff-only and rather than trusting a comment there is now a **`MEMBER_STAFF_ONLY_FIELDS` guard that THROWS at deploy** if any leaks into the member-visible/editable lists — `MEMBER_OWN_READABLE` and `LEADER_TEAM_MEMBER_FIELDS` both derive from those, so one careless append would hand them to every member and coach at once, and `transfer_status` in `MEMBER_EDITABLE_FIELDS` would let a member mark their **own** transfer done. **FIVB VIS (237) — the research corrected my premise.** Transfers are NOT federation-only: FIVB's own procedure manual makes the **receiving club** a party, and Swiss Volley issues club logins from `transfer@volleyball.ch`. ⚠⚠ But the VIS UI performs `Sign`/`Confirm`/`Release`/`CancelVolleyTransfer` — a stray click alters a real player's eligibility in a federation system, far worse than Basketplan's `Gedruckt` stamp — so the recon ran with an **intent-based guard** (allow only `Get*`/`Check*`/`Export*`/`List*` parsed from the POST body, fail closed on anything unrecognised) and performed **exactly one click in its life: the login button**. Finding: the app is **not** an SPA to scrape — it is a thin client over `POST proxy.app.fivb.com/proxy`, XML in / **clean JSON** out, so `vis-transfer-sync.mjs` replays the request with plain `fetch`, **no browser**. ⚠ `accept: application/json` **and** `origin` are REQUIRED — without them the proxy answers **406 InvalidRequestFormat** on a byte-identical body. Request type is a hardcoded constant asserted against the read-verb allowlist at module load; no code path builds it from input. `vis_transfers` is deliberately separate from `members.transfer_status` — one is what FIVB says, the other what the club believes, and conflating them would let a stale toggle mask an incomplete transfer. **It paid for itself immediately (238)**: transfer #3015 (Teixeira, ended 100%) came FROM federation **29 = BRA** (verified via `GetFederationList`, cross-checked against 189 = SUI), and that member had **no** federation of origin — now `BR`, derived from the register rather than asked for; his nationality independently reads BR. ⚠ FIVB uses IOC-style 3-letter codes (BRA/SUI), NOT ISO alpha-2 — only observed codes are mapped, unmapped fills nothing. **Mailbox (4th account)**: `vis_transfers@mail.kscw.ch` on `scheduling_emails`, because *"jede Handlung am internationalen Transfer löst jeweils ein Status-Mail aus"* and the ITC arrives by mail — the one signal that survives VIS changing its app, which Swiss Volley warns happens **yearly**. ⚠ `mail.kscw.ch` is a different domain from the other three boxes but the **same Migadu tenant** (MX aspmx1/aspmx2), so `SCHEDULING_IMAP_HOST` is unchanged. Gated to admin/superuser **+ the two sport admins**, deliberately NOT `is_spielplaner` (scheduling a fixture ≠ international eligibility). IMAP verified; INBOX empty until VIS's notification address is pointed at it (**user action**). Both containers **recreated**, not restarted — env changes need it — `.env.bak-20260725` on both; prod `license.source=env` survived. ⚠⚠ **Two process failures worth more than the features.** (1) `git checkout prod` failed on a **stale worktree** from another session, the chained shell command **kept going**, and `ext:deploy:prod` shipped the **PREVIOUS ref while printing success** — prod was not broken, it simply lacked the fix. Recovered by cherry-picking onto a **detached** worktree. **Chain deploy steps with `&&`, and never trust a deploy that did not print the ref you expected.** (2) A later merge silently landed on the user's `claude/d4-…` feature branch instead of `dev`, so `git push origin dev` correctly reported "up-to-date" while the work sat unpushed — **verify `git branch --show-current` before every push in this shared tree**. Also: the user's absences migration collided at **233** with `233-add-cote-divoire.sql` (already on prod) → renumbered to **236**, dev ledger row renamed alongside (same sha) so it did not re-run; the runner's **tamper detection then caught a comment-only edit made after the sha was recorded** — proven comment-only by stripping comments and re-hashing before repairing the ledger. Migration numbering must come off the branch that is actually deployed.

- **2026-07-25** **Basketplan person scrape — nationality 397→613 on prod, and two live bugs it flushed out (migrations 230 + 231, dev+prod)** — from *"for the basketplan: use all to fill empty information in Wiedisync"*. Basketplan is the **issuing authority** for Swiss Basketball licences and the only register we can reach carrying nationality on ~**100%** of licensed people. ⚠ `bp-sync.js` could NOT be extended: it reads the **public** `xmlView=true` games/rankings API, while person data needs an authenticated session — and the login **hashes the password client-side** into a hidden `j_password` + fingerprints the device, so curl/fetch cannot authenticate. Hence Playwright and a separate script (`basketplan-scrape-people.mjs`), following the ClubDesk shape: scrape → staging → **fill-only**, never a direct write to `members`. ⚠⚠ **NAVIGATION IS STRICT-ALLOWLIST AS A SAFETY REQUIREMENT, not style**: the licence list and person pages carry plain `<a href>` links that **MUTATE or DESTROY** club data — `hideLicenceFromClub.do`, `deleteLogo.do` — and the list's own form (`printLicences.do`) **stamps a `Gedruckt` timestamp on every licence it renders**. A generic crawler that follows links would quietly corrupt the club's licence register. The script never clicks a link or submits a form; every URL is asserted against two patterns first (guard unit-checked 6/6, incl. refusing both destructive endpoints and an off-host lookalike). **Result on prod: nationality 397 → 613 (+216), still-missing 312 → 96; OTR1 +49, OTR2 +36, OTN2 +8, OTN1 0.** 256 people scraped in ~6.5 min, 240 matched to members. **The OTN question was settled empirically, not inferred: all 6 pre-existing coarse `otn_bb` holders resolved to OTN 2 and NONE to OTN 1** — proof that migration 228's refusal to rename `otn_bb`→`otn1_bb` was right; the rename would have mislabelled every one of them. ⚠ **Nation ids, not labels.** Basketplan's picklist is 297 options whose value is an internal numeric id and whose label is a **FRENCH** country name — *hardcoded*, byte-identical under `locale=de/it/fr` — so neither `COUNTRY_NAME_TO_CODE` (German+English) nor `Intl` at runtime can resolve it; the numeric id is the only stable key and is mapped ONCE in **230**. 116 of 296 options were deliberately left `iso=NULL, ambiguous=true` (93 legacy abbreviation entries — `D`, `ALL`, `BRD`, `A`, `MAC`, `YUG`, `CH1`, `Suisse pour FSBA` — that coexist as SEPARATE options beside the proper names and are *still in live use*, plus territories outside our 196-country list). **231 fixes forward after the real scrape proved the flagging too broad**: the only two members actually blocked were `République tchèque` and `République islamique d'Iran` — not ambiguous at all, just FIBA-style French names CLDR doesn't emit. Also resolved `D`/`ALL`/`BRD`→DE and `A`→AT (one member carries `Suisse + D`). ⚠ **`MAC` stays flagged forever** — Macedonia or Macao, and a real member (Scalese) carries it as `nation_2`; a guess there puts a wrong nationality on a real person. ⚠ **Our OWN country list is missing Côte d'Ivoire entirely** (196 entries, no `CI`) — a gap in the kscw-website source list, not a Basketplan quirk; nobody is affected today, out of scope. **Deliberately NOT staged, so it cannot leak**: address (Basketplan's are frequently WORSE — `Hagenbuchrain` vs our correct `Hagenbuchrain 38`), birthdate (it holds a corrupt `0996`), AHV + IBAN (sparse, high-sensitivity, and **visible for minors** on those pages). Officials are **set-true only**: Basketplan stores acquisition DATES, and absence is not evidence of revocation (a lapsed licence keeps its date). Verified on prod: **0** CHECK violations, **0** rows missing the trigger-derived German name, 25 dual nationals, **0** coarse `otn_bb` holders without a level. **Two live bugs found while investigating a reported sync-up failure.** The failure itself was NOT one: the dispatcher shows the 16:31 and 16:32 runs both `committed:true`, and `Failed to fetch (directus.kscw.ch)` is a browser-level network error with no matching server-side request — a transient mobile drop, retried successfully. But the same log window held **`POST /clubdesk-update → 400 "No editable fields to update"`** for a member whose only change was `federation_of_origin`: the endpoint's `EDITABLE` whitelist never contained it, so the member saw an **error toast on a save that had actually succeeded**. ⚠ **Nationality had the identical bug silently since before 2026-07-25** — it was in `ProfileEditForm`'s diff but never in the whitelist, so a nationality-only edit always 400'd. Both added (with the ISO code rendered as a country name for the admin email) and a comment now ties the set to `clubdeskFields`. Second: the **sync-up modal overflowed the viewport on mobile** — Changes is a third column and a single IBAN is wider than it; it now collapses under the name as a per-member card below `sm` with `break-all` values, desktop unchanged, header and body cell hidden together so column counts stay balanced. ⚠⚠ **DEPLOY HAZARD, cost an incorrect prod deploy**: `git checkout prod` failed with *"already used by worktree"* (a stale worktree from another session held the branch), the chained shell command **kept going**, and `ext:deploy:prod` cheerfully shipped the PREVIOUS ref while reporting success — prod was not broken, it simply lacked the fix. Worse, the obvious recovery would have compounded it: `dev` by then also carried the user's **migration 232 Freibrief work, applied on dev ONLY**, so a straight merge would have pushed that endpoint code to prod ahead of its column — the exact 2026-06-19 `column … does not exist` pattern. Recovered by cherry-picking only the intended commit onto `origin/prod` via a **detached** worktree (avoids the branch-checkout conflict entirely) after asserting 232 was absent from the diff. **Lesson: chain deploy steps with `&&`, and never trust a deploy that did not print the ref you expected.**

- **2026-07-25** **Basketball OTN gains levels 1 + 2, end to end (migrations 228 + 229, dev+prod)** — from *"maybe add OTN 2 so that it is the same"*, then *"I added OTN1 and OTN2 in clubdesk"* + *"do same in Wiedisync"*. Migration 067 gave OTR two levels and OTN one **purely because ClubDesk's `Offiziellen Lizenz` picklist only ever emitted `VB SC / OTR1 / OTR2 / OTN`** — not because the licence has one level. Basketplan, the issuing authority, has always held `OTN 1 seit dem` (`nationalTableReferee1`) and `OTN 2 seit dem` (`nationalTableReferee2`) as separate dated fields, confirmed by an authenticated read of `findPersonById.do`. ⚠⚠ **NOT done as a rename, and the near-miss is the lesson.** The obvious move — rename `otn_bb`→`otn1_bb`, add `otn2_bb` — would have **mislabelled real people**: in an 86-person Basketplan sample **NOBODY held OTN 1 and two held OTN 2** (Moser Rachel, Pfammatter Eleni), and *both are among our six `otn_bb=true` members*. So today's coarse flag almost certainly means **OTN 2**, and the rename would have asserted the exact opposite. Both levels were added as NEW columns and left false until the issuing register fills them; nothing was guessed from a name. **228** adds `otn1_bb`/`otn2_bb` (+`directus_fields`, +both `setup-permissions.mjs` member lists) and re-documents `otn_bb` as the coarse *"holds some OTN"* flag. **229 is the load-bearing one**: `clubdesk_offliz_to_dx` (migration 066) knew only `'OTN'`, so an OTN1/OTN2 cell would have fallen through its `ELSE` to NULL and those members would have read as **UNLICENSED to every consumer of the basketball view the moment the user assigned the new options** — a bug armed by a change made in the ClubDesk UI, with nothing in the repo to notice it. Matching is deliberately tolerant of spacing and case (`'OTN 1'` as well as `'OTN1'`, verified: `OTN 1→otn1_bb`, `otn2→otn2_bb`, `Keine→NULL`) because those labels are hand-typed in ClubDesk and one character of drift silently routes a real licence to NULL. **New ClubDesk→wiedisync set-true mirror for OTR1/OTR2/OTN1/OTN2** in `import-clubdesk-csv.mjs`: there was none, on 067's reasoning that *"wiedisync owns those booleans and ClubDesk derives its string from them"* — true only while wiedisync was the sole place a BB licence could be granted, which it no longer is. ⚠ **SET-TRUE ONLY**, and not as a style preference: ClubDesk holds **one** value per contact while a member can hold several licences, so absence from the cell is absence of evidence — a clearing rule would delete a real licence from anyone holding two, the identical trap that let the VM cron wipe `scorer_vb`/`referee_vb` on 2026-07-13. **Everything else is strictly ADDITIVE** — every condition reading `otn_bb` became `otn_bb || otn1_bb || otn2_bb`, never a swap, because with both new columns false a replacement would have instantly dropped those 6 people out of basketball scorer assignment. **Proven against prod data rather than asserted: 46 members are BB 24s-eligible under the OLD rule and 46 under the NEW one** — identical, so no regression was possible. `deriveOffiziellenLizenz` keeps its bare `otn_bb` branch **LAST** for the same reason: dropping it would **blank those 6 members' ClubDesk cell** on the next push, strictly worse than sending the imprecise value we have always sent; it retires itself once the levels are filled. Wired through types, explorer grid/fields/filters, `RoleChipPicker`, announcement qualification targeting (`qual:otn1_bb`/`qual:otn2_bb`), profile licence labels, and scorer eligibility (delegation modal, `scorer-claim.js`, `AssignmentAlgorithmBb`) — ⚠ **including every `fields:` fetch array**, since an unfetched column arrives `undefined`, reads as false and **silently denies a claim with no error** (`scorer-claim.js`'s `.first(...)` is the sharp edge: a `lic` entry not selected there denies the claim). `event-notify.js`'s role list doubles as the **SQL-injection allowlist** for a dynamic `.where(role, true)` column name, so omitting the new names would have silently dropped an `otn1_bb` invite. `mapLicences` tests the levels BEFORE the bare branch (`!otn1 && !otn2 && includes('otn')`) or `"OTN 1"` would be swallowed into the coarse flag. i18n ×5 locales ×3 namespaces; `otn_bb`'s own labels relabelled *"(any level)"* since three OTN chips now render side by side. Gate: build ✓, lint 0 errors, `test:unit` 655, `test:scripts` 68/68. ⚠ Deploy-order hazard flagged and currently **unreachable**: new frontend + old backend would show the claim button and 403 — but `otn1_bb`/`otn2_bb` are 0/709, so no one can hit it; extensions were deployed alongside anyway. ⚠ `ClubStatsPage` counts only OTR1/OTR2 from a backend SQL view — OTN was never counted there and still isn't (separate view change if wanted).

- **2026-07-25** **Nationality backfilled from Volleymanager; Federation of Origin has NO machine source (migrations 225 + 226, dev+prod)** — follow-up ask: *"use Volleymanager and basketplan if possible to check for the federation of origin. if empty, use nationality (maybe also put the nationality in both Wiedisync and clubdesk based on Volleymanager and basketplan)"*. **The FoO half was dropped after auditing the data, and that is the finding.** ⚠ **`sv_vm_check.federation` looks like an exact match and is not one** — it holds **SVRZ 254 / SVRA 1 / SVRGSGL 1**, i.e. Swiss Volley *regional* associations of the member's **current** licence club, a different concept from a national federation of origin; writing it into `federation_of_origin` would have filled the column with confidently wrong data in the one field whose entire purpose is deciding whether to chase a transfer certificate. A schema-wide regex sweep (`federation|verband|freigab|transfer|origin|former|previous|herkunft|clearance|freibrief`, then widened to `vorher|letzt|ehemal|wechsel|erstlizenz|old_club|last_club`) found **nothing else** — and **Basketplan holds no person data at all**: `bp-sync.js` scrapes only the games/rankings XML into `games`/`rankings`, and the 2026-07-14 licence work was two one-shot migrations (208/210) with hardcoded VALUES lists covering `license_nr`/`licence_category`/`birthdate` only (0 hits for `nationalit`). ⚠ **Also rejected: deriving FoO from nationality** (the literal ask). FoO is the federation you were last licensed *with*, not citizenship — an Italian citizen raised in Zürich who has only ever played here has FoO = Swiss Volley or none, **not** Italy — so the derivation would send the club after clearances from FIPAV/DVV that were never issued. Worse than NULL, because NULL is honest. User confirmed: *"foo is currently empty, people will change if need to"* → stays member-declared (profile + intake form both ask; the ClubDesk down-sync fill-only path already exists). Only usable signal for *whom to ask* is `sv_vm_check.is_foreigner=true` (**16** members). **225 — the nationality half, which VM genuinely helps with but only marginally.** 223/224 left **327 of 709** members with no nationality because their only source was `clubdesk_export.nationalitaet` (507 rows) which 223's backfill had already consumed; `sv_vm_check.nationality` is a **second, independent citizenship field** (German country name, **256/256** populated) that nothing had ever read into `members`. It closes **15** of the 327 (382→**397**); the other **312** hold no licence in any mirrored register and can only be asked — recorded honestly rather than presented as coverage. All 13 distinct VM values resolve through migration 224's `country_name_aliases` (**256/256 rows, 0 gaps**), so there is no silent-skip class. ⚠⚠ **`sv_vm_check` has TWO nationality columns and only one is citizenship**: `nationality` = `person.nationality.countryName` (citizenship) vs `nationality_code` = `player.nationality.iocCodeOrIsoAlpha3`, the **licence's *playing* nationality** (Swiss-equivalent status for game eligibility). They disagree BY DESIGN on prod — `Deutschland|SUI` ×5, `Italien|SUI`, `Polen|SUI` ×2, `Kolumbien|SUI` ×2 — so feeding `nationality_code` into `nationalitaet_codes` would have **relabelled foreign citizens as Swiss**. Uses `nationality` only. Match is the same **5-step cascade** as `scorer-vm-check.js:46-77` / `vm-sync-check.mjs:636-655` (licence nr → email → vm_email → name+dob → name-only, colliding name keys dropped, earlier steps win) — 237 of 709 members match a VM row. **226 — the 19 disagreements 225 skipped, merged as APPENDS.** Where both registers held a nationality and disagreed, the pattern was unmistakable: **17 of 19 are "ClubDesk holds the non-Swiss passport, VM says Schweiz"** (DE/IT/FR/AT/PL/RU/BG/RS/GB vs Schweiz) and **2 are the reverse** (Dommen `CH` vs Schweden, Nenadic `CH` vs Serbien) — i.e. dual nationals, exactly what 223's multi-value column was built for (⚠ **0 of 709 rows held more than one code before this**). VM's code is **appended with the existing code kept FIRST**, and that ordering is the safety property rather than a stylistic choice: `members.nationalitaet` is trigger-derived from the **first** code only, so all 19 keep pushing ClubDesk the identical value and **226 writes nothing to the legal register** — verified on prod (`DE,CH`→Deutschland, `GB,CH`→**Großbritannien**, `CH,SE`→Schweiz). Deliberately a one-time reviewed merge and **NOT** a sync rule: `vm-sync-check.mjs` must not rewrite citizenship on a weekly cron. **226 part 2 queues the 15 fills for ClubDesk** by writing exactly what `POST /clubdesk-drift/flag` writes (`clubdesk_push_pending` + a `clubdesk_push_changes` fill entry with `old_value:null`, jsonb, shape matched against a live pending row) — pending **7→23**. ⚠ **It does NOT push**: the admin approval in the sync-up modal is untouched, and only members whose ClubDesk contact genuinely *lacks* a nationality are queued, so it can never queue an overwrite of a value ClubDesk owns. **Both migrations idempotent, proven rather than asserted** — the 226 body was re-run against dev: multi-nationality rows stayed 19, **0** triple-coded rows (`^..,..,`), pending stayed 23, **0** duplicated `nationalitaet` payload entries; 0 CHECK violations on prod. ⚠ Raw-SQL data migrations write **no `directus_revisions`/`user_logs` row** — same blind spot as 207's backfill; `kscw_migrations` + git are the audit trail. ⚠ `sv_vm_check.association_id` is **integer** and `members.license_nr` text — the cascade needs `::text` on both or the join dies on `btrim(integer)`. Unexploited hook for later: `vm-sync-check.mjs:201` already requests VM's `licenses` **history** array and never persists it — mining it would yield *previous club*, but every historical club sits under a Swiss Volley regional association, so still never a previous *national federation*.

- **2026-07-25** **Coded multi-nationality + Federation of Origin (migrations 223 + 224, DEV ONLY — prod pending sign-off)** — from "add a new field in members called FoO? Federation of Origin? and editable via edit profile. also nationality and FoO as dropdowns rather than free text (then converted to german for clubdesk). nationality can be multiselect if needed", plus "also have this in the registration form". **`members.nationalitaet` was free-text `varchar(100)` holding an ALREADY-GERMAN ClubDesk picklist value** ("Schweiz", "Italien") — so it was untranslatable for the four non-German UI locales, single-valued, and silently breakable: two prod rows held **English** names ("Italy", "Switzerland") that ClubDesk's import drops into its *"nicht erkannte"* bucket. So this is a storage change, not a UI change. **223** makes ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 canonical: `members.nationalitaet_codes` (ordered, comma-separated, `"CH,IT"` — **first code is primary** and is the single value ClubDesk gets) + `members.federation_of_origin` (the national federation the member was last licensed with — the Swiss Volley transfer-certificate / FIBA letter-of-clearance key; ISO code, the sentinel `'NONE'` = never licensed elsewhere, or NULL = not answered — ⚠ **NULL vs 'NONE' is a real distinction**, only an explicit 'NONE' lets the club skip a clearance chase), the same pair on `registrations`, a 196-row `country_codes` table, and format CHECKs. **`nationalitaet` is deliberately KEPT, not dropped** — the entire ClubDesk push/drift/echo apparatus reads it — but DEMOTED to a derived mirror by a **bidirectional `BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE` trigger**: codes written → `nationalitaet` := German name of the first code; name-only written (the ClubDesk down-sync, fill-only) → codes reverse-resolved from the name; codes win when both change in one statement. That is what let the nationality half of the ClubDesk push stay **completely unmodified** across four independent write paths (profile, admin explorer, down-sync, approval). Backfill resolved 381/382 and the trigger normalized the two English strays to `Italien`/`Schweiz` as a side effect. **224 fixes forward** on the trap the 223 dev run exposed via that one unresolved row: **ClubDesk's picklist says "Großbritannien" where the country list (from kscw-website, which seeded the picklist) says "Vereinigtes Königreich"** — so a single canonical German name per country is not enough to *parse*, and pushing the display name would land the row in "nicht erkannte". `country_codes.name_de_clubdesk` is now the wire format (identical for 195/196) and a **337-row `country_name_aliases`** table absorbs every other spelling (`USA`, `Holland`, `England`, `Weissrussland`, `Tschechische Republik`, …). ⚠ 7 of the 196 German names also differ from `Intl.DisplayNames`/CLDR (Botswana vs Botsuana, Moldau vs Republik Moldau, Kap Verde vs Cabo Verde …) — **the backend reads `country_codes` from Postgres and must never substitute Intl**; the frontend uses Intl only for *display* labels. **ClubDesk's "Federation of Origin" is a TEXT field** (confirmed with the user), so the pushed `'Keiner'` sentinel and any country name are safe — had it been a picklist, every pushed name would have needed to exist as an option. ⚠ **The echo-back could NOT ride on `federation_of_origin` itself**: wiedisync stores a CODE under a CHECK constraint while ClubDesk's cell is a German string, so assigning ClubDesk's value back onto that field would fail the code lookup and emit an **empty** cell — precisely the blanking the echo exists to prevent. The echo therefore lands in a transient `m.federation_of_origin_cd` that `buildPushCsv` uses verbatim when the member hasn't answered. Country→German map loaded **once per push/drift run**, never per row. **Fixed a pre-existing gap in passing**: `registrations.nationalitaet_code` (migration 161) was **never propagated to `members`**, so approval silently dropped the ISO code — the member-side display had been falling back to fuzzy name matching ever since. **FIBA treats a dual national holding Swiss nationality as Swiss**, so the basketball required-document gate resolves **"CH if any"** through one shared `fibaNatCode()` (`bb-docs.js`) at all three backend enforcement points (registration create, doc-status, approval gate) plus a frontend mirror in `AnmeldungenPage` and `bbNatCode()` in the public form — ⚠ **the form and the server MUST agree**, or the applicant sees a shorter document list than the server enforces and the submission is rejected *after* every upload. The ClubDesk CSV builders deliberately do **NOT** get this rule: ClubDesk's `Nationalität` is single-valued and holds the *primary* nationality, so forcing `Schweiz` for a CH/IT who listed IT first would file wrong data in the legal register. **Permissions — the subtle one**: `nationalitaet` was removed from `MEMBER_EDITABLE_FIELDS` (the DB owns it now), but ⚠ **`MEMBER_OWN_READABLE` and `LEADER_TEAM_MEMBER_FIELDS` both DERIVE from that list**, so the removal would have silently revoked own-read and coach-read too. It moved into a new `MEMBER_DERIVED_READ_FIELDS` folded into both — **not** into `MEMBER_VISIBLE_FIELDS`, which is what every member reads about every *other* member and which migration 024 deliberately keeps free of the `adresse`/`birthdate` PII tier nationality belongs to; putting it there to preserve own-read would have made nationality club-wide readable as a side effect of losing a *write* grant. Net: own-read unchanged, coach/TR read unchanged, finance widened by the two new columns, member write revoked. All three columns added to `REDACTED_FIELDS.members` in `audit.js`. **Public form (kscw-website)**: nationality became a multi-select with removable chips (selection order preserved and meaningful), a Federation-of-origin combobox added reusing the existing `.nationality-*` CSS wholesale (class-based, so zero new styles), both new values submitted, script cache-buster bumped `3.9.0`→`3.10.0`. ⚠ The design deliberately avoided touching `bbDocSet`/`updateBBDocs` because the user had **live WIP refactoring exactly those functions** (Freibrief waivers, `bb_recent_licence`, U12 exemption) — only the two `natCode` expressions changed. That WIP was stashed and re-popped mid-session and the merge landed clean (their 5-arg `bbDocSet` + our `bbNatCode()` at both call sites, no conflict markers). ⚠ **kscw-website was left UNCOMMITTED on purpose**: on re-pop the index held their in-flight `admin.astro` / `club/feedback.astro` / `CHANGELOG.md` / `package.json` / PDF-deletion work mixed with ours, so committing would have swept up unrelated changes. **Verification**: `npm run build` green, `npm run lint` 0 errors (91 pre-existing `data ?? []` warnings, none in touched files), `test:unit` 655 passed, `test:scripts` 68/68; trigger exercised both directions on dev (`CH,IT`→Schweiz, NULL→NULL, `Polen`→PL, `Italy`→IT+Italien, `GB`→Großbritannien, `USA`→US+Vereinigte Staaten); all new columns confirmed present and the drift-shaped join runs (382 rows); dev container log clean after `ext:deploy:dev` + `scripts:deploy:dev`. ⚠ **Permissions are NOT verifiable on dev** (keyless since 2026-07-15 → filtered rules unlicensed, `setup-permissions.mjs` errors and smoke fails by design) — `db:setup-perms:prod` must run as part of the prod deploy, and prod needs **schema before extensions** (`db:deploy:prod` → `ext:deploy:prod` → `scripts:deploy:prod`) or the endpoints 500 on the missing columns. ⚠ Grid consequence: a user whose persisted Explorer column set contained `nationalitaet` loses that column once (the derived free-text column was replaced by the coded one under the same "Nationality" label) — re-tick it once. Regenerate `SCHEMA.sql` (`db:baseline:prod`) after the prod migration.

- **2026-07-17** **Data Health: ClubDesk sync-up modal + multi-select mark + name-drift split + `would_duplicate` enforced (no migration, dev+prod)** — from "can we have a sync-up to ClubDesk there as well, maybe multiselect?" on `/admin/data-health`. Four things, plus a prod data-hygiene pass. **(1) Sync-up modal** now mounts on Data Health (header button, `onDone=rescan`) — the *same* `ClubdeskSyncUpModal` already on Anmeldungen + `/admin/clubdesk-sync`, so there's **one** approval gate for the club's legal register, not a third copy. Page is already `SuperAdminRoute`. **(2) Multi-select bulk mark**: checkboxes on markable drift/fill rows + a select-all bar; the selection collapses to **one** `/clubdesk-drift/flag` POST (it already takes an array and filters blank-risk itself — no per-row fan-out). **(3) Name-drift split — the load-bearing insight.** The UPDATE CSV is deliberately **name-less** (`CD_PUSH_CONTACT_HEADERS` has no Vorname/Nachname — ClubDesk owns legal names, CLAUDE.md), but `computeClubdeskDrift` *compares* names, so a name-only drift could be marked → pushed as a register no-op → flag cleared → **recomputed identical on the next scan**: a permanent treadmill, and multi-select would have made queuing the no-ops *faster*. Name-only drift (`NAME_DRIFT_FIELDS = first_name/last_name`, the exact compared-but-not-pushed set) now gets its own **info row with NO Mark button** (`clubdeskNameDrift`); mixed drift keeps its button and pushes the pushable half, the name part resurfacing as its own row. On prod: of 12 queued, **7 were name-only no-ops** (Sara Berke `Berke-Wenger→Berke` etc. — real marriage/spelling drift, but unpushable). **(4) `would_duplicate` is now ENFORCED, not just computed** — the 2026-07-05 audit #11 gap: `up-preview` flagged a CREATE that would duplicate an existing ClubDesk contact (same name, divergent email) with the comment *"Warn in the modal"*, but the modal never read the field and `/up` never re-checked it, so an approved CREATE could duplicate a contact in the legal register (grep: `would_duplicate` had exactly ONE occurrence, the producer). Modal now badges it + deselects by default; `/up` re-runs the name match and **refuses** collisions (`skipped_would_duplicate`, `409 code=would_duplicate`), mirroring the stale-link/blank-risk guards. **Verified end-to-end on dev** (reversible): unlinked a linked member so their own name collided → `up-preview would_duplicate:true` → `/up` returned `409 would_duplicate skipped:[633]`, restored. **UI driven headless on the dev app** (⚠ the [[dev-app-authed-screenshots]] recipe needs a token whose user HAS a `members` row — `admin@kscw.ch` is a bare ops account with none, so `fetchMember()`→null→logout→login page; use `luca.canepa@gmail.com`=member 8/superuser): Sync-up button renders, 51 row checkboxes, select 2 → "2 selected / Mark 2 for sync-up" bar, modal opens + loads ("3 to update, 0 to create"). Stopped at review — never clicked Push (would enqueue a real dev scrape). ⚠ **Dev is a poor visual test for the name-drift split**: the nightly scrub rewrites emails on one side (`scrub_*@devsink.invalid → member_*@devsink.invalid`) so dev shows **690 drift candidates, 0 name-only**; the classification was validated against **prod** drift instead (17→7 name-only). **Prod data-hygiene** (the "investigate Felix Stauch" ask): member 405 was `clubdesk_push_pending=true` with an **empty diff** and data matching ClubDesk exactly — a **stale flag** (the flag only clears on a committed push, so a resolved drift leaves it stuck; it then sits in the modal forever as an empty "Contact sync" no-op). Cleared 8 such stale/no-op pending flags on prod (405 + the 7 name-only), non-destructive — drift recomputes live and name drift resurfaces as the new info row. Result: sync-up modal **12→4** genuinely-pushable, drift view now **14 name-only info rows + 10 pushable**. ⚠ The 4 genuinely-pushable pending left alone include member 467 (email→a `@kscw.clubdesk.com` placeholder — questionable but out of scope). No migration, no permission rows. i18n en/de + `dh*` keys all 5 locales (`clubdeskUp*` is en/de with en fallback, pre-existing). ⚠ CF Pages shipped an incomplete build **twice** this session (missing `input-*`, then `Container`/`DrawLayer`/`jspdf index.es` chunks 404 at origin) — `assets:check:prod` caught both, force-rebuild fixed. See [[cf-pages-missing-chunks-after-deploy]].
- **2026-07-17** **Two crons fight over `members.scorer_vb` — Data Health now watches all three registers (no migration, dev+prod)** — asked to check "is anyone `scorer_vb=true` but not a Volleymanager scorer, or vice versa". Answer: **VM→flag is airtight (0)** — `vm-sync-check.mjs` sets `scorer_vb=true` for every matched VM writer — but **45 members are flagged without being in VM's `indoorwriter` registry**, and all 45 hold `VB SC` in ClubDesk. ⚠ **The count was the symptom, not the bug: two weekly crons write this column from different sources and VM wins.** `import-clubdesk-csv.mjs` (Sat 22:00) sets `scorer_vb=true` for `VB SC` holders (set-true only, migration 207); `vm-sync-check.mjs` (**Mon 04:00, a `kscw-hooks` cron — NOT in the system crontab**, which is why it isn't findable there) sets `scorer_vb = is_writer`, i.e. it also **clears** it for everyone it matches. Already observed in prod `directus_revisions`: member 180 set true 2026-07-11 13:29, cleared by the sync 2026-07-13 04:00:20 — 40 hours later. Migration 207's backfill (14th, raw SQL → **no revision row**, cf. the actor-capture rule) re-set them, and the next collision is Mon **2026-07-20 04:00**. Replicating the sync's exact match cascade (license→email→vm_email→name+dob→name, collisions dropped) says **28 of the 45 get cleared** (they match a VM row with `is_writer=false`); the other **17 have no `sv_vm_check` row at all**, so nothing touches them — the flag survives only where the sync is blind. ⚠ **The registers do NOT contradict each other**: `scorer_vb` = **154** = ClubDesk's `VB SC` **154** exactly, and VM's **109** writers are a strict **SUBSET** (zero VM writers lack `VB SC`). VM is *incomplete*, not disagreeing — so the sync isn't resolving a conflict, it's deleting the club register's data on the strength of a list that only holds *less* information. **FIXED the same session** (user picked set-true-only after seeing the subset evidence): `vm-sync-check.mjs` may still **grant** `scorer_vb` but no longer **clears** it for a ClubDesk `VB SC` holder — absence from VM is absence of evidence, not evidence of absence; only a register that knows a licence was *revoked* may remove it. Also guards `row.is_referee` (ClubDesk auto-grants scorer to every VB referee, so clearing one oscillates identically — 0 such members today, but the rule is real). The `clubdesk_export` read **fails loud, never degrades**: an empty result looks exactly like "nobody holds VB SC" and would silently re-arm the wipe on 45 members. Per-run count logged (`Kept scorer_vb …: 28`) — a silent guard is indistinguishable from a broken one. **Verified end-to-end on dev against a live VM sync**: 154 held (old code → 126), `Kept scorer_vb: 28`, 237 matched / 0 errors, and member 180 — the one provably wiped on the 13th — still flagged. ⚠ `directus/scripts/` is a **separate bind-mount**: ship with `scripts:deploy:*`, **NOT** `ext:deploy` (and it is **git-blind** — it rsyncs the working tree, so check for foreign WIP first). ⚠ Invoking the script by bare `docker exec` 401s and proves nothing: the hooks cron spawns it with `DIRECTUS_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8055` + `DIRECTUS_TOKEN`, and the script reads `ADMIN_EMAIL`/`ADMIN_PASSWORD` while the container only sets `DIRECTUS_SYNC_*`. VM's writer list has meanwhile grown 109 → **118 rows**. **`referee_vb` had the IDENTICAL bug and is now guarded the same way** — and the first read of it was wrong twice, worth recording as a method lesson: (1) "ClubDesk has no competing referee source" came from checking `offiziellen_lizenz` (which reads `VB SC` for all 13 — a *scorer* licence, no referee grade); ClubDesk actually feeds `referee_vb` from **group membership** (`gruppen_bracketed` ~ `VB Schiedsrichter*innen`), and `import-clubdesk-csv.mjs` calls that group *"the source of truth for is a referee for Wiedikon"*, set-true only. (2) "VM dropped them in the last 4 days, so it will fire Monday" — `directus_revisions` shows the sync **already cleared members 5 + 61 on 2026-07-13 04:00:16**, and something restored them with **no revision row** (raw SQL, same blind spot as 207's backfill): the flip was already in flight, not pending. Structure mirrors scorer exactly: VM `clubreferee` **11** ⊂ ClubDesk group **13** = `referee_vb` **13**, zero VM referees outside the group. Probed VM's live `clubreferee` directly (read-only) to be sure: Sharusanth (87636) + Thamayanth (100868) genuinely **ABSENT**, sibling Aniisanth (111828) present — VM is not blind to them (both are current `is_writer`), it specifically does not license them. **The two registers answer different questions** — VM = holds a current SVRZ referee *licence*; the ClubDesk group = the club's referee *roster* — and `referee_vb` means the latter (it drives the read-only "Referee for Wiedikon" badge). User's call: **keep ClubDesk/wiedisync**. ⚠ **Accepted consequence: NOTHING clears `referee_vb` automatically any more** (ClubDesk's rule is set-true-only too — "dropped from the group keeps the flag until manually cleared"), so revoking a referee = remove from the ClubDesk group AND clear the flag by hand. ⚠ The group name is a shared constant with the import's rule — **a drift between the two silently un-guards the column**; the match splits on comma rather than substring so a future group *containing* this name can't false-fire. **Verified on dev against live VM, both directions** (a guard only ever proven to keep is half-tested): restored dev to the prod state (154/13) → sync held **154/13** where the old code gives **126/11** (`Kept scorer_vb: 28`, `Kept referee_vb: 2`); then cleared member 62 (a real VM referee) → sync **re-granted both flags** false→true, proving the guards block clearing only and did not freeze the columns. New `GET /kscw/admin/scorer-vm-check` (`superGate` — ⚠ `sv_vm_check` direct read stays REVOKED for Members, the 2026-05-06 Critical that `smoke-test.mjs:157` asserts; never widen it), three aggregate rows on Data Health, each with the full list behind an **xlsx Export** (`exportRows` on `DataIssue` — the ClubDesk aggregates expand on the ClubDesk sync page, scorer has no such page). ⚠ **The third direction is the whole point**: `cd_vb_sc_not_flagged` reads **0** while ClubDesk last wrote and jumps to ~28 the moment the sync clears them — without it the check would report **fewer** incongruences exactly as data is destroyed, the same false all-clear the hall audit hit printing "✓ 0/80" on a 401. Same reason the endpoint failing raises an **error row** instead of the silent `catch {}` the ClubDesk checks use. **Proven both ways on dev** (a detector that only ever prints 0 is indistinguishable from a broken one): injected `scorer_vb=false` on member 30 → direction 3 fired 1, `flagged_not_in_vm` 45→44, total 154→153; restored → 0/45/154. Route verified registered+gated (403 unauthed vs 404 for a nonexistent route). ⚠ The check's cascade **must stay in step with `vm-sync-check.mjs`** — it exists to predict that script, so drift makes it confidently wrong. Also filled 4 `dhIssueClubdesk*` keys missing in gsw/fr/it (rendering raw key strings). No migration, no permission rows (raw knex).
- **2026-07-16** **Announcements: team + role audience targeting (migration 219, v1.46.0) + club-admin mailbox (migration 222, dormant) — dev+prod** — Vereinsnews could only address `all` or one `sport`. `teams`/`roles` were stubbed in 005 ("future use, hidden in v1 admin UI") — columns, `directus_fields` and the TS union all existed, nothing resolved them. **The stub failed SILENTLY, not loudly**: `resolveAnnouncementAudience` branched on `if (audience_type !== 'all')`, so a teams/roles post fell into the *sport* branch, found no `audience_sport`, returned `[]`, and was stamped `fanout_sent_at` having mailed nobody. Now an explicit switch; an unrecognised type fails closed instead of landing in a neighbouring branch. Roles span **three namespaces the schema all calls "role"**, so tokens are prefixed and allow-listed (unknown → dropped, never widening): `role:*` (`members.role` enum), `fn:*` (coach/team_responsible/captain, from the junctions — NOT `members.role` values), `qual:*` (`scorer_vb`/`referee_vb`/`otr1_bb`/`is_spielplaner`… booleans). **The read gate is the non-obvious half.** Members deliberately cannot read `audience_teams`/`audience_roles` (`setup-permissions.mjs` — exposing them reveals targeting intent), so unlike `sport` a targeted post **cannot be matched client-side**: it would have mailed + pushed + rung the bell while `/news` 404'd. So the publish fanout **materializes** one `announcement_recipients` row per resolved member and the Member/Leader filter gates teams/roles on that row (`all`/`sport` keep matching on the announcement itself, so materialization is purely additive for them and a failure can't hide an existing club-wide post). The junction does triple duty: read gate, **per-recipient delivery log** (`email_at`/`email_error` answer "who didn't get it", which log counts never could), and a frozen as-of-publish audience snapshot. No `push_at` — the push helper sends per locale bucket and reports nothing per-recipient, so any value would be a guess. ⚠ **SECURITY, pre-existing and reachable in prod**: `validateAnnouncementAudience`'s comment claimed it caught "audience_sport unset OR audience_type='all'" but the code **only ever checked `audience_sport`** — and the resolver's `all` branch ignores `audience_sport` — so **any vb_admin/bb_admin could set type=all + sport=<their sport> and mail the entire club**. Non-global admins can no longer select `all`. It also judged raw PATCH payloads (partial on update → both false-rejected innocent saves *and* let a cross-sport edit through); now merges payload over the stored row. Role targeting is global-admin only (`role:vorstand` crosses every sport). The mass-email confirm keyed on `=== 'all'`, which teams/roles walk past (`role:user` IS the club) → any email send now confirms, naming the audience. **Verified on dev**: teams-targeted post → 18/18 expected recipients materialized, 18 bell stamps, 0 email stamps (email off — dev sends live SES); the `recipients` walk resolves and the `_or` does **not** collapse (recipient sees `[sport, teams]`, non-recipient `[sport]` only). ⚠ **Policy-layer evaluation is NOT verifiable on dev** — see the keyless-dev entry below; prod is licensed and holds the relation + alias identically, and had 0 published announcements at deploy time so the live filter had nothing to break. **Mailbox (222)**: third account beside the two Spielplanung ones, at `/admin/mailbox` in the member app. **Extends `scheduling_emails` rather than forking** — the IMAP sync/MIME/Sent-append/attachment streaming are already account-agnostic and a fork = two copies of ~900 lines that drift (cf. team-availability ↔ slots/:token). Honest cost: the table + module keep names that no longer describe them (`account` was always a partition key; `sport` was just the only partition that existed). ⚠ **The route split is a security boundary, not cosmetics**: `/admin/mailbox` pins the account and `?sport=admin` on the terminplanung routes resolves to **null**, because the Spielplanung gate grants `is_spielplaner` and a volleyball scheduler has no business in the club's general inbox — `authForAccount('admin')` = superadmin OR admin/superuser OR **vorstand** only; frontend `VorstandRoute` mirrors it exactly (and this is its first use). **Per-user reads** (`scheduling_email_reads`): `scheduling_emails.read_at` is a single GLOBAL marker — right for Spielplanung (one shared desk) and wrong for a board inbox where several read independently; Spielplanung keeps `read_at` untouched. Mail path Migadu IMAP in / **SES SMTP out**, so Migadu's send quota is never consumed (only webmail sends touch it). ⚠ Sending needs `wiedisync.kscw.ch` SES-verified **and** `include:amazonses.com` in its SPF — that domain is DMARC `p=quarantine`, so without both, replies are **silently quarantined at the receiver with nothing in any log** (the finance@ / Google-Group failure mode). SPF landed; DKIM 2/3 CNAMEs (SPF alignment carries DMARC meanwhile). **ACTIVATED the same day (dev+prod)**: Migadu mailbox exists, password vaulted (`services/migadu-spielplanung / Migadu - admin@wiedisync.kscw.ch`), `ADMIN_MAILBOX_IMAP_PASSWORD` set in both `/opt/directus-kscw{,-dev}/.env` (backups `.env.bak-20260716`), both containers **recreated** (a `docker restart` silently keeps the old env → `configured:false`), prod `/server/info` re-checked → `license.source=env` survived the recreate. Verified end-to-end on both: IMAP connects, sync pulled the live INBOX message, unread badge agrees with the list. ⚠ **Access narrowed on request mid-session: admin/superuser ONLY — `vorstand` was considered and explicitly REJECTED.** `authForAccount('admin')` has no branch of its own; the admin/superuser early-return IS the grant. No existing frontend guard matched (`AdminRoute` also admits vb_admin/bb_admin whom the server 403s; `VorstandRoute` admits the board; `SuperAdminRoute` is `superuser`-only and would lock out plain admins) → added **`GlobalAdminRoute`** + switched nav gating in BOTH `useNavItems.tsx` and `MoreSheet.tsx` (which duplicates the admin list wholesale). ⚠ **Bug found while verifying**: for a caller with no `members` row (a bare Directus superadmin — the ops identity), `applyReadState` reported every row unread while `unreadCount` returned a flat 0 — the same response said both. Now counts them consistently; unreachable for the real audience since admin/superuser live on `members.role`. ⚠ **Dev and prod sync the SAME live mailbox** (as the Spielplanung boxes already do) — read-only + deduped per account and read state is per-DB so they don't fight, but **dev sends via live SES**, so a reply composed on dev is a real email. ⚠ **The activation password was pasted in chat in plaintext and is a guessable pattern — rotate it.** ⚠ **Vaulting exposed a separate live breakage: ALL `rbw` writes+sync from lenovoserver were silently dead** (reads kept working off the agent cache, so the vault looked healthy). Cause was DNS, not Vaultwarden: the public `.ts.net` name resolved to Tailscale **Funnel** IPv6 whose TLS handshake dies, while the vault server actually runs on that very box. Fixed with a user-run `/etc/hosts` pin to the tailnet IP; **MagicDNS would be the proper fix** (the pin goes stale if the IP changes). Regression-verified on dev: VB mailbox still 200/configured:true/530 unread, `?sport=admin`→400, `/admin/mailbox` 200 configured:false authed / 403 unauthed. ⚠ **Numbered 222, not 220** — 220/221 landed in parallel on the shared tree mid-session. ⚠ **`db:migrate:prod` is git-blind**: a parallel prod deploy at 10:26 swept migration **219 + the announcements perms + the hook code** to prod off the working directory, six minutes after commit and before any approval — harmless here (additive, and prod had 0 published posts) but it is exactly the hazard.

- **2026-07-16** **VM↔Wiedisync hall audit: 80/80 match — and VM grew a second combo gym (migration 220, DEV ONLY)** — re-ran the check migration 209 left open ("the 29 fixtures VM holds in 914 still need a deliberate push"). Live read of all KSCW home fixtures (`api\game/search`, read-only — no `validateGame`/`updateGame`): **all 80 2026/27 VB home fixtures match hall-for-hall** (31 KWI A / 22 KWI C / 17 KWI B / 10 Döltschi), every one has a hall set on both sides, and **VM now holds nothing in the 3-court combo 914** — the league re-placed them during the gym cleanup. ⚠ **We did not do that: `svrz_push_status` is null on all 80** — we have never pushed. The agreement is someone else's manual work, not something we enforce, so it can drift again silently; this audit is the only detector. **New fact: VM gym 4144 `Kantonsschule Wiedikon A+B`** (2 courts, homol. G, uuid `5261363c-da18-40e4-ab87-9d6bbdb6240b`) postdates 209 (which only knew 153/914/3231/3232/3989) and is **legitimately in use** — the H1/H3 derbies (#406192 23.09.26, #406237 08.01.27) are placed there by hand, and BB spans A+B as a matter of course. **The two notations agree**: our derby rows are `hall=KWI A` + `additional_halls=[KWI B]`, i.e. Wiedisync already says A+B; VM says one combo gym. ⚠ **A naive comparison reading only `games.hall` will now report these 2 as mismatches — they are not.** Migration 220 makes the 209 rule durable and teaches it 4144: 209 asserted it in a `DO` block, but the runner applies each migration **exactly once**, so that check fired on 2026-07-14 and can never fire again — it never covered the realistic path (a hall remapped by hand in the Directus admin). Now a `BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE` trigger on `halls` (`trg_halls_reject_vm_combo`), keyed on **either** `sv_hall_id` or `vm_hall_id`, for both combos. The rule is narrow and unchanged in spirit: **a combo is a property of a GAME, never of a hall row** — `hall_slots`/Hallenplan/conflict-checker all assume one row = one court, so a hall pointed at 4144 would book BOTH courts for every ordinary single-court game, silently (the push reports success). Verified on dev by exercising it: KWI A→4144 raises, KWI C→914 raises, legitimate single-court remap passes, unrelated edit passes, data unchanged. **Then closed the push gap (migration 221 + `vm-halls.mjs`)** — `vm-push-game.mjs` resolved its hall from `booking.slot.hall`, a single FK, so an A+B booking would have pushed as gym 3231 (A alone): silently, reporting success, overwriting a correct 4144. Same class as 209, mirrored — 209 booked too MANY courts, this books too FEW. ⚠ **Nothing links a booking to its game** (`bookings.game` 0/78 confirmed, `slots.game` 0/2017, `slots.booking` 0/2017 — the FKs exist in `directus_relations` but nothing writes them; slots↔games are matched implicitly by date/time/hall/team), so the combo could not be borrowed from `games.additional_halls`. **221** adds `game_scheduling_slots.additional_halls` (`json` + `cast-json`/`tags`, mirroring `games.additional_halls` exactly) so a slot can span courts. **`vm-halls.mjs`** is the pure resolver: hall SET → one VM gym, keyed on **VM's gym uuids, not our `halls.id`** (row ids are local to a DB; uuids survive a rebuild). It **fails closed** — an unregistered combo or a partially-mapped set returns `unmapped_combo` and pushes NO hall, rather than downgrading to the primary court. Handles Döltschi 1+2 → one gym (VM registers the venue once as 153) and the duplicated-hall shape the away derby rows carry (`hall=1, additional_halls=[1]`). 17 unit tests (`__tests__/vm-halls.test.mjs`, `npm run test:scripts` 68/68). Verified end-to-end on dev against the live VM read path: A+B slot → `slot spans 2 courts → VM combo gym "Kantonsschule Wiedikon A+B"`; A+C → `unmapped_combo` (refused, not downgraded); plain slot → unchanged. ⚠ **A new column needs a Directus RESTART** — the schema cache hid `slot.additional_halls` until the container bounced, and the field silently read `undefined` (harmlessly degrading to single-hall). ⚠ **The push is a no-op for 2026/27 regardless: 158 of 160 fixtures are `approved`, only 2 `open`** — the push only touches `open` games and correctly refuses to rewrite an approved fixture, which is why `svrz_push_status` is null everywhere. **Then made the audit repeatable (`vm-sync-check.mjs` Group C)** — the whole reason 4144 went unnoticed is that *nothing detects hall drift*: we never push, so VM's correctness is the league's manual work and the only detector was someone thinking to ask. The weekly VM cron now audits halls too, comparing our hall **SET → gym via `resolveVmHall`** (not `games.hall` vs `hall.name`, which would flag the A+B derbies as mismatches forever). Read-only and **never fatal** — a mismatch needs a human, but it isn't a sync error, and failing the run would alert + make the watchdog retry something a retry can't fix + falsely mark the person/team syncs (which DID apply) as failed. Only flags `approved` fixtures — while a fixture is `open` the schedule is still being negotiated and divergence is normal. Also `AUDIT_ONLY=1 node vm-sync-check.mjs` for the on-demand question, safe against prod (VM search + 2 Directus GETs, no writes). ⚠ **Two self-inflicted traps caught during verification, both worth remembering**: (1) the first version printed `✓ Hall audit: 0/80 … match` when a Directus **401** made its reads return `[]` — a false all-clear, the one outcome worse than no audit; the reads now throw and `checked === 0` reports **INCONCLUSIVE**, never ✓. (2) `checked` was incremented *before* the approved/unmapped guards, inflating the count with fixtures that were skipped; it now counts only what reached the comparison, and the skip reasons are printed so a silently-shrinking audit can't hide behind a reassuring number. Proven both ways on dev by **injecting drift** (KWI C → the KWI B gym): 22 fixtures flagged, then clean again after restore — a detector that only ever prints 0 is indistinguishable from a broken one. Mismatch text carries the gym **uuids**, because "we say KWI C, VM says Kantonsschule Wiedikon C" reads as if the two sides agree. Verified against live prod: **80 VM home fixtures, 80 compared, 0 mismatches**. **Finally the A+B picker (manual booking)** — migration 221 gave the slot the column and `vm-halls.mjs` the translation, but nothing could *create* a multi-court slot. `ManualBookingForm` now offers the other courts as "Also uses" once a primary hall is picked (all 5 locales); `/terminplanung/admin/manual-booking` validates them (non-array → 400, unknown id → 400, primary dropped from the set) and persists on **both** the reuse and mint paths. Opt-in end to end: no ticks = the exact payload and behaviour as before, and extras are written even when empty so a re-entry that drops the second court **shrinks** the booking rather than quietly keeping yesterday's combo. ⚠ **The find that mattered — `rebalanceSaturdayHalls` already owns hall assignment for KWI Saturday games** (lone game → C, keeping A+B free for basketball; two concurrent → A and B; three → A+B+C) and rewrites `slots.hall` **in place on every booking change**. It assumes one game = one court, so applied to a combo it would move the primary and strand `additional_halls`, leaving a set like {C, B} that no VM gym exists for (→ `unmapped_combo`, push refuses). Combo slots are now **excluded** from the rebalance — a deliberate two-court booking beats the automatic re-sort. Surfaced only by accident: a dev test booking on 14.11.2026 came back as **KWI C when KWI A was sent**. Residual, documented: a Saturday combo + two other concurrent games would over-assign (the rebalance no longer counts the combo's courts) — that needs 4 courts in a 3-court gym, so it is already physically impossible. Verified on dev against the live endpoint (both 400s fire; a combo mints a slot carrying `[2]`; existing slots untouched), test data removed. Extension + frontend, no migration, no permission rows. **dev+prod** — deployed ext byte-identical to the prod branch (checked; no working-tree WIP leaked).

- **2026-07-16** **ClubDesk group REMOVAL tool — `clubdesk-remove-group.mjs` (prod; script deployed + git dev+prod)** — the counterpart to `clubdesk-scrape-groups.mjs` (which only *adds*): the "In a ClubDesk group, not on the roster" consistency-check on `/admin/clubdesk-sync` surfaces strays, but removing a group had no automation. The toolbar path (`Gruppen ▼ → Kontakt aus Gruppe entfernen`) is **greyed out in "Alle Kontakte"** — it only enables inside a specific group's left-nav view — so the tool drives the **contact-detail** view instead: Filtern by uuid → double-click → the `Gruppen:` field renders each allocation as a chip with a 16px `<a>` × icon → click the × of the chip whose label === the target → **Speichern & Schließen**. Precise per-allocation (never touches the other chips), `preview`/`commit` modes, and a **verify-before-save** guard (re-reads the chip after the × click; if it's still present it aborts *without saving*, so a mis-click can't persist the wrong state). ⚠ Two recon traps: (a) the × `<a>` sits **inside** the label's right edge (VB H2 label `[943,1080]` → × at `cx=1067`), so a "just past `L.right`" offset misses into the gap → match the rightmost small `<a>` whose center is in the label's right ~2/3; (b) the chip list needs **~4s to settle** — a shorter wait showed a phantom bare `VB Trainer*innen` beside the real `VB Trainer*innen (Trainer*in)` (the `(Trainer*in)` suffix hadn't painted). First use: removed a **stale `VB H2 (Spieler*in)`** from Lasse Meinen (member 77) — he played H2 in **2025/26** but this season only *coaches* DU23-1, and the consistency check correctly flagged the leftover player group; his coach/scorer chips (`VB DU23 (Trainer*in)`, `VB Schreiber*innen`, `VB Trainer*innen (Trainer*in)`) were left intact. Reusable via the same `/opt/clubdesk-sync/` + `.sync.lock` + Playwright-docker harness as the add tool; picked up by `npm run clubdesk:deploy` (globs `clubdesk-*.mjs`). **Then wired to a weekly cron — `clubdesk-group-cleanup.sh` (Sun 06:00 UTC, after the Sat 22:00 down-sync refreshes `clubdesk_export`).** It recomputes strays with the exact `/clubdesk-group-sync` `straySql` logic and auto-removes only the user-approved envelope: `active=false` (left the club) OR active coach/TR (the Lasse pattern — staffs a team, plays on none → the player group is stale). Active *plain/official* strays are left for the manual consistency-check card (usually a missing roster row, not a wrong group). Two runaway guards: abort if `<20` member_teams rows exist for the season (a DB glitch would otherwise flag the whole club), and a `CAP` (default 75) on the removal set. Commit gated behind `CLUBDESK_ENV=prod`+`CLUBDESK_CLEANUP_COMMIT=1`; dev forced to preview. Initial catch-up removed **48** stale player groups (spot-verified: target chip gone, scorer/coach chips intact). ⚠ **A multi-contact bug the dry-run caught before any write** — run across many contacts in one session, the tool sometimes read the PREVIOUS contact's detail (a stale detail tab): 28/49 previews read a *neighbour's* chips, which in commit mode would have stripped the wrong person's group. Diagnosed with fresh-browser-per-uuid reads (all matched `clubdesk_export` → the export was right, the tool was wrong). Fixed with an **identity assertion**: the tool now confirms the open detail's Wiedisync-ID field `== target uuid` before touching a chip AND re-asserts right before `Speichern` (re-selects+reopens on mismatch; `skip_identity_unconfirmed` if it can't confirm). Re-preview after the fix: 48/48 correct, 1 safe no-op (Lasse — the stale export still lists the H2 already removed earlier). No migration, no permission rows, no frontend, no version bump (scripts-only).
- **2026-07-15** **ClubDesk sync suite unstuck (pg16 rename) + unveränderte guard + Sektion sync-up + group-assignment UI automation — prod (scripts deployed direct; git dev+prod)** — (1) **Root cause of the stuck sync-up 409**: the 2026-07-11 Postgres container rename (`supabase-db-…`→`kscw-postgres`) never reached the `/opt/clubdesk-sync/` cron scripts (a separate bind-mount with NO deploy target), so every `docker exec <dead-container>` failed — silently, because each `psqlc` is guarded `|| echo 0`/`|| true` — and the ENTIRE ClubDesk cron suite (sync-up, sync-down, weekly member sync, nightly finance) was dead on prod for 4 days while the lock-file mtime kept it *looking* alive. Redeployed the 7 repo scripts + added `npm run clubdesk:deploy` (fails if the stale name reappears). Manual CLI syncs were unaffected (they run the repo scripts) → masked it. (2) **Unveränderte-aware sync-up guard** (`clubdesk-member-up-dispatch.sh` + `clubdesk-scrape-import.mjs`): the update-set dup guard required `veraendert==total`, which FALSE-refused any push containing byte-identical rows (common right after a sync-down) — now reads the "Unveränderte" count and requires `neu==0 AND neu+veraendert+unveraendert==total` (stronger: any dropped/renamed summary line still fail-closes). (3) **`members.sektion`** derived club-wide (current-season team→sport; else `VB`/`BB` fee category→sport; else KSCW — 75 changed) then one-off `[Id];Sektion` push to ClubDesk (74) so ClubDesk stays authoritative; revert snapshot `members_sektion_backup_20260715`. (4) **NEW `clubdesk-scrape-groups.mjs`** — Playwright UI automation for ClubDesk group membership (no CSV path, no API — it's GWT-RPC `update` with an Allocation). Assigned **234/236** current members to their groups: team `(Spieler*in)`/`(Trainer*in)`, sport-wide `VB Trainer*innen`/`BB Trainer`, `VB Schreiber*innen`, `VB Schiedsrichter*innen`, `BB Schiedsrichter`, `BB Offiziellen`, guests `(Guest)`. **⚠ Matches contacts by Wiedisync ID (`members.uuid`) in "Alle Kontakte"** — the `clubdesk_id` isn't Filtern-searchable and non-members live outside "Mitglieder"; 2 left (a kein-Mitglied can't hold a player role — OK stays disabled). `teams.clubdesk_group` re-mapped for the ClubDesk DU19→DU20 / HU23-1→HU23 / DU23-1+2→DU23 renames.

- **2026-07-15** **Basketball scheduling prep (migration 214) + member display names / nicknames (migration 215) — dev+prod, v1.44.0** — **Basketball**: the Spielplanung app now splits by sport via a clear Volleyball/Basketball tab toggle in `SchedulingLayout` (shown only to bb-admins; volleyball-only admins see it unchanged). Basketball is deliberately **NOT** the volleyball bilateral engine — ProBasket (Nord-Ostschweizer BV) owns the schedule (physical Spielplansitzung 5 Sep + Basketplan), so there are **no opponents/tokens/bookings**. The new `basketball_hall_availability` collection (per team, per Fri/Sat/Sun date: `unavailable` + `windows` jsonb of `{hall,from,to}`) backs a per-team "prep" view (`BasketballPrepPage` + `useBasketballAvailability`) that overlays VB hall use / hall closures / club-blocks / BB training onto each KWI A/C candidate date and lets a coordinator record availability for the meeting and the 17-Aug hall-availability Excel. Candidate window + blackouts come from ProBasket's real *Spiel- und Sperrdaten 2026/27* (Vorrunde 19.09–13.12.26, Herbstferien 05–11.10) pulled via the ProBasket Nextcloud **WebDAV** (`public.php/webdav`, share token as basic-auth user). Sport-Admin CRUD (UI-gated to bb-admins); items-API writes (auto actor-log), **no custom endpoint**. ProBasket Excel export deferred to next season (schema is export-ready). **Nicknames**: `members.nickname` preferred display name shown across the app UI (`nickname ?? first_name`) — rosters, chat, RSVP, absences, scorer, finance, scheduling — while legal `first_name` stays on match sheets / VM / ClubDesk / invoices / public website; the field is in the member perm allow-lists (`setup-permissions.mjs` MEMBER_VISIBLE/EDITABLE + leader/finance) and the messaging extension resolves display names server-side. ⚠ **Deploy gotcha — `db:setup-perms:dev` is now non-authoritative**: dev runs keyless (Core grace, see the entry below), so `POST /permissions` carrying custom rules (row filters) 403s *"custom_permission_rules_enabled is a restricted resource"* — 124 such on the run, but **non-destructive** (the delete of a license-gated perm also 403s, so the existing filtered perms survive untouched; dev perm-count = prod + the new **filterless** grants). No migration, no permission rows, no frontend, no prod change.

The keypair is **created silently at login** (`identityBootstrap.ts`) because that's the *only* moment the app holds the plaintext password — every other render restores an httpOnly cookie. `POST /kscw/change-password` verifies the **current** password so a voluntary change **re-wraps** the key; without it, all four existing password paths orphan it, and for a **coach** that cascades to every player's ID in the squad. **No escrow, no recovery code, no admin recovery** — a lost password means re-upload (the member still has the card in their wallet, which is the only reason E2EE is affordable here). **Provisional/server-generated keys were proposed and explicitly REJECTED**: coverage grows *only* as members log in. "someone else cannot open an envelope", "the device key cannot be exported") + a live dev round-trip using the real `e2ee.ts`.
