- **2026-07-13** **Auto Einsatzliste — file the VM nomination list from confirmed RSVPs (migration 206; dev)** — the other half of this morning's reader: instead of a coach hand-typing the Einsatzliste into Volleymanager before every game, a `*/5` cron picks up games whose kickoff is **T-65 → T-35 min**, matches the confirmed RSVPs to VM's licence holders, files the list and closes it. Opt-in, **off by default**. Control cascade **reuses the existing auto-confirm-RSVP idiom** rather than inventing a second one: `COALESCE(games.auto_nomination_list, teams.features_enabled->>'auto_nomination_list', false)` — per-game tri-state (null = inherit) + a team default in the `features_enabled` bag. Resolved at **push time**, not stamped at game-create time, so (unlike auto-confirm) it needs **no backfill hook** when the team toggle flips and the ~350 games/season sv-sync inserts via raw knex — bypassing every items hook — are covered for free. New `directus/scripts/vm-push-nomination.mjs` (worker, mirrors `vm-push-game.mjs`: `registerWindow` live-WS gate, closed in a `finally`), `kscw-endpoints/src/nomination-push.js` (`POST /kscw/games/:id/nomination-push` — coach/TR/sport-admin, `writeUserLog`), cron in kscw-hooks, migration 206 (6 cols + `directus_fields` registration + partial index), UI in RosterEditor (team default, VB-only) / ManualGameModal + GameDetailModal (per-game tri-state + status box + "Push now" on failure), i18n ×5. **Two safety properties are the point of the change, both unit-tested (51/51):** (1) **There is NO VM staging** — dev and prod authenticate against the same *real* Swiss Volley system with the same club credentials, so an armed dev cron would file real Einsatzlisten; the worker **forces DRY_RUN when `DB_DATABASE` matches `/dev/i`** (`VM_NOMINATION_ALLOW_DEV_WRITE=1` overrides for a supervised test) — verified inside the dev container. (2) **Save and close are the SAME `PUT api\nominationlist`**, one boolean (`isClosedForTeam`) apart, and VM marks `nominationList_hasTooFewNominations` (#37) + `nominationList_isMissingCoachPerson` (#33) as **`isFineable: true`** → an unconditional auto-close would earn the club **fines** on a thin-RSVP week. So: fill → re-read VM's recomputed validation → close **only** on a clean read-back (`fineableBlockers()` empty), else leave open (`status='filled'`) for the coach. `assertNotClosing()` guards every fill payload. **Established by probing the live API against 3 real filed lists (8/8/14 players)**: own side only (the opponent's half is never readable *or* writable); **home AND away** — VM offers each club a list slot for every game (the away path returned `nominationListTeamAway` populated; the scorer sheet stays home-only for an unrelated reason), 172/172 home + **180/180 away** `vb_` games join by game number; `person.associationId` **IS** `members.license_nr`; and the entity carries **no jersey number and no captain** — the Einsatzliste is a *licence-eligibility* list, not a line-up, so those were never pushable. We never fabricate a nomination: we fetch VM's own `getPossibleIndoorPlayerNominationsForNominationList` and keep the candidates whose `associationId` is in our confirmed set, exactly as the browser does. **Perms**: `games` writes become a **field allow-list** (`GAME_WRITE_FIELDS`, 58 cols) — coaches keep every write they had and gain the toggle, but the 5 `vm_nomination_*` journal cols are cron-owned, because a coach who could PATCH `vm_nomination_status='closed'` would **silently suppress their own team's push**; scoped on **Spielplaner too**, since Directus *unions* the rows of every policy a user holds. ⚠ Verify future additions to that list against the **live DB, not `SCHEMA.sql`** (the baseline lagged 202/203/205 and the first pass missed `duty_late_json` + `duty_leader_alert_json` — both backend-owned, so harmless, now excluded deliberately). dev: migration + setup-perms (523/0) + smoke 26/26 + ext/scripts deploy. **⚠ Off-season**: `getPossibleIndoorPlayerNominationsForNominationList` returns `[]` until licences are validated (~Sept), so out of season the push can only ever report `skipped` — **the first real fill is unverifiable until then and must be supervised.** Not on prod.
- **2026-07-13** **Home ticker polish — all-hall closures collapse to "All halls" (dev+prod)** — a school-holiday closure is one `hall_closures` row **per hall**, so the merged calendar entry listed all 14 names ("Sommerferien 2026 · Borrweg 1, Borrweg 2, Döltschi 1, … Zurlinden") and blew out the pill. `useCalendarData` now also reads the `halls` collection (gated on `fetchClosures`, so public visitors don't 403 — `halls` is Public-readable anyway) and renders the existing `common:allHalls` string when a closure covers every hall; below that it still falls back to `compactHallList()`. Same collapse applies in the calendar views, which share the hook, and matches what `ClosureManager` already showed. Verified against the DB: 14 halls total, and every holiday/Auffahrt closure covers exactly 14 → collapses. Also: upcoming ticker pills now carry the date (`Mo 14.07 19:00`, new `formatDayMonthZurich()` in dateHelpers — de-CH dot format, Europe/Zurich; today's pills keep "Today"), and `turnsAge` capitalised in all 5 locales ("turns 33" → "Turns 33"). Frontend-only — no migration, no perms, no ext deploy.
- **2026-07-13** **Scorer match sheet reads the real Volleymanager Einsatzliste (dev+prod)** — the scorer's "View roster" no longer infers the line-up from RSVPs: it serves the **Einsatzliste the team actually filed in VM**, i.e. the document they copy onto the match sheet, so a nominated player who never RSVP'd is still on the sheet. New `vm-nomination-list.js` (kscw-endpoints) calls `api\game/getNominationListOrTeamForActivePartyByGame` **read-only** through the existing `vm-client.mjs` login (session cached 15 min, 8 s timeout); `scorer-roster.js` picks source **`vm` → else `rsvp`** and returns it as `source` so RosterModal captions which sheet you are looking at (+ licence column, ⚠ marker on `isEligible:false`). **Matched BY GAME NUMBER**: `games.game_id` (`vb_<SV gameId>`) → strip prefix → `svrz_games.svrz_number` → `svrz_persistence_id` → VM. 172/172 home VB games join; `svrz_number` is unique. Guard: `nominationListTeamHome` is only served when `svrz_games.home_club_id = 912530`, so a mis-resolved number cannot leak an opponent's list. **Two hard limits, established by probing the live API** — the source HAR only ever showed *empty* lists, so the field names were probed against 5 real past games (all had filed + closed lists): (1) **own side only** — the endpoint is scoped to the "active party", `nominationListTeamAway` is *always* null for us, so the opponent's list is unreadable (hence home-games-only, which gate 3 already enforced); (2) the list carries **no jersey number and no captain** — those exist only in our DB, so they are merged in by joining VM's `person.associationId` to **`members.license_nr`** (verified identical: 8/8 probe players matched on licence nr + birthdate + licence category; 395/708 members carry one). ⚠ **`person.birthday` is an instant at Zurich midnight** (`1988-10-06T23:00:00Z`) — slicing it yields the PREVIOUS day; always use `formattedAndTimezoneIndependentBirthday`. Jersey `0` now renders blank (not a legal VB number; `members.number` is mostly unset). RSVP fallback fires on basketball (`bb_` prefix — short-circuits before any VM call), unfiled/empty list, and VM down/slow/unauthenticated — never a 500. **The read is side-effect-free**: opening the page in a browser CREATEs a blank list (a separate `POST api\nominationlist`) — we never call that. Verified on dev against real games: 394467 + 382693 → `source=vm`, 8 players each with RLL licences + VM coaches; a Sept-2026 game → `source=rsvp`; a BB game → `source=rsvp`. No schema migration, no setup-perms change. i18n en/de/fr/it (gsw has no roster keys, existing convention). ⚠ These files were swept into commit `17a64864` by a parallel session's `git add -A`, so they ride in with the ClubDesk consistency check.
- **2026-07-13** **Empty event-invite picker for every coach/TR — `members.wiedisync_active` was finance-only (perms fix, dev+prod)** — found while checking the prod error log after a deploy (NOT caused by it; first seen 2026-07-12). `errors-*.jsonl`: `page: /events`, `"You don't have permission to access field \"wiedisync_active\""`, member 442. **Root cause**: `MemberMultiSelect` (the invite picker inside `EventForm`) queries `members` **unfiltered** with `filter: { wiedisync_active: { _eq: true } }`, and **Directus rejects a *filter* on a field the caller cannot read**. `wiedisync_active` was in `FINANCE_MEMBER_FIELDS` **only** — not `MEMBER_VISIBLE_FIELDS`, `MEMBER_OWN_READABLE` or `LEADER_TEAM_MEMBER_FIELDS`. Because the query is club-wide it resolves against MEMBER_POLICY, **not** the team-scoped LEADER read → 403 → `useCollection` returns undefined → the picker rendered a **silently EMPTY** member list with **no error shown to the user**. ⚠ Note `useAuth:183` folds team-responsible teams into `coachTeamIds`, so `isCoach` is true for TRs — member 442 is a **TR of D2/D1**, which is how a role-`["user"]` account reached a coach-gated form. So this broke event invites for **every coach AND TR**, not a niche case. **Fix = permissions only** (no migration, no code, no frontend): added `wiedisync_active` (plain activation boolean, no PII) to `MEMBER_VISIBLE_FIELDS` + PERMISSIONS.md same commit. ⚠ **Two dead ends worth remembering**: (a) I first theorised `EventForm` was mounted unconditionally (`EventsPage:288`) and always firing a `limit:-1` all-members fetch — **wrong**: Radix/vaul portals don't `forceMount`, and `useMediaQuery` seeds from `matchMedia`, so the picker only mounts when the form is actually opened. Verified before shipping a pointless change. (b) The real reason a plain-looking `["user"]` hit it was the TR→coach fold, not a broken gate. **Verified**: dev setup-perms 521/0 → the exact failing query as a plain member returns **193 members** (was 403) → `db:smoke:dev` 26/26; prod setup-perms 521/0, confirmed via `directus_permissions` (KSCW Member ×2 + Team Responsible now carry the field; Public correctly does not). Perms take effect ONLY via `db:setup-perms:*` — pushing the branch does nothing, since permissions are not in migrations.
- **2026-07-13** **ClubDesk consistency check — 4 new checks + the team→group mapping moved out of code (migration 205; dev)** — the "Group check" card grew into a full **Consistency check** (7 collapsible sections, one `/clubdesk-group-sync` call, Excel worklist export). **New checks**: (1) `coach_no_group` — coaches missing their team's `'<group> (Trainer*in)'` token (12 on prod data; NB `Trainer*in` is the ONLY coaching role token ClubDesk carries — there is no team-responsible equivalent, so TRs are deliberately out of scope); (2) `fee_no_roster` — pays a *playing* Beitragskategorie but sits on no current-season roster, i.e. **billed to play while on no team** (166: **136 never rostered**, 30 lapsed). Bucketed never/lapsed/older from roster history so it's triageable instead of a flat 166-row dump; coach/TR duties annotated because a non-playing coach on a playing fee is a judgement call, not automatically an error. Non-playing kat (`Passivmitglied`, `Gratis`) excluded. (3) `unmapped_teams` — a **config guard**. **Migration 205** materialises `teams.clubdesk_group`, retiring the hardcoded inline SQL `CASE`: ⚠ the endpoint's comment claimed it was "keyed on teams.clubdesk_group (migration 201)" — **no such column and no such migration ever existed** (files jump 200→202); the mapping was 7 hardcoded team names, same failure class as the sv-sync allow-list. Three-state semantic so a new team can never be silently skipped: `NULL` = unconfigured → **flagged**, `''` = intentionally none (the 2 league umbrellas), else the token. Backfill reproduces the old CASE 1:1 — **verified by regression: missing=37, strays=24, no_group=78 identical before/after the switch**; 27 mapped + 2 umbrellas + 0 NULL. Registered in `directus_fields` so the mapping is fixable without a migration. **Consolidation**: all 7 signals now live on the sync page (the *workbench*); Data Health stays the *alarm* but the 4 new checks are pushed as **aggregate** rows (one each) — per-member rows would have added ~250 entries and drowned the page (same reason drift `fills` are aggregated); unmapped teams are the exception (rare, and each one silently blinds every other check, so one row apiece). ⚠ `db:setup-perms:dev` skipped (vault locked) — **no permission rows changed**, and the endpoint reads the new column via raw knex so no field perms are needed. i18n en+de.
- **2026-07-13** **ClubDesk group check — flag members with no group / a missing group (dev+prod)** — ClubDesk Gruppen are manual-only (CSV import treats them as a no-op), so they drift silently from the rosters. New `no_group` bucket on `GET /kscw/clubdesk-group-sync` (additive — Data Health destructures `{missing, strays, no_team_groups}` and is untouched): linked, non-muted, active members whose CD contact carries NO group token at all, annotated with current-season teams + Beitragskategorie. **This was invisible before** — `missing` only inspects members who are ON a team, so someone with no team AND no group was flagged by nothing. New "Group check" card on `/admin/clubdesk-sync` (`ClubdeskGroupCheck.tsx`): two tables — *No ClubDesk group* (no-group members, the ones WITH a team sorted first + amber-badged: they play but the register has them in nothing) and *Missing a group* (has groups, missing their team's; no-group members filtered out so nobody is double-listed) — plus an Excel worklist export (English headers). Read-only by design: the fix is manual in ClubDesk. Prod at ship time: **78 no-group (21 of them on a team), 16 missing, 24 strays**. i18n en+de only (this superadmin page has zero fr/it/gsw clubdesk keys by existing convention). No schema migration. ⚠ **Follow-up spotted in the data**: of the 57 no-group members WITHOUT a team, most sit on *playing* fee categories (BB Jugend Meisterschaft ×15, VB Schüler*in Meisterschaft ×10, VB Student*in ×8) — i.e. billed as players while on no roster and in no group. That's a `member_teams` gap, not a group gap; worth its own check.

Tokens cleared + dummy removed after.

- **2026-07-10** **Scorer-assign upload: survive a stale exceljs chunk** — user report: the "Upload corrected" on `/admin/scorer-assign` "does not work" (red error). Traced end-to-end with the user's actual file (`260710_kscw_scorer_assignment_vb_2026-27.xlsx`): the file is a clean 80-row export, and the upload parser applies **80/80** against live prod data (0 unmatched, 0 unknown teams) — so it's **not** a data/parse bug. The only throwable op in `handleUploadXlsx` is `await import('exceljs')` (a **930 kB lazy chunk** whose hash rotates every deploy); a tab left open across a deploy gets the CF Pages SPA fallback for the now-missing chunk → import throws → the **bare `catch` swallowed it into "Could not read the file"** and skipped the app-wide `maybeReloadOnStaleChunk` recovery every other lazy export uses (download worked earlier because its chunk was still cached — the download/upload asymmetry). Fix: import failure → `maybeReloadOnStaleChunk()` hard-reload to the current bundle (falls back to a new `uploadStale` "app updated, refresh" message if reload is on cooldown); genuine parse failures → `captureApiError()` (were silently discarded); + a `uploadNotReady` guard for the race where the localStorage-restored Upload button appears before the season's games finish loading (previously a confusing "no matches"). New i18n `uploadNotReady`/`uploadStale` ×5. Frontend-only, no schema/ext; build+eslint green. **dev+prod** (dev 2908cdbe, prod cherry-pick 4724355d).
- **2026-07-10** **CF Pages CSS-breaks-on-deploy poison fixed + repo/docs cleanup** — user hit "Refused to apply style from `.../assets/index-CIAnGfI5.css` because its MIME type ('text/html')" → whole app unstyled, broken in **every** browser, cleared only by a CF dashboard "Purge Everything". **Root cause**: CF Pages serves the SPA `index.html` fallback (`200 text/html`) for a *missing* `/assets/<hash>` (not-yet-propagated / reused hash during a deploy), and `public/_headers` stamps `Cache-Control: immutable` on everything matching `/assets/*` **by URL** — so the CF **edge** caches that `200 text/html immutable` under the real asset URL and serves it to all clients until a manual purge; `_headers` can't tell a real asset from a miss (same URL). **Dead ends (both tested on dev)**: `_redirects` can't return a `404` status (rule silently dropped → poison returns); a `public/404.html` makes CF serve it for ALL not-found → every SPA deep link 404s. **Fix**: Pages Function `functions/assets/[[path]].js` — `env.ASSETS.fetch()` the request; a 404 or `text/html` result (asset missing) → real `404` + `Cache-Control: no-store`; else pass the real asset through untouched (keeps immutable, still edge-cached). `public/_redirects` keeps only `/* /index.html 200`. **Verified on prod** `wiedisync.kscw.ch`: misses → `404 no-store`, real `index-CIAnGfI5.css` → `200 text/css immutable`, SPA routes → `200`. Documented in `INFRA.md → Troubleshooting`. Frontend-only, no schema/ext. **dev+prod** (dev c8020fce, prod merge 3614d834). **Cleanup same session**: purged the CF Pages deployment backlog (wiedisync had 600+; kept newest 5 + live prod on both `wiedisync` and `kscw-website`); pruned merged local `security-fixes` branch + `git gc` (73M→20M) on lenovoserver; shrank the large tracked `.md` docs by archiving historical content into `-archive.md` siblings (DEVLOG 240K→146K, IMPROVEMENTS 123K→3.7K, SECURITY 73K→18K, CHANGELOG 34K→14K, PERMISSIONS 38K→32K; INFRA shrunk locally into gitignored `INFRA-archive.md`, `.gitignore` `INFRA.md`→`INFRA*.md` so no infra secrets are committed).
- **2026-07-10** **Date pickers standardized app-wide + calendar un-squashed** — resolves the "what is this picker?" cosmetic flagged 2026-07-09 (event modal mixed native `<input type=date>` for start/end with the custom `DatePicker` for respond-by). **Root cause of the squash** (reproduced in an isolated vite+Playwright harness): the shared `Calendar` (`components/ui/calendar.tsx`) flex grid collapsed its columns inside the `DatePicker`'s `w-auto` popover (jammed days, uneven weekday headers) and an oversized `min-h` left dead space under short months. Fix: pin the month grid to explicit `w-[calc(7*var(--cell-size))]`, drop the `min-h`, tighten `--cell-size` 2.75→2.5rem — corrects **every** DatePicker site. **Standardization**: replaced all ~28 native `date`/`datetime-local` inputs across events, finance (7), game-scheduling (4), trainings, games, polls, js-export, audit-log, announcements, form-builder, and the generic admin record/field editors with the branded `DatePicker` (date-only) + a new `DateTimePicker` (`components/ui/DateTimePicker.tsx` — date + time field emitting the same `YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm` string a native datetime-local produces, so it's a true drop-in even for the raw Directus editors). Event start/end now match respond-by; a time field shows only when the event isn't all-day (new `startTime`/`endTime` state, split date-only + combine-on-submit). Added a **Clear** button to `DatePicker` (shown when a value is set) for parity with the native inputs it replaces. New i18n: `events.startTime/endTime`, `common.time/clear` (all 5 locales). Frontend-only, no schema/ext; build+lint(0 new) green; visual QA of the fixed calendar, DateTimePicker layout/format, timed-mode event form, and Clear button (light+dark). No version bump (polish). **dev+prod** (dev e75a76d0).

- **2026-07-09** **Scorer-assignment: count-based Legends rebalance (supersedes the 3:1 ratio)** — corrects a notation misread: Thamy's "3/4" meant the count "3 or 4", not the fraction ¾. An explicit D1/HU23 scorer bias was tested and **deliberately omitted**: it overshoots (D1→8 while *starving* HU23→3, re-concentrating load — the exact Nadine/Livi overload this relieves). Honest gap: D1 lands +1 not +2 (greedy engine can't smoothly hit +2 — it's +1 or an overshoot +3; use a dropdown override / `duty_credit -1` for the exact count). i18n: generic `reason_teamRoleBias` replaces the two `reason_legends*` keys, `ruleVbSoftLegends` reworded (5 locales). - **2026-07-09** **Mute cosmetic "game updated" notification spam (migration 199)** — `trg_games_notify` fired a "Game updated: … on DD.MM.YY" push to a team's WHOLE roster on every non-completion/cancellation `games` UPDATE. sv-sync rewrites a game row whenever any of its `COMPARE_FIELDS` drift — which includes `referees_json`, `sets_json`, `league`, `round`, and in-progress scores — so the SV feed reshuffling referee assignments / partial set data between syncs re-notified the *same* fixture (H3 vs Voléro 02.10.26, H3 vs RTG 18.12.26, …) every few hours (user-reported: "why still updating?"). Fix: gate the `game_updated` (ELSE) branch on an actual reschedule — `IF NEW.date IS NOT DISTINCT FROM OLD.date AND NEW.time IS NOT DISTINCT FROM OLD.time THEN RETURN NEW` — so referee/set/league/round/score churn writes the row silently; only a date/time change notifies. Cancellation is untouched (separate `status → cancelled` → `game_deleted` branch still fires), as is completion (`game_result`) and creation (`game_created`). Applied at the trigger so it covers sv-sync, bp-sync AND manual reschedules uniformly. A pure venue/hall-only change is intentionally muted too (SV hall is frozen anyway; can add `hall` to the gate later if manual venue moves should ping). Also cleared the existing backlog: **2066** `game_updated` rows (2018 unread) deleted from prod `notifications` for a clean slate. Migration 199 (schema-only, `CREATE OR REPLACE`); `SCHEMA.sql` updated to match. Deploy gotcha: the first chained `db:snapshot:prod && db:migrate:prod` reported "✓ Up to date" and silently no-op'd (transient `psql`-over-ssh read during the busy snapshot window made `getApplied()` return a bad tracker set) — verified prod had NOT applied (no tracker row, gate absent), re-ran `db:migrate:prod` standalone → applied cleanly. **dev+prod** (dev fa5624f8, prod cherry-pick f17be51e). No frontend/version bump (backend-only).
- **2026-07-09** **Stale-version recovery: cache-busting reload** — the "A new version is available / Reload" screen (`chunkReload.ts` recovery, shown after a deploy rotates hashed chunk names and a stale tab can't import a now-gone chunk → CF SPA-fallback HTML rejected by `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`) could get *stuck*: `reloadNow()` + the auto-reload paths used `window.location.reload()`, which a privacy extension / corporate proxy can satisfy from a stale cached index.html, so repeated reloads kept returning the same broken document (user-reported: "Reload doesn't work"). Fix: new internal `hardReload()` navigates via `location.replace()` with a unique `?_v=<ts36>` cache-bust param — a fresh URL cannot be served from any HTTP cache, so the current index.html (which only references chunks that exist) is guaranteed to load; `location.replace` (not reload) also keeps the broken state out of back-history. `stripCacheBustParam()` strips it synchronously in `main.tsx` before React/the router mount, so it never enters routing or accumulates in the URL bar. Every reload path routed through it: auto `reloadOnce` (vite:preloadError / bootstrap catch / global error+unhandledrejection listeners), user-clicked `reloadNow`, and both generic `SentryFallback` (App) / `SchedulingFallback` "Reload page" buttons. The 10s cooldown loop-guard is unchanged. Diagnosis note: the live prod deploy was verified self-consistent at incident time (index.html `no-store` → entry `index-CFrxQ8__.js` → App + all ~30 boot chunks 200 `application/javascript`) and the push SW is fetch-handler-less, so the screenshot was a transient deploy-propagation race — this change just makes the recovery bulletproof so a stuck client can always escape. Frontend-only, no schema/endpoint; build+lint green, 582/582 unit tests pass. **dev+prod**.
- **2026-07-09** **v1.30.0 — Team filter on multi-team event rosters** — the participation roster modal (`ParticipationRosterModal.tsx`) gains a multi-select **team** dropdown (next to the status filter) that shows only for events with 2+ invited teams (hidden for single-team games/trainings and club-wide events). Selecting team(s) narrows the WHOLE modal — summary counts, member list, waitlist, coaching-staff section and all three exports (CSV/PNG/PDF) — to those teams; default "All teams". Design: fetches key on the full roster (`rosterMemberIds`), so switching teams recomputes counts from the already-loaded participations with **no refetch** (`useCollection` keys structurally). `useMultiTeamMembers` extended with a `teamsByMember` map built from the RAW junction rows (before the member-dedupe drops the 2nd team) so a player shared across two invited teams shows under either; staff narrowing via a new per-team coach/TR map (`leadershipTeamsByMember`). i18n `allTeams`/`teamsCount`/`filterByTeam` ×5. Frontend-only, no schema/endpoint. **dev+prod** (dev 470803bb, prod cherry-pick eb3295ce; shipped surgically off a shared tree carrying unrelated in-flight scorer/schema work — only the 10 feature files committed).
- **2026-07-09** **Scorer-assignment: cup games are free slots** — Züri Cup + Swiss/Mobiliar Volley Cup home games are the playing team's own responsibility (club ruling, confirmed by Thamy) — the assigner must never summon another team for cup duty. New `isCupGame(league)` (wide `/cup|pokal|coupe|coppa/` net — `detectCupMatch` only knows the two canonical names but the feed labels the Swiss cup "Mobiliar Volley Cup") short-circuits the assignment loop into a new `'cup'` `GameAssignment` mode: rendered as a read-only light-blue "Cup — playing team's own duty" free-slot row, assigned to nobody, excluded from team duty counts, roll-out, upload re-apply, and the red "no assignment" flag; xlsx export gets a light-blue `'cup'` status. `cupOwnDuty` i18n (all 5 locales); 1 new test (14/14 pass). Frontend-only. **dev+prod** (dev 040bfe0b, prod cherry-pick 6a02709e). Related tuning notes: Thamy's "1-2 more scorer for D1/HU23" is now mostly a side-effect of the Legends de-bias (D1 has 12 scorer licences, HU23-1 has 18 — they were out-competed by Legends' old +8) plus the manual `duty_credit` knob (negative = more duties); the Nadine/Livi "4 derby slots" is real-world *individual* load (both single-rostered on D1, no double-count) that the team-level assigner can't spread — use manual credit / dropdown overrides to move whole derbys off D1.
- **2026-07-09** **Scorer-assignment: Legends role-lean corrected** — Thamy clarified the Legends rule was backwards: instead of the flat `+8` bias *toward scorer*, Legends should mostly do the easier **scoreboard** duty (~3:1 scoreboard:scorer, soft — "not a hard requirement") and still take ~1/4 scorer. Replaced the flat bonus in `AssignmentAlgorithm.ts` with a ratio-aware **see-saw** on Legends' own running scorer/scoreboard tallies (new `roleCounts` split, threaded through `scoreTeam`, fed by both fresh and pre-existing scorer/scoreboard assignments): while it still owes scoreboard toward `3 × (scorer + 1)` → scoreboard `+12` / scorer `−12`; once caught up the sign flips so it picks up its scorer share. Symmetric ±12 only breaks ties between comparable candidates, so fair-rotation still governs total load — only the mix shifts; combined/referee roles untouched. i18n: signed `reason_legendsScorer` + new `reason_legendsScoreboard`, reworded `ruleVbSoftLegends` (all 5 locales); 2 new deterministic tests (lean direction + catch-up, 13/13 pass). Frontend-only, no schema/endpoint. **dev+prod** (dev 77e05d77, prod cherry-pick 5cabe0a9).
- **2026-07-09** **Members: trim leading/trailing whitespace** — a trailing space had been saved onto member 461 ("Irini " / "Zoubos ") via the Directus admin UI. Two-part fix: (1) **prevention** — new `trimMemberStrings` filter in `kscw-hooks` on `members.items.create`/`.update`, registered before the priv-strip/dup-email filters, trims the ends of every string field in the payload on every items-API write (admin UI + member app), covering names + email/phone/address/etc.; `.trim()` also strips NBSP/zero-width, not just ASCII; internal whitespace preserved; raw-knex paths (registration already trims at ingestion, ClubDesk = source of truth) untouched. (2) **cleanup** — migration 198 (data-only, idempotent, dynamic over every text/varchar column on `members`) trims existing rows. Verified on dev: 461 → `[Irini]`/`[Zoubos]`, 0 rows with name whitespace remaining. Deployed surgically (only pending migration was 198; prod already carried the other unmerged dev extension changes so `ext:deploy:prod` shipped only this hook). **dev+prod**.
- **2026-07-09** **Hotfix: Hallenplan "Too many re-renders" crash** — `/admin/hallenplan` white-screened (Sentry, prod). Root cause in `useHallenplanData`: the anti-flash "hold last consistent slots" guard did `if (!showingStale && stableSlots !== computedSlots) setStableSlots(computedSlots)` during render, but `computedSlots` was a NEW reference every render (its input `rawSlots = flattenM2MTeams(...)` builds a fresh array + fresh objects each call), so the guard never settled → infinite setState loop. Fixed by making the memo inputs referentially stable: memoized `rawSlots`, and swapped every `?? []` fallback for a shared module-level `EMPTY` constant (a fresh `[]` literal per render was also unstable while loading). Frontend-only. **dev+prod**.
- **2026-07-09** **Hall-conflict hook: same-sport + A+B + full-cover cancel; derby & D4 hall data** — `game-training-shorten.js` reworked: (1) **same-sport scoping** — a volleyball game no longer shortens/cancels a basketball training (or vice-versa) even when they share a hall (KWI C); the old hall-only match had been cross-sport-shortening 6 BB trainings via VB games (restored on prod); (2) **honors `additional_halls`** so an A+B combo (e.g. the H1↔H3 derby occupying KWI A+B) affects trainings in *both* halls; (3) **cancels fully-covered trainings** (a training sitting entirely inside a game block, e.g. D1/H2 under the 20:00 A+B derby), restorable; (4) **normalizes rotted KWI A/B combos** (`additional_halls` pointing back at the primary hall → complementary half). Prod data corrected directly: both derby home rows (408/410) → true A+B `additional_halls=[KWI B]`; D1 (2659) + H2 (2645) trainings on 23.09 cancelled (silenced, restorable); D4's 7 Thu home games KWI C→KWI A (their slot — the mis-hall that caused the Lions D1 BB collision). Rule learned: on Fridays KWI C is H1's training, so D3/H3 play A/B (their KWI-A/B game halls were already right). these are vb_ VM-synced games, so a **hall freeze** now stops the SV feed overwriting an existing game's `hall` (`FREEZE_HALLS` in `sv-sync.js`; creates still take the feed hall; flip off once SV renames the halls to be unambiguous — user is requesting that). Also **VB Döltschi fix**: BB owns Döltschi 1 (21 trainings), VB trains only in Döltschi 2 (14) — but the feed dumps all VB Döltschi games into Döltschi 1 (only hall with `sv_hall_id`). Moved the 15 VB Döltschi games (DU23-1/HU20/HU23-1) → Döltschi 2, and re-pointed `sv_hall_id` 153 from Döltschi 1 → Döltschi 2 so future feed games land there (VB court; BB stays in Döltschi 1). Hook + sync deployed surgically (single files; Directus runs src/ directly). **dev+prod**.
- **2026-07-09** **Scorer-assignment: corrected-file upload + live notes + game no./weekday** — export now carries the Swiss Volley / Basketplan **game number** (`games.game_id`) as the first column plus a **weekday** column; an **Upload corrected** button re-applies an edited `.xlsx` to the draft (matched by game number, team names → ids, mode-clamped so a stray column can't pollute roll-out, unmatched/unknown-team rows reported). - **2026-07-08** **Registrations get an authoritative `member` FK — ClubDesk status badges resolve ID-first (migration 194-registrations-member-fk; dev+prod).** User rule: "lookup should be by ID". The approval hook always computed the member id (`createMemberFromRegistration` returns it on both create and link-to-existing paths) and threw it away, so the Anmeldungen CD badge re-derived reg→member by email+first-name heuristics — false "No member record" on divergent-email rows (Neo Paladino: reg under the mother's email, member row under his own; interim fix same day: unique exact-name fallback + widened `nachname` selects the fallback silently needed). Now: hook stamps `registrations.member`; **migration 194** = FK (ON DELETE SET NULL) + two-pass unique-candidate backfill (email+first-name-prefix → exact-last-name; ambiguous stays NULL) + `directus_fields`/`directus_relations` rows — **16/16 backfilled on prod**, 14/15 dev (the 1 = a pending reg, correct); `cdStatusForRegistration` resolves by FK first (heuristics = legacy fallback only), and its unlinked branch now checks `clubdesk_export.wiedisync_id` (uuid + pre-184 numeric id) BEFORE any email/name scan (`matched_by: 'wiedisync_id'`, ambiguity-free). Also fixed en route: prod "Sync down" failing with `flock: Permission denied` — the 2026-07-07 rsync to `/opt/clubdesk-sync/` copied git's 100644 mode over the root 755 `clubdesk-sync.sh`; VPS chmod'd + all five `clubdesk-*.sh` now 100755 in git; INFRA Troubleshooting entry (local). Deploys: dev `db:deploy:dev` + surgical 2-file ext copy; prod snapshot→migrate→surgical (tree carried parallel expense WIP — no full rsync). ⚠ Open: parallel session's uncommitted `194-members-js-id.sql` collides with the committed 194 — renumber theirs →195 + tracker UPDATE on both DBs; `setup-perms` not re-run (rbw locked; 194 changes no permissions).

- **2026-07-08** **Hallenplan: future-slot leak + closure collapse + BB H1 Tue hours (dev+prod).** Four fixes off the calendar week view. (1) **Horizon leak** — past the ~12-week training-generation horizon no concrete `trainings` row exists yet, so every recurring template survived suppression and rendered as a green claimable "Frei" slot (01.10/02.10 onward); `buildFreedRecurringSlots` now skips freed-marking beyond `today + FREED_HORIZON_WEEKS` (mirrors `INDEFINITE_HORIZON_WEEKS` in slot-cascade.js), so un-generated future slots show as occupied (planned) trainings, not grabbable. Threaded through `mergeVirtualSlots` (optional arg → 16 characterization tests unchanged). (2) **Ghost-slot closure block** — 11 orphan `hall IS NULL` trainings (D4 18:30 Jul–Aug + 1 team-80 row) were invisible virtual slots that still counted as day-content and defeated the all-halls-closed collapse, so Sommerferien weeks rendered a "Closed" block in every hall column instead of one merged "All halls" block; `trainingToVirtualSlot` now drops hall-less trainings and `WeekSlotView.allClosedDays` ignores them (defence-in-depth). (3) **Schulferien for the two new halls** — 13 Hohlstr./14 Freilager had zero closures; copied hall 4's curated 46-row `school_holidays` set to both (prod+dev, 92 rows). (4) **Vorstand** — BB H1 Tue keeps KWI A+B until 20:00: slots 69/70 + 13 future concrete trainings end-time 19:30→20:00 (game-shortened row 2566 preserved at 19:15, notify silenced), prod+dev. Frontend cherry-picked to prod (`c1ceb731`, isolated worktree — parallel session's clubdesk commit `c33dd732` deliberately left off prod); 21 unit tests (5 new), build+lint clean. Follow-ups open: 11 orphan hall-less trainings carry placeholder RSVPs (0 attending) — deletion pending user call; BB H1 KWI B 20:00 overlaps D2 slot 71 (19:30–21:30) 19:30–20:00 — flagged to Vorstand.

Build + eslint clean, 535 unit tests pass. No version bump (cosmetic).

- **2026-07-08** **Expense TK (Sport-Admin) confirmation flow + member reimbursement-IBAN gate (dev+prod).** Per-section confirmation on expense reimbursements (`finance_expenses`, migration **192** — additive/idempotent): 7 new columns — `section` (vb/bb/club, derived at submit from the submitter's `members.sektion`: Volleyball→vb / Basketball→bb / KSCW→club, existing rows backfilled), `member_already_paid`, `tk_confirmed_at` + `tk_confirmed_by_name/email`, `tk_already_paid`, `tk_note` — all registered in `directus_fields`. Two new endpoints in `expense-upload.js` (raw-knex + `writeUserLog` actor capture, **self-gating so NO `setup-permissions.mjs` change**): `GET /kscw/expenses/tk-queue` (section-scoped — `vb_admin`→vb / `bb_admin`→bb; board/finance→all incl. null-section) and `POST /kscw/expenses/:id/tk-confirm` (`{confirmed,already_paid,note}`; stamps the confirmation once, keeps the original actor on later edits, un-confirm clears). Informational only — never touches the paid/rejected lifecycle, the auto-payout, or the ClubDesk mirror (per user: "must not clash with ClubDesk"). Frontend: new **`TkExpensesPage`** (`/finance/tk-expenses`, `TkRoute` gated on vb_admin/bb_admin/finance; nav item for section TKs) with per-row Confirm button + Already-paid toggle + note; treasurer **Expenses tab** gains a TK-confirmation column (confirmed-by/date, "Paid by section", member-already-paid, TK note); member **expense upload** now **requires a valid IBAN** (client + server), relabels the field "Reimburse on this account" (prefilled from `members.iban`), and adds an "Already paid?" checkbox (`member_already_paid`). `EXPENSE_FIELDS` split into member-safe (`MY_EXPENSE_FIELDS`, within `MEMBER_POLICY` field scope) vs board (extended; Vorstand/Finance read `*`) so the member's own-view read never requests unpermitted TK columns. Also fixed a **finance-tab flash-of-empty race** (`FinancePage`): the boot gate keyed off the transactions query's `isLoading`, but that query is disabled until fiscal years load and a disabled react-query reports `isLoading:false` → the "No data" empty state rendered for one paint before the dashboard; replaced with a `bootLoading` flag over fiscal-years+accounts+invoices+transactions. i18n ×5. Verified on dev: migration applied + backfilled (1 vb / 1 bb), routes 401/404-gated, authenticated tk-queue/tk-confirm round-trip + audit log (`expense_tk_confirm`/`_unconfirm`). **Prod:** migration 192 applied (snapshot first), `expense-upload.js` surgically deployed (src-only extension; the parallel session's uncommitted `clubdesk-update.js` WIP deliberately NOT shipped), frontend via dev→prod merge. `npm run build` + eslint clean, 131 endpoint tests pass. (191 auto-shorten confirmed already fully on prod — hook sha matches, registered.)

- **2026-07-08** **ClubDesk sync-up: UPDATE push switched to `[Id]`-keyed name-less rows (supersedes the same-day name-echo fix; backend dev+prod).** Spike v2 against the live import wizard (preview probes + user-approved throwaway contact `create→[Id]-update→delete`, register untouched) refuted the old "no ID upsert" assumption and established the ground truth now tabled in `INFRA.md → ClubDesk import-wizard semantics`: (1) a `[Id]` CSV column is consumed as the **record identity** (never field-mapped; a known `[Id]` upserts regardless of name/email; only carried columns touched — all others byte-identical after a real commit); (2) an **unknown `[Id]` silently hard-aborts the ENTIRE import** (dialog closes, no summary, nothing written); (3) an **empty mapped cell never blanks** the stored value (settles the long-open empty-cell question; echo-back + blank-risk guards kept as defense-in-depth); (4) the custom Wiedisync ID field is NOT a match key; (5) `Veränderte` counts matched rows. Implementation (`clubdesk-update.js`): `CD_PUSH_CONTACT_HEADERS` extracted; UPDATE set = `['[Id]', …contact]` (name-less — immune to ALL name drift, can never rename a register contact), CREATE set = `['Vorname','Nachname', …contact, …extras]` (byte-identical to before); `buildPushCsv` emits `clubdesk_id` at col 0 for updates; the same-day `cd_match_*` name-echo removed; **new stale-link guard** in `/up` (skips + reports `skipped_stale_link` for members whose `clubdesk_id` has no `clubdesk_export` row — one stale id would otherwise abort the whole ~700-row push; runs BEFORE `pushMembers` so a skipped member never enters `up_member_ids`/loses its pending flag); dispatcher dup-guard kept as regression backstop (message updated + copy synced to `/opt/clubdesk-sync/`). Tests rewritten for the 13-col layout. Hand-fixes on prod (birthdate-confirmed): member 232 Mathias→**Oliv** Gutscher, member 409 Felix→**Edgar** Stinson (CD contact was right, wiedisync first name wrong — Felix b.1985 = father #533, Edgar b.2017 = #409, Elwin b.2019 = #566, family shares one email). - **2026-07-07** **Dues-run guard extended to ClubDesk-mirror invoices (double-bill fix).** The dues preview/issue idempotency guard only counted NATIVE invoices (`dues_run IS NOT NULL`) — ClubDesk-mirror rows (`source='clubdesk'`, `dues_run` NULL per migration 138) were invisible, so a treasurer could natively re-bill a cohort ClubDesk had already invoiced; reachable at scale since the same-day member-create pass added ~76 `Passivmitglied` rows to the dues cohorts. **Backend** (`finance.js` `resolveDuesCohort` + `/dues-runs/issue`): new `clubdesk_billed` per-member flag — a member holding a non-`Storniert` mirror invoice **with a non-empty `fee_category`** for the fiscal year is excluded from `billable` (fee_category vocabulary == `members.beitragskategorie`, so a categorised mirror invoice IS a dues bill; blank fee_category = other invoice kinds, doesn't block; `Entwurf`/`Abgeschrieben` still block — drafted/written-off ≠ unbilled). Issue re-checks the mirror set inside the advisory-lock txn too (the nightly finance sync can land mirrors between preview and issue) and reports `skipped_clubdesk_billed`. **Frontend** (`DuesRunManager.tsx` + `useFinance.ts` types): purple `clubdeskBilled` row badge + preview-summary note; de/en keys (`duesStatusClubdeskBilled`, `duesClubdeskBilledNote`) — the dues UI is de/en like the rest of the module. tsc + eslint + 465 unit tests green. **Deploy**: `ext:deploy:dev` → `ext:deploy:prod` (extension), frontend dev→prod.

No permission change (games.read `*`, LEADER update `*`). **Migration 183** makes `stats_games_missing_schreiber` HU20-aware (label "Schiedsrichter" not "Anzeiger", flags a missing referee), recreated `WITH (security_invoker=true)`. **Algorithm** (`AssignmentAlgorithm.ts`, rewritten): new referee mode for HU20 (scorer + referee, never combined even at Döltschi/4L/5L); **dropped the scorer-licence HARD rule** (team needs no licenced scorer) AND the member-level self-claim licence gate in `ScorerRow` (else a non-licenced team assigned scorer duty is unfillable); kept Legends +8 / HU20-Täfeler +15; **excluded MiniVB + DU20** as providers. **Member surface**: `ScorerRow` referee role end-to-end (self-claim no-licence, confirm cols, delegation union in `types`), `ScorerPage` visibility gates include `referee_member`/`referee_duty_team`. **Confirm-actor + delegation hooks** gain the referee role. Reverted the earlier `/scorer` sport-toggle-always-visible change (user: not needed). 4 vitest cases (`AssignmentAlgorithm.test.ts`) cover HU20 referee mode / MiniVB+DU20 exclusion / no-licence assignment. i18n `scorer` + `scorerAssign` in 5 locales. **Deploy**: dev (`db:migrate:dev` 182+183 → `ext:deploy:dev` restart → push). Prod: `db:migrate:prod` (182+183) → `ext:deploy:prod` (hook + restart to serve new fields) → merge `dev`→`prod` → `db:baseline:prod`.

New `__tests__/import-clubdesk-csv.test.mjs` (emit-sql structural invariants incl. paren-balance guard; `test:scripts` 30/30 green). ⚠ Operator notes: the ~76 new `Passivmitglied` rows enter native dues cohorts but ClubDesk already bills passives and the dues-run guard only counts native invoices — check ClubDesk-mirror invoices before any passive dues run (INFRA.md ClubDesk section has the warning); suspected-duplicate report currently surfaces only in the VPS cron log. **Deploy**: change is inert until rsync'd to `/opt/clubdesk-sync/`; one-off prod SQL (staging-freshness-gated passes tail + archived-team-1 `active` revert) prepared for the backfill without waiting for Saturday's cron.

- **2026-07-07** **v1.24.0 — Club stats season-scoped (migration 181, dev+prod).** The Club-stats page aggregated Schreiber coverage + W/L results across ALL seasons (views had no season filter), so at the 2026/27 start it was dominated by the finished 2025/26 data ("old season data"). **Migration 181** adds a `season` dimension to `stats_schreiber_coverage` (`SELECT g.season` + `GROUP BY … , g.season` → one row per team+season; recreated `WITH (security_invoker = true)` to keep the 004/072 control — a CASCADE recreate silently reverts it). `stats_game_results` already carried `season`. **Frontend** (`ClubStatsPage.tsx`): a season `<select>` (dark-mode bg) next to the sport toggle, defaulting to `getCurrentSeason()` (Jun-1 cutover) and falling back to the latest season with data when the current one is empty; effective-season derived (no setState-in-effect — eslint `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` gate). Only Schreiber coverage + results are season-filtered; roster/members/participation/missing-schreiber stay current/rolling. **Deploy**: migration applied dev (`db:migrate:dev`) then prod (`db:migrate:prod`) BEFORE the frontend merge (schema-first — else `schreiber[].season` is undefined and the section renders empty); `SCHEMA.sql` re-baselined from prod. No permission delta (`setup-perms` skipped; endpoint is admin-gated, view is `security_invoker`). Pre-existing `fetchStats` on-mount lint left as-is (not a regression).

- **2026-07-07** **v1.23.0 — surfaced the pre-built Scorer-assignment admin page in the Admin nav (commit on `dev`, frontend-only, dev→prod).** The auto-assignment page `/admin/scorer-assign` (`ScorerAssignPage.tsx`, ~480 lines: VB + BB engines, editable per-game duty-team dropdowns, save-all, team summary) was fully built but **never linked into either nav registry** (git `-S` confirms it was never added, not removed), so it was unreachable. - **2026-07-07** **Error-log triage: archive/important buttons, bulk actions, class-level mute rules (commit `d3e64348`, migration 179, dev+prod).** The Error Logs page was collecting non-bug noise nobody could act on — mistyped-password logins (`auth_error` "Invalid user credentials") and transient mobile "Load failed" network drops (`network_error`). Three-part fix: **(1) Source** — `captureAuthError` (`src/lib/sentry.ts`) now carves out `login` + "Invalid user credentials" (logs at `warn`, skips Sentry), mirroring the existing token-expired carve-out, so a wrong password never pages Sentry or logs at error level. The transient network case was already carved out. **(2) Schema (migration 179)** — new `error_mute_rules` table (`event` + case-insensitive message substring, `enabled`, actor) that hides a WHOLE category from the default view, unlike per-hash `error_annotations` which only hide one occurrence (hash = md5 of ts|event|error, so noise recurs daily with a fresh hash). Raw-knex-only like `error_annotations`, no `directus_fields` registration. Seeded idempotently with the two known-noise categories. **(3) Backend** — `GET /admin/error-logs` applies enabled mute rules (an explicit `important` annotation still wins over a category mute); new `GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /admin/error-logs/mute-rules` (requireAdmin + `writeUserLog` actor capture). **(4) Frontend** — per-row Archive/Important/Reopen buttons, row-checkbox bulk-archive, "Mute all like this" inline form, a mute-rules management panel, and the "Show solved" toggle relabelled "Show archived" (reveals solved + muted). en/de i18n. Also archived today's three noise entries on prod via `annotate-bulk` before the fix. **Deploy**: dev `db:migrate:dev` (179 ✓, seeds verified) → push (frontend) → `ext:deploy:dev` → CRUD round-trip verified. Prod: `db:migrate:prod` → `ext:deploy:prod`. `setup-perms` skipped (no permission delta — endpoints are admin-only via `requireAdmin`, table is internal raw-knex). No version bump (user opted out of changelog for this).

- **2026-07-07** **Two mail forwards that couldn't land, both fixed by not forwarding (commit `0898a1df`, migration 178, dev+prod).** Root pattern: **Migadu transparent forwarding keeps the original `From:`, so any forward from a strict-auth sender is refused/quarantined at the receiver — silently** (nothing in Pending/Spam, refused at SMTP; the forward leg isn't even in Migadu's traffic logs). **(1) `volleyball@spielplanung.kscw.ch` → Google Group `vb_spieplanung_kscw@googlegroups.com`**: forward kept `From: gs-mk@svrz.ch` (svrz.ch = M365, SPF `-all`, NO DMARC) → Google Groups spoof-**rejected** it; the July 5 "AW: Döltschi Gym" never posted. Proof was in the group itself — only soft-auth senders (gmail/swissonline/proton/own domain) ever posted, no `svrz.ch`-origin msg ever did. **Fix = "option 4" remailer** (`scheduling-mailbox.js` `repostInboundToGroup`, wired into the 10-min `runMailboxSync` cron): re-mails qualifying inbound **AS the mailbox** (DKIM-aligned for `spielplanung.kscw.ch` → authenticates → posts), `Reply-To` = original sender, forwarded-header block, attachments incl. inline CID preserved, threaded; scope = **human correspondence only** (skips amazonses/no-reply/bounce); loop-safe (skips self/group/`List-Id`/`X-Google-Group-Id`/own `X-KSCW-Group-Repost` + Message-ID dedup); reuses the existing `EMAIL_SMTP_*` SES transport. **Migration 178** adds `scheduling_emails.group_reposted_at` (cron bookkeeping) + **seals the backlog** so first run doesn't dump 60 days. Gated by `SCHEDULING_GROUP_REPOST_COMMIT` (**prod=1** actually posts, **dev unset** = dry-run that logs "would post" + stamps, so dev never double-posts off the shared mailbox); `SCHEDULING_GROUP_ADDRESS` (code-defaulted), `SCHEDULING_GROUP_REPOST_WINDOW_DAYS=3`. **(2) Expense reimbursements never reached the treasurer**: `expense-upload.js` mailed only `finance@mail.kscw.ch` → forwarded to `radovanovic.radomir@kscw.ch` = **ClubDesk** (`kscw.ch`, DMARC `p=quarantine`) → quarantined the softfail forwarded copy. **Fix**: send **directly** to the treasurer's real inbox via SES too — new env `FINANCE_NOTIFY_EMAILS` (default `radomir.radovanovic.b@gmail.com`, the Gmail approval mail already reaches him at), `finance@` kept as archive CC, **empty on dev**. **Deploy**: dev `deploy:dev` (migration 178 applied, ext restart, dry-run validated — the July 5 svrz.ch msg logged "would post"). Prod: `db:migrate:prod` (snapshot first) → ext staged (no restart) → **user deleted the Migadu group forward** (prevents dups) → `SCHEDULING_GROUP_REPOST_COMMIT=1` added to `/opt/directus-kscw/.env` → **container recreated** (env-file change) → live-validated: un-sealed prod row 11401 → 00:10 cron **`posted` the July 5 message to the group for real** (SES accepted; user confirms Google display). INFRA.md env tables updated (finance §, mailbox §). `setup-perms` skipped (no permission delta — the new column is internal raw-knex bookkeeping). ⚠ Working tree carries unrelated parallel `src/` WIP (ErrorLogsPage + nav) — committed only my 3 files.

- **2026-07-07** **Expense reimbursements: in-app status queue + member notifications (v1.22.0, migration 177).** The `/finance/expense` upload was email-only (nothing persisted → uploader had no status view, finance had no in-app queue). New `finance_expenses` collection (**migration 177** — renumbered from a stale WIP 175 after 176 landed on dev; `member` FK is `ON DELETE RESTRICT` per migration 174's audit-preservation rule) gives every submission a `pending → paid | rejected` lifecycle. `/kscw/expenses/submit` now persists the row (email kept as belt-and-braces side channel; a mail failure no longer 500s once the row is saved, killing the duplicate-on-retry path). New finance-gated `PATCH /kscw/expenses/:id` (status/detail/note edits) wrapped in a **transaction + `forUpdate` row lock** so a double-fired "paid" can't mint two payouts; on `→paid` it auto-creates the linked `finance_payouts` QR-bill row (CHF + valid CH/LI IBAN + complete address, else skipped with a machine reason code), on leaving `paid` it cancels the payout, and edits to a paid expense sync the payout snapshot. Member notified in-app + email + push (5 locales, Swiss `dd.mm.yyyy` dates, localized card labels). `GET /kscw/expenses/:id/receipt` streams the file (owner-or-finance). Frontend: "My submissions" table on the upload page (own-member-filtered — a finance viewer's read-all grant would otherwise leak all rows into the member view; same fix applied to `useMyPayouts`), new **Expenses** tab in `/admin/finance`. Perms: member read own (field-whitelisted — actor columns endpoint-only), finance+Vorstand read-all, writes endpoint-only. Went through the xhigh workflow code review (43 agents) → **all 15 confirmed findings fixed** before ship. Build+eslint+node --check green, smoke test extended (`finance_expenses (own)`). Deploy: dev (`deploy:dev` schema-first) → prod (`deploy:prod`).

- **2026-07-07** **Wiedisync ID round-trip — the sync is now ID-anchored (prod, endpoints+importer+scraper deployed).** User created a `Wiedisync ID` custom text field in ClubDesk. Verified ClubDesk's import CANNOT match on any id (built-in `[Id]` has no import field; `Benutzer-Id` is the login username) — so update-matching stays email+name + the commit guard. But the field is perfect for the DOWN-link: sync-UP now pushes `members.id` into it on every create+update (14th push col, wiedisync-owned, never echoed); the down-sync captures it (`clubdesk_export.wiedisync_id` + HEADER_TO_COL + TARGET_COLS) and a NEW **ID-first linker pass** links `members.clubdesk_id` by an EXACT `wiedisync_id=members.id` match, running BEFORE the licence/email heuristics. This closes the create round-trip (up → ClubDesk assigns `[Id]` → down-link) with zero name/email/accent ambiguity. **Proven**: unlinked member 20 (Bäbler, clubdesk_id→NULL) → sync-down RE-LINKED him to 1000680 via his Wiedisync ID. Backfilled all 686 linked contacts (veraendert=686, neu=0; the guard correctly refused the 687th = Kaspar's un-round-trippable `ć` name). - **2026-07-07** **19 mangled ClubDesk duplicate contacts cleaned + root-cause dup-guard shipped (prod).** Discovered 19 garbage duplicate contacts with accented chars corrupted to `?` (C?dric, B?rkli, Cl?ver, Jo?l, M?ller, …), each shadowing a correct linked twin (all 'Kein Mitglied', unlinked). **Root cause**: the up-dispatcher (`clubdesk-member-up-dispatch.sh`) previewed the UPDATE set but only checked the preview *reached its summary* — it never inspected `neu`. When a name failed to match its stored contact (ClubDesk decodes some CP1252 high-bytes to `?` on import), ClubDesk offered to CREATE it and the dispatcher committed anyway → mangled dup. My push pipeline itself is clean end-to-end (verified: shell→up_csv keeps `c3bc`, dispatcher pull+iconv→`fc`); the gap was the missing commit guard. **Fix**: dispatcher now REFUSES the update-set commit whenever the preview reports `neu>0` (an update push must only match, never create) — mechanism-independent, catches any future encoding/name-drift regression (repo prod `4a0996a4` + deployed to VPS). **Cleanup**: wrote a conservative delete-scraper (exact name-cell match on the mangled `Nachname Vorname`, excludes Kaspar's real `Curavi?`, verifies each row gone before counting) → deleted all 19; re-pushed the licence/officials/sex data (Offiziellen Lizenz, Schiedsrichter, Lizenznummer, Lizenzart, Geschlecht) that had gone to the dups back onto the 17 correct contacts (veraendert=17, neu=0). End state: 1149 contacts, only Kaspar's legitimate `Curavi?` mangled (ć genuinely can't be CP1252).

(2) `deriveGruppen`: VB scorer → `VB Schreiber*innen` (correct group name; referee group notes removed). (3) `deriveOffiziellenLizenz`: VB SR dropped — a VB referee is AUTOMATICALLY a scorer (VOLLEYBALL only; BB referees are not auto table-officials) → `scorer_vb||referee_vb → VB SC`; importer sets `scorer_vb=true` wherever `referee_vb=true`. (4) **Down-sync round-trip**: importer reads the `VB Schiedsrichter*innen`/`Schiedsrichter BB` group membership → sets `referee_vb`/`referee_bb` (set-true), so the profile's existing read-only Licences card shows 'Schiedsrichter-Lizenz' (no frontend change needed — `licencesOf()` already renders it). - **2026-07-06** **Finance board fix: phantom `team_name` in `INVOICE_FIELDS` broke `/admin/finance` for the finance role (dev `288b33f1`).** Error-log check surfaced 8× `You don't have permission to access field "team_name" in collection "finance_invoices" or it does not exist` (prod, finance user Radomir, 15:45). - **2026-07-06** **"Participations cross-team read leak" investigated → NOT a leak (false positive); smoke test fixed instead.** Followed up the 2026-07-05 audit's flagged item (the 2 long-standing `db:smoke` coach-token failures). Deep re-investigation: the "leak" was a MISDIAGNOSIS — TR 155 coaches teams 6/81 but is ALSO a rostered **player** on 11/82, and the "leaked" members 27/467 are on 11/82, i.e. genuine same-team teammates visible via the correctly-enforced MEMBER `SAME_TEAM_AS_ME` walk. Verified on prod: every member a coach sees is within their FULL scope (played ∪ coached ∪ TR) — zero rows outside it; single-item + writes were always enforced. Both smoke "failures" were the same false positive: the test computed coach scope from COACHED teams only (missing player/TR teams) and expected `user_logs` to 403 when the MEMBER policy correctly grants an OWN-scoped read (coach sees only their own 155 rows). **Fix = `smoke-test.mjs`** (coach scope now unions coached+TR+played teams; user_logs check asserts own-scope, no foreign rows) → **27/27 both envs** (was 25/27). A scoping read-hook prototyped mid-investigation was deployed to dev, found unnecessary + harmful (would hide legitimate guest RSVPs on a coach's own team activities), and **reverted** (dev redeployed clean; prod never received it). No code/permission change shipped. SECURITY.md open item corrected to "Resolved — false positive". Lesson: a coach is usually also a player — never scope-check attendance visibility against coached teams alone.
